
===== FILE frontend/src/components/ui/async-state.tsx =====
import type { HTMLAttributes, ReactNode } from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

/** Geometry is owned by the caller (className); no pretend data while loading. */
export function Skeleton({ className, ...props }: HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>) {
  return <div {...props} aria-hidden="true" className={cn("visual-skeleton rounded-sm bg-surface-sunken", className)} />
}

export function EmptyState({ title, description, action, className, ...props }: Omit<HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>, "title"> & { title: ReactNode; description?: ReactNode; action?: ReactNode }) {
  return <div {...props} className={cn("space-y-3 py-6 text-sm", className)}>
    <p className="font-semibold text-ink">{title}</p>
    {description != null && <p className="text-muted-foreground">{description}</p>}
    {action}
  </div>
}

export function ErrorState({ title, description, action, className, ...props }: Omit<HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>, "title"> & { title: ReactNode; description?: ReactNode; action?: ReactNode }) {
  return <div role="alert" {...props} className={cn("space-y-3 py-6 text-sm", className)}>
    <p className="font-semibold text-danger">{title}</p>
    {description != null && <p className="text-ink-2">{description}</p>}
    {action}
  </div>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/ui/badge.tsx =====
import type { HTMLAttributes } from "react"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const tones = {
  neutral: "border-line-strong bg-surface-sunken text-ink-2",
  active: "border-active-border bg-active-surface text-active-text",
  warning: "border-warning/20 bg-warning-wash text-warning",
  danger: "border-danger/20 bg-danger-wash text-danger",
  success: "border-success/20 bg-success-wash text-success",
}

export function Badge({
  className,
  tone = "neutral",
  ...props
}: HTMLAttributes<HTMLSpanElement> & { tone?: keyof typeof tones }) {
  return (
    <span
      className={cn(
        // 标签永远一行：中文能在任意两个字之间换行，窄表格列里“待处理”会竖排成一个圆（10-03 纠偏分析页）。
        "visual-control inline-flex h-6 min-h-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md border px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold leading-none [&_svg]:size-3.5 [&_svg]:shrink-0",
        tones[tone],
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/ui/button.tsx =====
import * as React from "react"
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "visual-control visual-press inline-flex min-h-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md px-4 text-sm font-bold transition-[background-color,color,border-color,transform] duration-[180ms] ease-[var(--visual-ease-out)] disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-45 [&_svg]:size-[18px] [&_svg]:shrink-0",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        primary: "bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-brand-hover",
        secondary: "border border-line-strong bg-canvas text-foreground hover:border-active-border hover:bg-surface-sunken",
        ghost: "bg-transparent text-foreground hover:bg-surface-sunken",
        danger: "visual-button-danger bg-danger text-white hover:bg-[var(--visual-danger-hover)]",
      },
      size: {
        default: "h-11 min-w-11",
        sm: "visual-button-small relative my-[3px] h-9 min-h-9 min-w-hit px-3 text-[0.8125rem]",
        icon: "size-11 px-0",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: { variant: "primary", size: "default" },
  },
)

export interface ButtonProps
  extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>,
    VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants> {
  asChild?: boolean
}

export const Button = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ className, variant, size, asChild = false, ...props }, ref) => {
    const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "button"
    return <Comp ref={ref} className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size }), className)} {...props} />
  },
)
Button.displayName = "Button"

export { buttonVariants }

===== FILE frontend/src/components/ui/card.tsx =====
import { forwardRef, type HTMLAttributes, type ReactNode } from "react"
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

/** One card per independent region. asChild preserves the real link/button.
 * A section (even with onClick) never acquires interactive hover presentation.
 */
export const Card = forwardRef<HTMLElement, HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement> & { asChild?: boolean; narrow?: boolean }>(
  ({ className, asChild = false, narrow = false, ...props }, ref) => {
    const Component = asChild ? Slot : "section"
    return <Component ref={ref} className={cn("visual-control visual-card rounded-lg bg-canvas p-card text-foreground", narrow && "p-card-narrow", className)} {...props} />
  },
)
Card.displayName = "Card"

export function CardHeader({ title, description, actions, narrow = false, className, ...props }: Omit<HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement>, "title"> & {
  title: ReactNode; description?: ReactNode; actions?: ReactNode; narrow?: boolean
}) {
  return <header className={cn("mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3", className)} {...props}>
    <div className="min-w-0">
      <h2 className={cn("visual-card-title text-ink", narrow && "visual-card-title-narrow")}>{title}</h2>
      {description != null && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{description}</p>}
    </div>
    {actions}
  </header>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/ui/confidence-ring.tsx =====
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

/** Confidence is a caller-supplied fraction [0,1], never a verdict or an action gate.
 * The contract does not fix the ring diameter: require an explicit CSS length
 * (or pixel number) from the page owner instead of inventing a global default.
 */
export function ConfidenceRing({ value, label, size, className }: { value: number | null | undefined; label: string; size: string | number; className?: string }) {
  const known = value != null && Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0 && value <= 1
  const text = known ? `${Math.floor(value * 1000) / 10}%` : "—"
  return <span role="img" aria-label={`${label}：${text}`} className={cn("inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-ink-2", className)}>
    <svg viewBox="0 0 40 40" style={{ width: size, height: size }} className="shrink-0 -rotate-90" aria-hidden="true">
      <circle cx="20" cy="20" r="17" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="3" className="text-line-strong" />
      {known && <circle cx="20" cy="20" r="17" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="3" pathLength="1" strokeDasharray={`${value} 1`} className="text-active-border" />}
    </svg>
    <span className="font-data tabular-nums">{text}</span>
  </span>
}

import type { HTMLAttributes } from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const colors: Record<string, string> = {
  L1: "bg-level-1-wash text-level-1", L2: "bg-level-2-wash text-level-2",
  L3: "bg-level-3-wash text-level-3", L4: "bg-level-4-wash text-level-4", L5: "bg-level-5-wash text-level-5",
}

/** No allowed-level list or level inference: unfamiliar labels stay visible. */
export function LevelBadge({ level, className, ...props }: HTMLAttributes<HTMLSpanElement> & { level: string | null | undefined }) {

}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/ui/pager.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { CaretLeft, CaretRight } from "@phosphor-icons/react"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { pageWindow } from "@/features/assets/pagination"
import { pageRange } from "@/lib/paging"

/** 翻页条：先说清楚「这一页是第几条到第几条、一共多少条」，再给上一页/下一页。
 *
 *  之前这些列表只取最前面的一批，后面的数据在页面上根本不存在；
 *  把条数写出来才能看出自己有没有漏看。 */
export function Pager({
  page,
  fallbackLimit,
  onChange,
  loading = false,
  unit = "条",
  showPageNumbers = false,
}: {
  page: { total: number; offset: number; limit: number; has_more: boolean } | undefined
  fallbackLimit: number
  onChange: (offset: number) => void
  loading?: boolean
  unit?: string
  showPageNumbers?: boolean
}) {
  const total = page?.total ?? 0
  const offset = page?.offset ?? 0
  const limit = page?.limit ?? fallbackLimit
  const window = pageWindow(total, offset, limit)
  const range = pageRange({ total, offset, limit })
  // 翻页按钮以后端说的 has_more 为准；pageWindow 只负责页码显示。
  const canNext = Boolean(page?.has_more)
  // 始终保留首尾页；中间只展开当前页附近，页数再多也不撑宽页面。
  const pageNumbers: Array<number | string> = window.pageCount <= 7
    ? Array.from({ length: window.pageCount }, (_, index) => index + 1)
    : window.page <= 4
      ? [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, "后续页", window.pageCount]
      : window.page >= window.pageCount - 3
        ? [1, "前序页", ...Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, index) => window.pageCount - 4 + index)]
        : [1, "前序页", window.page - 1, window.page, window.page + 1, "后续页", window.pageCount]

  if (!window.canPrevious && !canNext) {
    return (
      <p data-testid="pager-summary" className="px-4 py-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        共 {total} {unit}，已全部显示。
      </p>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div data-testid="pager" className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-3">
      <p data-testid="pager-summary" className="font-data text-xs tabular-nums text-muted-foreground">
        第 {range.from}–{range.to} {unit} / 共 {total} {unit} · 第 {window.page}/{window.pageCount} 页
      </p>
      <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-2">
        <nav aria-label="分页" className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <Button
            type="button"
            size="sm"
            variant="secondary"
            disabled={!window.canPrevious || loading}
            onClick={() => onChange(Math.max(0, offset - limit))}
          >
            <CaretLeft />上一页
          </Button>
          {showPageNumbers && pageNumbers.map((number) => typeof number === "number" ? (
            <Button
              key={number}
              type="button"
              size="sm"
              variant={number === window.page ? "primary" : "secondary"}
              aria-label={`第 ${number} 页`}
              aria-current={number === window.page ? "page" : undefined}
              disabled={loading || number === window.page}
              className="font-data tabular-nums disabled:opacity-100"
              onClick={() => onChange((number - 1) * limit)}
            >{number}</Button>
          ) : <span key={number} aria-hidden="true" className="px-1 text-muted-foreground">…</span>)}
          <Button
            type="button"
            size="sm"
            variant="secondary"
            disabled={!canNext || loading}
            onClick={() => onChange(offset + limit)}
          >
            下一页<CaretRight />
          </Button>
          </nav>
        {showPageNumbers && (
          <PageJump key={`${offset}:${limit}`} currentPage={window.page} pageCount={window.pageCount}
            loading={loading} onChange={(number) => onChange((number - 1) * limit)} />
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function PageJump({ currentPage, pageCount, loading, onChange }: {
  currentPage: number
  pageCount: number
  loading: boolean
  onChange: (page: number) => void
}) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState("")
  const target = Number(value)
  const valid = /^\d+$/.test(value) && Number.isSafeInteger(target) && target >= 1 && target <= pageCount
  return (
    <form className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground" onSubmit={(event) => {
      event.preventDefault()
      if (valid && !loading && target !== currentPage) onChange(target)
    }}>
      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap">
        跳至
        <Input aria-label="跳转页码" type="number" inputMode="numeric" min={1} max={pageCount} step={1}
          className="h-9 w-20 text-center font-data tabular-nums" placeholder={String(currentPage)}
          value={value} onChange={(event) => setValue(event.target.value)} disabled={loading} />
        页
      </label>
      <Button type="submit" size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={loading || !valid || target === currentPage}>跳转</Button>
    </form>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/ui/progress.tsx =====
import { useId } from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

export interface ProgressProps { label: string; value: number | null | undefined; max?: number; className?: string }

export function Progress({ label, value, max = 100, className }: ProgressProps) {
  const id = useId()
  const known = value != null && Number.isFinite(value) && Number.isFinite(max) && max > 0 && value >= 0 && value <= max
  const ratio = known ? value / max : 0
  // Never round an unfinished value up to 100%; raw numerator stays visible too.
  const percentage = Math.floor(ratio * 1000) / 10
  return <div className={cn("space-y-2", className)}>
    <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-2 text-xs"><span id={id}>{label}</span><span className="font-data tabular-nums">{known ? `${value} / ${max} · ${percentage}%` : "—"}</span></div>
    <div role="progressbar" aria-labelledby={id} aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={max > 0 && Number.isFinite(max) ? max : undefined}
      aria-valuenow={known ? value : undefined} aria-valuetext={known ? `${value} / ${max}，${percentage}%` : "进度未知"}
      className="h-1.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-surface-sunken">
      <span className="visual-progress-fill block h-full w-full origin-left rounded-full bg-active-border transition-transform duration-[180ms] ease-[var(--visual-ease-out)]"
        style={{ transform: `scaleX(${ratio})` }} />
    </div>
  </div>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/ui/segmented-control.tsx =====
import { useId, useRef, type ReactNode, type KeyboardEvent } from "react"
import * as m from "motion/react-m"
import { useMotionFeaturesReady } from "@/components/motion/motion-root"
import { useReducedMotionPreference } from "@/lib/motion/reduced-motion"
import { motionTokens } from "@/lib/motion/tokens"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

export interface SegmentedOption<Value extends string> { value: Value; label: ReactNode; disabled?: boolean }
export interface SegmentedControlProps<Value extends string> {
  label: string
  value: Value | null
  options: readonly SegmentedOption<Value>[]
  onValueChange: (value: Value) => void
  disabled?: boolean
  className?: string
}

/** Controlled radio group. Values/order/availability are supplied by the caller. */
export function SegmentedControl<Value extends string>({ label, value, options, onValueChange, disabled = false, className }: SegmentedControlProps<Value>) {
  const id = useId()
  const refs = useRef(new Map<Value, HTMLButtonElement>())
  const reduce = useReducedMotionPreference()
  const ready = useMotionFeaturesReady()
  const enabled = options.filter(option => !disabled && !option.disabled)
  const tabValue = enabled.find(option => option.value === value)?.value ?? enabled[0]?.value
  const select = (next: Value) => { if (next !== value) onValueChange(next) }
  const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLButtonElement>, current: Value) => {
    if (!enabled.length || !["ArrowLeft", "ArrowRight", "ArrowUp", "ArrowDown", "Home", "End"].includes(event.key)) return
    event.preventDefault()
    const index = enabled.findIndex(option => option.value === current)
    const nextIndex = event.key === "Home" ? 0 : event.key === "End" ? enabled.length - 1 :
      (index + (event.key === "ArrowRight" || event.key === "ArrowDown" ? 1 : -1) + enabled.length) % enabled.length
    const next = enabled[nextIndex].value
    refs.current.get(next)?.focus()
    select(next)
  }
  return <div role="radiogroup" aria-label={label} aria-disabled={disabled || undefined} className={cn("inline-flex flex-wrap gap-1 rounded-md bg-surface-sunken p-1", className)}>
    {options.map(option => {
      const selected = value === option.value
      return <button key={option.value} type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={selected} disabled={disabled || option.disabled}
        tabIndex={option.value === tabValue ? 0 : -1} ref={node => { if (node) refs.current.set(option.value, node); else refs.current.delete(option.value) }}
        onClick={() => select(option.value)} onKeyDown={event => onKeyDown(event, option.value)}
        className={cn("visual-control relative isolate min-h-11 min-w-hit rounded-md px-4 text-sm disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-45", selected ? "font-semibold text-ink" : "font-normal text-ink-2")}>
        {selected && (ready && !reduce ? <m.span aria-hidden="true" layoutId={`segment-${id}`} initial={false}
          transition={motionTokens.spring} className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 -z-10 rounded-md bg-canvas shadow-card" /> :
          <span aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 -z-10 rounded-md bg-canvas shadow-card" />)}
        {option.label}
      </button>
    })}
  </div>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/workflow-flow-canvas.tsx =====
import { ArrowRight, LinkSimple, X } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react"
import type { DragEvent, PointerEvent as ReactPointerEvent } from "react"

import type { WorkflowPaletteNode } from "@/features/workflow-manager/types"
import {
  adaptiveWorkflowCanvasSize,
  workflowCanvasFocusScroll,
} from "@/features/workflow-manager/operator-presentation"

export type FlowPosition = { x: number; y: number }
export type FlowCanvasNode = {
  key: string
  kind: string
  display_name: string
  ui_position: FlowPosition
  summary: string
}
export type FlowCanvasEdge = { from: string; to: string; branch?: string }

type Props = {
  nodes: FlowCanvasNode[]
  edges: FlowCanvasEdge[]
  selectedKey: string | null
  onSelect: (key: string) => void
  onMove: (key: string, position: FlowPosition) => void
  onDropNode: (node: WorkflowPaletteNode, position: FlowPosition) => void
  onConnect: (from: string, to: string) => void
  suggestedTargetKeys?: string[]
  readOnly?: boolean
}

const NODE_WIDTH = 196
const NODE_HEIGHT = 92

const kindTone: Record<string, string> = {
  upstream_connector: "border-alias-edge-006 bg-alias-surface-021",
  prompt: "border-alias-edge-010 bg-alias-surface-046",
  rule_module: "border-alias-edge-019 bg-alias-surface-072",
  downstream_projection: "border-alias-edge-002 bg-alias-surface-020",
  image_first_seen_lookup: "border-alias-edge-013 bg-alias-surface-078",
  branch: "border-alias-edge-005 bg-alias-surface-024",
  join: "border-alias-edge-005 bg-alias-surface-024",
}

export function WorkflowFlowCanvas({
  nodes,
  edges,
  selectedKey,
  onSelect,
  onMove,
  onDropNode,
  onConnect,
  suggestedTargetKeys = [],
  readOnly = false,
}: Props) {
  const viewportRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const centeredWorkflowStart = useRef<string | null>(null)
  const moving = useRef<{ key: string; offsetX: number; offsetY: number } | null>(null)
  const [connectingFrom, setConnectingFrom] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [viewportSize, setViewportSize] = useState({ width: 640, height: 540 })
  const nodeByKey = useMemo(() => new Map(nodes.map((node) => [node.key, node])), [nodes])
  const canvasSize = useMemo(() => adaptiveWorkflowCanvasSize({
    viewportWidth: viewportSize.width,
    viewportHeight: viewportSize.height,
    nodePositions: nodes.map((node) => node.ui_position),
  }), [nodes, viewportSize.height, viewportSize.width])

  useEffect(() => {
    const viewport = viewportRef.current
    if (!viewport) return
    const update = () => {
      const width = viewport.clientWidth
      const height = viewport.clientHeight
      setViewportSize((current) => (
        current.width === width && current.height === height
          ? current
          : { width, height }
      ))
    }
    update()
    if (typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined") return
    const observer = new ResizeObserver(update)
    observer.observe(viewport)
    return () => observer.disconnect()
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    const viewport = viewportRef.current
    const workflowStart = nodes.find((node) => node.kind === "upstream_connector") ?? nodes[0]
    if (!viewport || !workflowStart || centeredWorkflowStart.current === workflowStart.key) return
    const position = workflowCanvasFocusScroll({
      viewportWidth: viewport.clientWidth,
      viewportHeight: viewport.clientHeight,
      canvasWidth: canvasSize.width,
      canvasHeight: canvasSize.height,
      focusPosition: workflowStart.ui_position,
    })
    viewport.scrollLeft = position.left
    viewport.scrollTop = position.top
    centeredWorkflowStart.current = workflowStart.key
  }, [canvasSize.height, canvasSize.width, nodes])

  useEffect(() => {
    const move = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      const active = moving.current
      const canvas = canvasRef.current
      if (readOnly || !active || !canvas) return
      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect()
      onMove(active.key, {
        x: clamp(event.clientX - rect.left - active.offsetX, 12, canvasSize.width - NODE_WIDTH - 12),
        y: clamp(event.clientY - rect.top - active.offsetY, 12, canvasSize.height - NODE_HEIGHT - 12),
      })
    }
    const end = () => { moving.current = null }
    window.addEventListener("pointermove", move)
    window.addEventListener("pointerup", end)
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("pointermove", move)
      window.removeEventListener("pointerup", end)
    }
  }, [canvasSize.height, canvasSize.width, onMove, readOnly])

  const startMove = (event: ReactPointerEvent, node: FlowCanvasNode) => {
    if (readOnly) { onSelect(node.key); return }
    const target = event.target as HTMLElement
    if (target.closest("button")) return
    moving.current = {
      key: node.key,
      offsetX: event.clientX - event.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect().left,
      offsetY: event.clientY - event.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect().top,
    }
    onSelect(node.key)
    event.preventDefault()
  }

  const drop = (event: DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (readOnly) return
    const encoded = event.dataTransfer.getData("application/x-workflow-node")
    if (!encoded || !canvasRef.current) return
    let node: WorkflowPaletteNode
    try {
      node = JSON.parse(encoded) as WorkflowPaletteNode
    } catch {
      return
    }
    const rect = canvasRef.current.getBoundingClientRect()
    onDropNode(node, {
      x: clamp(event.clientX - rect.left - NODE_WIDTH / 2, 12, canvasSize.width - NODE_WIDTH - 12),
      y: clamp(event.clientY - rect.top - NODE_HEIGHT / 2, 12, canvasSize.height - NODE_HEIGHT - 12),
    })
  }

  const chooseConnectionTarget = (target: string) => {
    if (!readOnly && connectingFrom && connectingFrom !== target) onConnect(connectingFrom, target)
    setConnectingFrom(null)
  }

  return (
    <div ref={viewportRef} className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto bg-alias-surface-035">
      <div
        ref={canvasRef}
        data-testid="workflow-flow-canvas"
        className="relative select-none"
        style={{
          width: canvasSize.width,
          height: canvasSize.height,
          backgroundImage: "radial-gradient(#cbd1c6 1px, transparent 1px)",
          backgroundSize: "20px 20px",
        }}
        onDragOver={(event) => event.preventDefault()}
        onDrop={drop}
        onClick={() => setConnectingFrom(null)}
      >
        <svg className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 size-full" viewBox={`0 0 ${canvasSize.width} ${canvasSize.height}`} aria-hidden="true">
          <defs>
            <marker id="workflow-arrow" markerWidth="8" markerHeight="8" refX="7" refY="4" orient="auto">
              <path d="M0,0 L8,4 L0,8 Z" fill="#60675d" />
            </marker>
          </defs>
          {edges.map((edge, index) => {
            const source = nodeByKey.get(edge.from)
            const target = nodeByKey.get(edge.to)
            if (!source || !target) return null
            const x1 = source.ui_position.x + NODE_WIDTH
            const y1 = source.ui_position.y + NODE_HEIGHT / 2
            const x2 = target.ui_position.x
            const y2 = target.ui_position.y + NODE_HEIGHT / 2
            const bend = Math.max(45, Math.abs(x2 - x1) / 2)
            return (
              <g key={`${edge.from}-${edge.to}-${index}`}>
                <path d={`M ${x1} ${y1} C ${x1 + bend} ${y1}, ${x2 - bend} ${y2}, ${x2} ${y2}`} fill="none" stroke="#60675d" strokeWidth="2" markerEnd="url(#workflow-arrow)" />
                {edge.branch && <text x={(x1 + x2) / 2} y={(y1 + y2) / 2 - 8} textAnchor="middle" className="fill-[#60675d] text-xs">{edge.branch}</text>}
              </g>
            )
          })}
        </svg>

        {nodes.length === 0 && (
          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center text-center text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">
            <div><ArrowRight className="mx-auto mb-3" size={28} /><p className="font-bold text-[var(--ink)]">从左侧拖入第一个步骤</p><p className="mt-1">系统会用流程图展示先后与分支关系</p></div>
          </div>
        )}

        {nodes.map((node) => {
          const suggested = suggestedTargetKeys.includes(node.key)
          const visionPolicySummary = compactVisionPolicySummary(node.summary)
          return (
            <article
              key={node.key}
              draggable={false}
              className={`absolute rounded-sm border-2 px-4 py-3 shadow-card ${kindTone[node.kind] ?? "border-alias-edge-012 bg-white"} ${selectedKey === node.key ? "ring-2 ring-ink ring-offset-2" : suggested ? "ring-2 ring-primary ring-offset-2" : "hover:shadow-raise"}`}
              style={{ left: node.ui_position.x, top: node.ui_position.y, width: NODE_WIDTH, height: NODE_HEIGHT }}
              data-suggested-continuation={suggested ? "true" : undefined}
              data-vision-policy-summary={visionPolicySummary || undefined}
              onPointerDown={(event) => startMove(event, node)}
              onClick={(event) => { event.stopPropagation(); onSelect(node.key) }}
            >
              <p className="truncate text-sm font-bold">{node.display_name}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 line-clamp-2 text-xs leading-4 text-[var(--muted)]" title={node.summary}>{node.summary}</p>
              {suggested && (
                <span className="absolute -top-3 left-3 rounded-md bg-primary px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-bold text-ink">
                  可从这里继续
                </span>
              )}
              <button
                type="button"
                className="visual-control absolute -right-3 top-1/2 flex size-6 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-alias-edge-003 bg-white text-alias-content-003 shadow-card"
                aria-label={`从${node.display_name}开始连接`}
                disabled={readOnly}
                title="从这里连接下一步"
                onClick={(event) => { event.stopPropagation(); setConnectingFrom(node.key) }}
              >
                <LinkSimple size={12} weight="bold" />
              </button>
              {connectingFrom && connectingFrom !== node.key && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="visual-control absolute -left-3 top-1/2 flex size-7 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 border-primary bg-white text-primary shadow-card"
                  aria-label={`连接到${node.display_name}`}
                  disabled={readOnly}
                  title="连接到这里"
                  onClick={(event) => { event.stopPropagation(); chooseConnectionTarget(node.key) }}
                >
                  <X className="rotate-45" size={14} weight="bold" />
                </button>
              )}
            </article>
          )
        })}

        {connectingFrom && (
          <div className="absolute bottom-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 rounded-md bg-ink px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white shadow-float">
            请选择要连接的下一步；点击空白处取消
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function clamp(value: number, minimum: number, maximum: number) {
  return Math.min(maximum, Math.max(minimum, value))
}

function compactVisionPolicySummary(summary: string) {
  return summary.match(/目标 (?:low|high|xhigh|image_pixel_limit) \/ 锚点 (?:low|high|xhigh|image_pixel_limit)/)?.[0] ?? ""
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/workflow-stepper.tsx =====
import { Check, LockKey, WarningCircle } from "@phosphor-icons/react"

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

export type WorkflowStepState = "completed" | "current" | "pending" | "blocked"

export type WorkflowStep = {
  key: string
  label: string
  note: string
  state: WorkflowStepState
  required?: boolean
}

export function WorkflowStepper({
  steps,
  workflowLabel,
}: {
  steps: readonly WorkflowStep[]
  workflowLabel: string
}) {
  return (
    <section className="border-y border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white" aria-label={`${workflowLabel}步骤`}>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-4">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">当前工作流</p>
          <h2 className="mt-1 text-lg font-bold">{workflowLabel}</h2>
        </div>
        <Badge tone="neutral">串行推进 · 人工发布闸门</Badge>
      </div>
      <ol className="grid gap-px bg-[var(--line)] lg:grid-cols-5">
        {steps.map((step, index) => {
          const completed = step.state === "completed"
          const blocked = step.state === "blocked"
          const current = step.state === "current"
          return (
            <li key={step.key} className={cn("min-w-0 bg-white px-4 py-4", current && "bg-alias-surface-044", blocked && "bg-alias-surface-080")}>
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <span className={cn(
                  "font-data flex size-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md border text-xs font-bold",
                  completed && "border-alias-edge-007 bg-alias-surface-007 text-alias-content-001",
                  current && "border-alias-edge-011 bg-primary text-primary-foreground",
                  blocked && "border-alias-edge-053 bg-alias-surface-068 text-alias-content-033",
                  !completed && !current && !blocked && "border-[var(--line-strong)] text-[var(--muted)]",
                )}>
                  {completed ? <Check weight="bold" /> : blocked ? <WarningCircle weight="fill" /> : index + 1}
                </span>
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold leading-5">{step.label}</p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{step.note}</p>
                  {step.required && step.state === "pending" && (
                    <p className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-alias-content-033"><LockKey size={13} />前置未完成</p>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </li>
          )
        })}
      </ol>
    </section>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/workspace-page.tsx =====
import { PageHeading } from "@/components/page-heading"
import * as Dialog from "@radix-ui/react-dialog"
import { X } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useMotionValue } from "motion/react"
import * as m from "motion/react-m"
import { forwardRef, useCallback, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState, type ComponentProps, type ReactNode, type SyntheticEvent } from "react"
import { flushSync } from "react-dom"

import { useMotionFeaturesReady } from "@/components/motion/motion-root"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { useReducedMotionPreference } from "@/lib/motion/reduced-motion"
import { enterDistance, motionTokens } from "@/lib/motion/tokens"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

export function WorkspacePageHeader({
  title,
  description,
  actions,
}: {
  index?: string
  title: string
  description?: string
  actions?: ReactNode
}) {
  return <PageHeading title={title} description={description} actions={actions} />
}

export function StatusSummaryStrip({ children, className }: { children: ReactNode; className?: string }) {

      <summary className="cursor-pointer text-sm font-bold focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary">{summary}</summary>

export interface SecondaryDrawerProps {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  title: string
  description?: string
  children: ReactNode
  footer?: ReactNode
  size?: "default" | "wide"
  className?: string
}

// Contract §5.1 permits 120–220ms ease-out exits. Use the lower end so
// unmount/layout work also has room inside the 220ms interaction deadline.
const exitMilliseconds = 120
const exitTransition = { ...motionTokens.easeOut, duration: exitMilliseconds / 1000 }

/** Escape ownership: inner controls first, the focused workspace layer last.
 * - An open native picker (select, date) gets the key alone: the browser's
 *   default action closes it, and no page listener (Radix included) runs.
 * - Inner Radix layers such as menus are the highest Radix layer and dismiss
 *   themselves with preventDefault. Inner inputs that own Escape stop
 *   propagation. IME composition keeps its Escape.
 * - Only an unclaimed Escape closes the workspace layer that holds focus, or
 *   the most recently opened one when focus fell back to body.
 * Layers register in the commit that opens them, so a dialog that already has
 * focus owns Escape before Radix's passive layer stack catches up.
 */
type WorkspaceLayer = { panel: () => HTMLElement | null; dismiss: () => void }
const workspaceLayers: WorkspaceLayer[] = []
// Escapes whose preventDefault came only from a workspace dialog blocking
// Radix's own dismissal; any other prior preventDefault means it was claimed.
const dialogBlockedEscapes = new WeakSet<Event>()

function nativePickerOpen(target: EventTarget | null) {
  if (!(target instanceof HTMLSelectElement || target instanceof HTMLInputElement)) return false
  try { return target.matches(":open") } catch { return false } // No :open support: treat as closed.
}
function yieldEscapeToNativePicker(event: KeyboardEvent) {
  // Window capture runs before every document listener. Never preventDefault:
  // the picker closes through its default action.
  if (event.key === "Escape" && nativePickerOpen(event.target)) event.stopImmediatePropagation()
}
function dismissWorkspaceLayer(event: KeyboardEvent) {
  if (event.key !== "Escape" || event.isComposing || event.keyCode === 229) return
  if (event.defaultPrevented && !dialogBlockedEscapes.has(event)) return
  const target = event.target instanceof Element ? event.target : null
  const dialog = target?.closest('[role="dialog"]')
  let owner: WorkspaceLayer | undefined
  // Focus in another dialog (lightbox, navigation, popover) or in a portaled
  // menu is not ours. Body is the refused-dismissal fallback.
  if (dialog) owner = workspaceLayers.find(layer => layer.panel() === dialog)
  else if (!target || target === document.body || target === document.documentElement) {
    owner = [...workspaceLayers].reverse().find(layer => layer.panel()?.isConnected)
  }
  if (!owner) return
  event.preventDefault()
  owner.dismiss()
}
function registerWorkspaceLayer(layer: WorkspaceLayer) {
  if (!workspaceLayers.length) {
    window.addEventListener("keydown", yieldEscapeToNativePicker, true)
    window.addEventListener("keydown", dismissWorkspaceLayer)
  }
  workspaceLayers.push(layer)
  return () => {
    const index = workspaceLayers.indexOf(layer)
    if (index >= 0) workspaceLayers.splice(index, 1)
    if (!workspaceLayers.length) {
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", yieldEscapeToNativePicker, true)
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", dismissWorkspaceLayer)
    }
  }
}

/** Radix keeps the modal boundary until the exiting content actually unmounts.
 * The parent remains the only owner of open; no animation callback changes it.
 */
function DialogLayer({ open, onOpenChange, kind, className, children }: {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  kind: "drawer" | "confirm"
  className: string
  children: ReactNode
}) {
  const ready = useMotionFeaturesReady()
  const reduced = useReducedMotionPreference()
  const animated = ready && !reduced
  const [retained, setRetained] = useState(open)
  const present = open || retained
  const panelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)
  const capturedPanelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)
  const returnFocusRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null)
  const wasOpenRef = useRef(false)
  const closeDeadlineRef = useRef(0)
  const requestChange = useCallback((next: boolean) => { if (open) onOpenChange(next) }, [open, onOpenChange])
  const rememberEntry = () => {
    const active = typeof document !== "undefined" && document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement ? document.activeElement : null
    // Do not replace a live entry with body in the removal/autofocus gap, or
    // with a child that already took autofocus during a StrictMode replay.
    if ((active && active !== document.body && !panelRef.current?.contains(active)) || !returnFocusRef.current?.isConnected) {
      returnFocusRef.current = active
    }
  }
  // Preserve the old component's capture-before-mount behavior: children such
  // as category-asset-manager use React autoFocus, ahead of Radix's effect.
  if (open && !wasOpenRef.current && !panelRef.current?.isConnected) rememberEntry()
  if (!open && wasOpenRef.current) closeDeadlineRef.current = performance.now() + exitMilliseconds
  wasOpenRef.current = open

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (open) { setRetained(true); return }
    if (!retained) return
    if (!animated) { setRetained(false); return }
    // Commit/layout work consumes this same exit budget; never start another
    // full delay after an expensive closing commit.
    const remaining = Math.max(0, closeDeadlineRef.current - performance.now())
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => flushSync(() => setRetained(false)), remaining)
    // Reopening or changing the motion preference invalidates this close.
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer)
  }, [open, animated, retained])
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (open || !present) return
    // Inert can move focus to body. Keep Tab/Enter from reaching background
    // controls while the modal's final frame is still on screen.
    const block = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const targetDialog = event.target instanceof Element ? event.target.closest<HTMLElement>('[role="dialog"]') : null
      // A different modal can be opened by the parent while this one exits.
      if (targetDialog && targetDialog !== panelRef.current && !targetDialog.inert) return
      event.preventDefault(); event.stopImmediatePropagation()
    }
    document.addEventListener("keydown", block, true)
    return () => document.removeEventListener("keydown", block, true)
  }, [open, present])
  const dismissRef = useRef(requestChange)
  useLayoutEffect(() => { dismissRef.current = requestChange })
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return
    // Same commit as the focus move; an exiting layer no longer takes Escape.
    return registerWorkspaceLayer({ panel: () => panelRef.current, dismiss: () => dismissRef.current(false) })
  }, [open])

  return (
    <Dialog.Root open={present} onOpenChange={requestChange}>
      {present && <Dialog.Portal>
        <Dialog.Overlay asChild>
          <DialogShade open={open} animated={animated} />
        </Dialog.Overlay>
        <Dialog.Content asChild ref={panelRef}
          onEscapeKeyDown={(event) => {
            // Radix's stack can still be the previous one in a new layer's
            // first frame. Block its dismissal; dismissWorkspaceLayer decides.
            if (!event.defaultPrevented) dialogBlockedEscapes.add(event)
            event.preventDefault()
          }}
          onOpenAutoFocus={() => {
            // StrictMode replays effects on the same host. Do not replace the
            // entry focus with a control inside the already-open dialog.
            if (capturedPanelRef.current !== panelRef.current) {
              capturedPanelRef.current = panelRef.current
              rememberEntry()
            }
          }}
          onCloseAutoFocus={(event) => {
            event.preventDefault()
            // Radix dispatches this after unmount on a timer. Ignore both its
            // StrictMode cleanup and a stale cleanup after an immediate reopen.
            if (!panelRef.current?.isConnected) returnFocusRef.current?.focus()
          }}>
          <DialogPanel open={open} animated={animated} kind={kind} className={className}>{children}</DialogPanel>
        </Dialog.Content>
      </Dialog.Portal>}
    </Dialog.Root>
  )
}

const DialogShade = forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, { open: boolean; animated: boolean } & ComponentProps<typeof m.div>>(
  function DialogShade({ open, animated, ...props }, ref) {
    const enter = useRef(animated)
    const opacity = useMotionValue(enter.current ? 0 : 1)
    useLayoutEffect(() => {
      if (!animated) { opacity.stop(); opacity.set(open ? 1 : 0) }
      return () => opacity.stop()
    }, [animated, open, opacity])
    return <m.div {...props} ref={ref} className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-foreground/30" style={{ ...props.style, opacity }}
      initial={enter.current ? { opacity: 0 } : false} animate={{ opacity: open ? 1 : 0 }}
      transition={!animated ? { duration: 0 } : open ? motionTokens.easeOut : exitTransition} />
  },
)

// Radix's asChild must reach the same DOM host for focus, dismiss and refs.
// Keep a mounted host when reduced motion changes; never replace a live form.
const DialogPanel = forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, {
  open: boolean
  animated: boolean
  kind: "drawer" | "confirm"
} & ComponentProps<typeof m.div>>(function DialogPanel({ open, animated, kind, children, ...props }, ref) {
  const enter = useRef(animated)
  const opacity = useMotionValue(enter.current ? 0 : 1)
  const offset = useMotionValue(enter.current ? enterDistance : 0)
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!animated) {
      opacity.stop(); offset.stop()
      opacity.set(open ? 1 : 0); offset.set(0)
    }
    return () => { opacity.stop(); offset.stop() }
  }, [animated, open, opacity, offset])
  const blockClosing = (event: SyntheticEvent) => {
    if (!open) { event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation() }
  }
  return <m.div {...props} ref={ref} data-workspace-layer={kind} data-visual-state={open ? "open" : "closing"} inert={!open}
    onClickCapture={(event) => { blockClosing(event); if (open) props.onClickCapture?.(event) }}
    onPointerDownCapture={(event) => { blockClosing(event); if (open) props.onPointerDownCapture?.(event) }}
    onKeyDownCapture={(event) => { blockClosing(event); if (open) props.onKeyDownCapture?.(event) }}
    style={{ ...props.style, opacity, ...(kind === "drawer" ? { x: offset } : { y: offset }) }}
    initial={enter.current ? { opacity: 0, [kind === "drawer" ? "x" : "y"]: enterDistance } : false}
    animate={{ opacity: open ? 1 : 0, [kind === "drawer" ? "x" : "y"]: open || !animated ? 0 : enterDistance }}
    transition={!animated ? { duration: 0 } : open ? motionTokens[kind === "drawer" ? "soft" : "spring"] : exitTransition}>
    {children}
  </m.div>
})

export function SecondaryDrawer({ open, onOpenChange, title, description, children, footer, size = "default", className }: SecondaryDrawerProps) {
  return (
        <DialogLayer open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange} kind="drawer"
          className={cn(
            "fixed inset-y-2 right-2 z-50 flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-canvas shadow-float focus:outline-none",
            size === "wide" ? "w-[min(820px,calc(100vw-1rem))]" : "w-[min(680px,calc(100vw-1rem))]",
            className,
          )}
        >
          <div className="flex shrink-0 items-start justify-between gap-4 px-6 pb-2 pt-5 sm:pl-7 sm:pr-4">
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <Dialog.Title className="font-editorial break-words text-[26px] font-bold leading-[1.2]">{title}</Dialog.Title>
              {description && <Dialog.Description className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">{description}</Dialog.Description>}
            </div>
            <Dialog.Close asChild>
              <Button variant="secondary" size="icon" aria-label="关闭抽屉"><X size={20} /></Button>
            </Dialog.Close>
          </div>
          <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-6 pb-6 pt-3 sm:px-7">{children}</div>
          {footer && <div className="shrink-0 border-t border-line px-6 pb-6 pt-4 sm:px-7">{footer}</div>}
        </DialogLayer>
  )
}

export function ConfirmDialog({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  title,
  description,
  confirmLabel = "确认",
  cancelLabel = "取消",
  onConfirm,
  children,
}: {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  title: string
  description?: string
  confirmLabel?: string
  cancelLabel?: string
  onConfirm: () => void
  children?: ReactNode
}) {
  const confirmed = useRef(false)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (open) { confirmed.current = false; setPending(false) }
  }, [open])
  const confirmOnce = () => {
    if (!open || confirmed.current) return
    confirmed.current = true
    setPending(true)
    onConfirm()
  }

  return (
        <DialogLayer open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange} kind="confirm"
          className="fixed left-1/2 top-1/2 z-50 flex max-h-[calc(100dvh-2rem)] w-[min(460px,calc(100vw-2rem))] -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 flex-col overflow-y-auto rounded-lg bg-canvas p-6 shadow-float focus:outline-none sm:p-7"
        >
          <Dialog.Title className="visual-card-title break-words pr-12">{title}</Dialog.Title>
          {description && <Dialog.Description className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">{description}</Dialog.Description>}
          {children && <div className="mt-4">{children}</div>}
          <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-2">
            <Dialog.Close asChild><Button variant="secondary">{cancelLabel}</Button></Dialog.Close>
            <Button onClick={confirmOnce} disabled={pending}>{confirmLabel}</Button>
          </div>
          <Dialog.Close asChild><Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="关闭对话框" className="absolute right-3 top-3"><X size={18} /></Button></Dialog.Close>
        </DialogLayer>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/assets/asset-format.ts =====
/** 素材页格式化与状态推导 —— 从 assets-page.tsx 抽出的纯函数，不含 JSX。 */

import { ApiError } from "@/lib/api"
import type { Asset, EvaluationCategoryProfile, UploadFileIssue } from "@/lib/types"

export type CategoryKey = EvaluationCategoryProfile["category_key"]

export type UploadFeedback = {
  source: string
  successful: string[]
  skipped: UploadFileIssue[]
  failed: UploadFileIssue[]
}

export function fileSize(bytes: number) {
  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${Math.max(1, Math.round(bytes / 1024))} KB`
  return `${(bytes / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB`
}

export function fileType(mimeType: string) {
  return mimeType.split("/")[1]?.toUpperCase().replace("JPEG", "JPG") || "图片"
}

export function snapshotFiles(files: FileList | null) {
  return files ? Array.from(files) : []
}

export function relativeBrowserFileName(file: File) {
  const relativePath = (file as File & { webkitRelativePath?: string }).webkitRelativePath
  const raw = (relativePath || file.name).replaceAll("\\", "/")
  const parts = raw.split("/").filter((part) => part && part !== "." && part !== "..")
  const absolute = raw.startsWith("/") || raw.startsWith("//") || /^[A-Za-z]:\//.test(raw) || raw.split("/").includes("..")
  return (absolute ? parts.at(-1) : parts.join("/")) || "unnamed-file"
}

export function fileSkipReason(file: File, category?: EvaluationCategoryProfile) {
  const filename = relativeBrowserFileName(file)
  const parts = filename.split("/")
  const basename = parts.at(-1)?.toLowerCase() ?? ""
  if (
    parts.some((part) => part.startsWith("."))
    || parts.some((part) => part.toLowerCase() === "__macosx")
    || basename === "thumbs.db"
    || basename === "desktop.ini"
  ) return "隐藏或系统元数据"
  const suffixIndex = basename.lastIndexOf(".")
  const suffix = suffixIndex >= 0 ? basename.slice(suffixIndex) : ""
  const allowedSuffixes = new Set(category?.pipeline_config.allowed_suffixes.map((item) => item.toLowerCase()) ?? [])
  if (!allowedSuffixes.has(suffix)) return `当前类目不支持 ${suffix || "无扩展名"} 格式`
  return null
}

export function uploadIssuesFromError(error: unknown, key: "skipped_files" | "failed_files") {
  if (!(error instanceof ApiError) || !Array.isArray(error.detail?.[key])) return []
  return error.detail[key].filter((item): item is UploadFileIssue => (
    Boolean(item)
    && typeof item === "object"
    && "filename" in item
    && typeof item.filename === "string"
    && "reason" in item
    && typeof item.reason === "string"
  ))
}

export function evaluationStatus(value: Asset["evaluation_status"]) {
  return ({
    not_evaluated: "未评测",
    evaluated_old: "仅旧版本",
    evaluated_current: "当前版本已评测",
    queued: "已排队",
    running: "运行中",
    failed: "失败",
  } as const)[value ?? "not_evaluated"]
}

export function statusTone(value: Asset["evaluation_status"]) {
  if (value === "evaluated_current") return "success"
  if (value === "failed") return "danger"
  if (value === "queued" || value === "running") return "warning"
  return "active"
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/assets/case-dimension-import.tsx =====
import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react"
import { FolderSimple } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { api } from "@/lib/api"
import type {
  DesignCaseDimensionImportResult,
  DesignCaseDimensionPreview,
} from "@/lib/types"

const LEVELS = ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"] as const
const CASE_GRADE: Record<string, string> = {
  L1: "好",
  L2: "中等",
  L3: "中差",
  L4: "极差",
  L5: "过滤",
}

function levelLine(counts: Record<string, number>, levels: readonly string[]) {
  return levels
    .filter((level) => counts[level])
    .map((level) => `${CASE_GRADE[level]}(${level}) ${counts[level]}`)
    .join(" · ")
}

/**
 * 案例两维度样本：从 NAS 交付目录导入。
 *
 * 一个目录同时喂两条基准——逐图人工档位给图片维度，案例正文里的聚合统计给
 * 素材维度。预检会把每张图都读一遍（要算内容哈希），所以看到的数字与真正
 * 落库的完全一致；目录大时这一步会慢，属于正常。
 */
export function CaseDimensionImport({
  categoryKey,
  packageName,
  disabled,
  onImported,
  render,
}: {
  categoryKey: string
  packageName: string
  disabled?: boolean
  onImported: () => Promise<void> | void
  /** Only presentation enters the drawer; the preview/mutation session stays. */
  render?: (controls: ReactNode) => ReactNode
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [rootUri, setRootUri] = useState("")
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<DesignCaseDimensionPreview | null>(null)

  function body() {
    return JSON.stringify({
      root_uri: rootUri.trim(),
      category_key: categoryKey,
      ...(packageName.trim() ? { package_name: packageName.trim() } : {}),
    })
  }

  const previewSamples = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () =>
      api<DesignCaseDimensionPreview>("/api/design-case-dimension-samples/preview", {
        method: "POST",
        body: body(),
      }),
    onSuccess: setPreview,
    onError: (error: Error) => {
      setPreview(null)
      toast.error(error.message)
    },
  })

  const importSamples = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () =>
      api<DesignCaseDimensionImportResult>("/api/design-case-dimension-samples/import", {
        method: "POST",
        body: body(),
      }),
    onSuccess: async (data) => {
      setPreview(null)
      setRootUri("")
      setOpen(false)
      await onImported()
      toast.success(
        `“${data.package.name}”已导入 ${data.summary.created_count} 个案例、`
          + `${data.summary.image_asset_created} 张新图`
          + (data.summary.image_asset_reused ? `（复用已有 ${data.summary.image_asset_reused} 张）` : "")
          + (data.summary.duplicate_count ? `，跳过重复案例 ${data.summary.duplicate_count} 个` : ""),
      )
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const busy = previewSamples.isPending || importSamples.isPending

  const controls = (
    <>
      <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)} disabled={disabled}>
        <FolderSimple />案例两维度样本
      </Button>
      {open ? (
        <div className="col-span-full min-w-0 rounded-sm bg-surface-sunken p-4 text-sm">
          <label className="block">
            <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold">NAS 交付目录</span>
            <Input
              value={rootUri}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setRootUri(event.target.value)
                setPreview(null)
                previewSamples.reset()
                importSamples.reset()
              }}
              placeholder="nas://maps/采集任务交付文件/.../案例-普通样本/测试"
              disabled={busy}
            />
          </label>
          <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
            目录下按 01好 / 02中等 / 03中差 / 04极差 / 05过滤 分档，
            每个案例一个「档位_案例编号」子目录，里面是正文 txt 与「档位_图片编号」图片。
            图片维度没有过滤档，单张图片只分好 / 中等 / 中差 / 极差。
          </p>
          <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <Button
              variant="secondary"
              onClick={() => previewSamples.mutate()}
              disabled={busy || !rootUri.trim()}
            >
              {previewSamples.isPending ? "正在读取 NAS" : "预检"}
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="secondary"
              onClick={() => {
                setOpen(false)
                setPreview(null)
              }}
              disabled={busy}
            >
              取消
            </Button>
          </div>

          {previewSamples.error || importSamples.error ? (
            <p role="alert" className="mt-3 break-words rounded-sm bg-danger-wash px-3 py-2 text-sm text-danger">
              {(importSamples.error ?? previewSamples.error)?.message}
            </p>
          ) : null}

          {preview ? (
            <div className="mt-4 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-3">
              <p className="font-semibold">
                共 {preview.summary.cases} 个案例、{preview.summary.images} 张图片
              </p>
              <dl className="mt-2 space-y-1 text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
                <div>
                  <dt className="inline font-semibold">案例档位</dt>
                  <dd className="ml-2 inline">{levelLine(preview.summary.case_levels, LEVELS)}</dd>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <dt className="inline font-semibold">图片档位</dt>
                  <dd className="ml-2 inline">
                    {levelLine(preview.summary.image_levels, LEVELS.slice(0, 4))}
                  </dd>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <dt className="inline font-semibold">图片类型</dt>
                  <dd className="ml-2 inline">
                    {Object.entries(preview.summary.traits)
                      .map(([name, count]) => `${name} ${count}`)
                      .join(" · ") || "（未统计）"}
                    {preview.summary.dropped_trait_images
                      ? `（剔除未返回类型 ${preview.summary.dropped_trait_images} 张）`
                      : ""}
                  </dd>
                </div>
              </dl>
              <ul className="mt-3 max-h-56 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto text-xs">
                {preview.cases.slice(0, 200).map((item) => (
                  <li key={item.cloud_id} className="flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-x-3 gap-y-1">
                    <span className="min-w-0 break-words">
                      {CASE_GRADE[item.case_level] ?? item.case_level} · {item.cloud_id}{" "}
                      {item.case_name}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-muted-foreground">
                      {item.image_count} 张 · {levelLine(item.image_levels, LEVELS.slice(0, 4))}
                    </span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
              {preview.cases.length > 200 ? (
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  仅列出前 200 个，导入按全部 {preview.cases.length} 个执行
                </p>
              ) : null}
              <div className="mt-3">
                <Button onClick={() => importSamples.mutate()} disabled={busy || !preview.summary.cases}>
                  {importSamples.isPending
                    ? "正在导入"
                    : `确认导入 ${preview.summary.cases} 个案例 / ${preview.summary.images} 张图`}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </>
  )
  return render ? render(controls) : controls
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/assets/case-sample-import.tsx =====
import { useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react"
import { FileXls } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { api } from "@/lib/api"
import type { CaseSampleImportResult, CaseSamplePreview } from "@/lib/types"

/** 素材（案例）维度样本：一行一个案例，正文里带图片总数、分数分布、类型分布。 */
export function CaseSampleImport({
  categoryKey,
  packageName,
  disabled,
  onImported,
  render,
}: {
  categoryKey: string
  packageName: string
  disabled?: boolean
  onImported: () => Promise<void> | void
  /** Keep the import session mounted while its drawer's controls are absent. */
  render?: (controls: ReactNode) => ReactNode
}) {
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null)
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<CaseSamplePreview | null>(null)

  function form(picked: File) {
    const body = new FormData()
    body.append("file", picked)
    body.append("category_key", categoryKey)
    if (packageName.trim()) body.append("package_name", packageName.trim())
    return body
  }

  const previewSamples = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (picked: File) =>
      api<CaseSamplePreview>("/api/design-case-samples/preview", { method: "POST", body: form(picked) }),
    onSuccess: (data, picked) => {
      setFile(picked)
      setPreview(data)
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => {
      setFile(null)
      setPreview(null)
      toast.error(error.message)
    },
  })

  const importSamples = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (picked: File) =>
      api<CaseSampleImportResult>("/api/design-case-samples/import", { method: "POST", body: form(picked) }),
    onSuccess: async (data) => {
      setFile(null)
      setPreview(null)
      await onImported()
      toast.success(
        `“${data.package.name}”已导入 ${data.summary.created_count} 个案例`
        + (data.summary.duplicate_count ? `，跳过重复 ${data.summary.duplicate_count} 个` : "")
        + (data.summary.rejected_rows ? `，无法解析 ${data.summary.rejected_rows} 行` : ""),
      )
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const busy = previewSamples.isPending || importSamples.isPending
  const flagged = preview?.rows.filter((row) => !row.ok || row.warnings.length) ?? []

  const controls = (
    <>
      <input
        ref={inputRef}
        type="file"
        accept=".xlsx"
        className="sr-only"
        tabIndex={-1}
        onChange={(event) => {
          const picked = event.target.files?.[0]
          event.target.value = ""
          if (picked) previewSamples.mutate(picked)
        }}
      />
      <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => inputRef.current?.click()} disabled={disabled || busy}>
        <FileXls />素材维度样本表
      </Button>
      {preview && file ? (
        <div className="col-span-full min-w-0 rounded-sm bg-surface-sunken p-4 text-sm">
          <p className="break-words font-semibold">
            {file.name}：共 {preview.summary.total_rows} 行，可导入 {preview.summary.usable_rows} 个案例
            {preview.summary.rejected_rows ? `，无法解析 ${preview.summary.rejected_rows} 行` : ""}
            {preview.summary.warned_rows ? `，${preview.summary.warned_rows} 行有提醒` : ""}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
            工作表 {preview.source.sheet_name} 第 {preview.source.column_number} 列
            {preview.source.header_is_data ? "（首行即数据）" : "（已跳过表头）"}
            ；带图片统计的案例才能进素材维度基准集
          </p>
          {flagged.length ? (
            <ul className="mt-3 max-h-48 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto text-xs">
              {flagged.slice(0, 50).map((row) => (
                <li key={row.row_number} className={`break-words ${row.ok ? "text-muted-foreground" : "text-danger"}`}>
                  第 {row.row_number} 行 {row.case_name || "（无标题）"}：
                  {[...row.issues, ...row.warnings].join("；")}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          ) : null}
          <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <Button onClick={() => importSamples.mutate(file)} disabled={busy || !preview.summary.usable_rows}>
              {importSamples.isPending ? "正在导入" : `确认导入 ${preview.summary.usable_rows} 个案例`}
            </Button>
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => { setFile(null); setPreview(null) }} disabled={busy}>
              取消
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </>
  )
  return render ? render(controls) : controls
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/assets/material-package-cover.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { ImageSquare } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import type { MaterialPackageCoverAsset } from "@/lib/types"

function CoverSlot({ asset }: { asset?: MaterialPackageCoverAsset }) {
  const [broken, setBroken] = useState(false)
  return (
    <span data-cover-slot="" className="relative flex min-h-0 min-w-0 items-center justify-center bg-surface-sunken first:row-span-2">
      {asset && !broken ? (
        <img src={asset.image_url} alt="" loading="lazy" onError={() => setBroken(true)} className="block h-full w-full min-w-0 object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <span className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1 p-2 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          <ImageSquare size={20} aria-hidden="true" />
          {broken ? "封面加载失败" : "无封面"}
        </span>
      )}
    </span>
  )
}

/** The approved collage crops covers only. Gallery/original images stay whole.
 * Data comes solely from the list's optional cover_assets; no cover lookup API.
 */
export function MaterialPackageCover({ assets }: { assets?: MaterialPackageCoverAsset[] }) {
  return (
    <span data-package-cover="" className="relative grid aspect-[16/10] w-full shrink-0 grid-cols-[2fr_1fr] grid-rows-2 gap-1 overflow-hidden rounded-md">
      {[0, 1, 2].map(index => <CoverSlot key={`${index}:${assets?.[index]?.asset_id ?? "empty"}:${assets?.[index]?.image_url ?? ""}`} asset={assets?.[index]} />)}
      <span aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 rounded-md shadow-[var(--image-outline)]" />
    </span>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/assets/pagination.ts =====
export type PageWindow = {
  page: number
  pageCount: number
  canPrevious: boolean
  canNext: boolean
}

export function pageWindow(total: number, offset: number, limit: number): PageWindow {
  const safeTotal = Number.isFinite(total) ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(total)) : 0
  const safeLimit = Number.isFinite(limit) && limit > 0 ? Math.floor(limit) : 1
  const pageCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(safeTotal / safeLimit))
  const requestedPage = Math.floor(Math.max(0, offset) / safeLimit) + 1
  const page = Math.min(pageCount, requestedPage)

  return {
    page,
    pageCount,
    canPrevious: page > 1,
    canNext: page < pageCount,
  }
}

/** 上传结果反馈面板 —— 从 assets-page.tsx 抽出。 */

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import type { UploadFeedback } from "./asset-format"

export function UploadFeedbackPanel({ feedback }: { feedback: UploadFeedback }) {
  const groups: Array<{
    key: "successful" | "skipped" | "failed"
    label: string
    tone: "success" | "warning" | "danger"
    items: string[]
  }> = [
    { key: "successful", label: "成功", tone: "success", items: feedback.successful },
    {
      key: "skipped",
      label: "跳过",
      tone: "warning",
      items: feedback.skipped.map((item) => `${item.filename} · ${item.reason}`),
    },
    {
      key: "failed",
      label: "失败",
      tone: "danger",
      items: feedback.failed.map((item) => `${item.filename} · ${item.reason}`),
    },
  ]
  return (
    <div className="min-w-0 border-t border-line py-4" aria-live="polite">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <p className="min-w-0 break-words text-sm font-bold">{feedback.source}处理结果</p>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {groups.map((group) => <Badge key={group.key} tone={group.tone}>{group.label} {group.items.length}</Badge>)}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3">
        {groups.map((group) => (
          <details key={group.key} className="visual-native-details border-t border-line pt-2" open={group.key !== "successful" && group.items.length > 0}>
            <summary className="cursor-pointer text-xs font-semibold">{group.label}文件（{group.items.length}）</summary>
            <div className="font-data mt-2 max-h-32 space-y-1 overflow-auto text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
              {group.items.length
                ? group.items.map((item, index) => <p className="break-all" key={`${item}-${index}`}>{item}</p>)
                : <p>无</p>}
            </div>
          </details>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/acceptance-query.ts =====
type AcceptanceRun = {
  id: number
  total: number
  status: string
  workflow_runtime?: { pending: number } | null
}

export function baselineAcceptanceReady(
  selectedRunId: number,
  run: AcceptanceRun | undefined,
): boolean {
  return selectedRunId > 0
    && run?.id === selectedRunId
    && run.total > 0
    && ["completed", "partial_failed", "failed"].includes(run.status)
    && (run.workflow_runtime?.pending ?? 0) === 0
}

/** The complete review queue is needed only after model execution settles. */
export function baselineAcceptanceQueryOptions<Item>({
  selectedRunId,
  run,
  fetchPage,
  requested = true,
}: {
  selectedRunId: number
  run: AcceptanceRun | undefined
  fetchPage: (runId: number, offset: number, limit: number, signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<{ items: Item[] }>
  requested?: boolean
}) {
  const pageSize = 1000
  return {
    queryKey: ["baseline-acceptance", selectedRunId, run?.total] as const,
    enabled: requested && baselineAcceptanceReady(selectedRunId, run),
    refetchInterval: false as const,
    queryFn: async ({ signal }: { signal: AbortSignal }): Promise<Item[][]> => {
      const pages: Item[][] = []
      for (let offset = 0; offset < (run?.total ?? 0); offset += pageSize) {
        signal.throwIfAborted()
        const detail = await fetchPage(selectedRunId, offset, pageSize, signal)
        pages.push(detail.items)
        if (detail.items.length < pageSize) break
      }
      return pages
    },
  }
}

export function isImageMimeType(value: unknown): boolean {
  return typeof value === "string" && value.startsWith("image/")
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/asset-selection.ts =====
/** 基准集创建时的素材勾选：素材分页展示，但选择集必须跨页累加。 */

export function isPageFullySelected(
  selected: ReadonlySet<number>,
  pageAssetIds: readonly number[],
): boolean {
  // 不能拿选择集总数去比本页条数：跨页累加后总数必然大于本页，判断会失真。
  return pageAssetIds.length > 0 && pageAssetIds.every((id) => selected.has(id))
}

export function togglePageSelection(
  selected: ReadonlySet<number>,
  pageAssetIds: readonly number[],
): Set<number> {
  const next = new Set(selected)
  if (isPageFullySelected(selected, pageAssetIds)) {
    for (const id of pageAssetIds) next.delete(id)
  } else {
    for (const id of pageAssetIds) next.add(id)
  }
  return next
}

export function addToSelection(
  selected: ReadonlySet<number>,
  assetIds: readonly number[],
): Set<number> {
  const next = new Set(selected)
  for (const id of assetIds) next.add(id)
  return next
}

/** 分页取全所有 id 时的 offset 序列。 */
export function selectionPageOffsets(total: number, pageSize: number): number[] {
  if (total <= 0 || pageSize <= 0) return []
  const offsets: number[] = []
  for (let offset = 0; offset < total; offset += pageSize) offsets.push(offset)
  return offsets
}

export function BalancedRebuildForm({
  survey,
  loading,
  error,
  perLevel,
  strategy,
  seed,
  onPerLevel,
  onStrategy,
  onSeed,
}: {

  loading: boolean
  error: unknown
  perLevel: number
  strategy: BalancedRebuildStrategy
  seed: number

  onStrategy: (value: BalancedRebuildStrategy) => void
  onSeed: (value: number) => void

        </p>
        {current.max_asset_id != null && <p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">

          <input

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/baseline-error-guidance.ts =====
const WORKFLOW_ERROR_GUIDANCE: Record<string, string> = {
  call_b_unavailable:
    "AI 提示词调用节点失败：调用 B 没有产出美感基础分。请先检查模型可用性并重试；持续失败时，在工作流管理器切换提示词或模型版本。",
  prompt_pipeline_mismatch:
    "AI 提示词调用节点配置不匹配：所选提示词属于另一条评分管线。请返回工作流管理器，更换提示词或规则合同版本。",
  operator_prompt_contract_conflict:
    "提示词与规则合同节点版本冲突：两者要求的输出结构不同。请返回工作流管理器，重新配对版本后再运行。",
}

const RESULT_ERROR_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  kg_output_value_invalid: "模型输出的字段值不符合合同规则",
  kg_output_fields_missing: "模型输出缺少合同要求的字段",
  kg_output_identity_mismatch: "模型没有原样返回素材 ID",
  structured_output_schema_mismatch: "模型输出不符合提示词冻结的 JSON 结构",
  model_config_snapshot_drift: "该版本冻结的模型已过期（模型管理里的模型 ID 或端点已变），模型未被调用；请改选或重新生成机制版本",
  model_source_unavailable: "冻结的模型已停用或密钥不可用",
  model_real_execution_disabled: "管理员已关闭该模型的真实调用授权",
  "technical:operator_canceled": "本轮被人工取消",
  missing_level: "未形成 L1-L5 有效等级",
  no_authoritative_score: "未形成服务端权威分数",
  missing_quality_evidence: "未返回画质证据",
  missing_confidence: "未返回模型置信度",
  missing_precheck_scope_status: "调用 A 未返回评测范围字段，无法继续调用 B",
  missing_prompt_b_response: "调用 B 未返回结果",
  missing_aesthetic_result: "未形成八个美感维度结果",
}

export function baselineErrorMessage(error: string) {
  // 整串就是一个已知错误码时直接给人话（原生工作流的条目只存错误码，没有冒号后缀）
  const direct = RESULT_ERROR_LABELS[error.trim()]
  if (direct) return direct
  for (const [code, guidance] of Object.entries(WORKFLOW_ERROR_GUIDANCE)) {
    const codeIndex = error.indexOf(code)
    if (codeIndex < 0) continue
    const detail = error
      .slice(codeIndex + code.length)
      .replace(/^[\s:：-]+/, "")
      .trim()
    return detail ? `${guidance} 系统详情：${detail}` : guidance
  }

  const separatorIndex = error.indexOf(":")
  if (separatorIndex < 0) return error
  const reasons = error.slice(separatorIndex + 1)
  return reasons
    .split(",")
    .map((reason) => RESULT_ERROR_LABELS[reason] ?? reason)
    .join("；")
}

/**
 * 把被拒的模型输出压成一句话：优先只展示出错的那个字段（从原因文案里认出字段名），
 * 认不出时展示前几个非空字段，避免把整段 JSON 糊到列表上。
 */
export function rejectedOutputSummary(
  output: Record<string, unknown>,
  reason: string,
): string {
  const entries = Object.entries(output).filter(([, value]) => value !== "" && value !== null)
  const named = entries.filter(([key]) => reason.includes(key))
  const picked = (named.length ? named : entries).slice(0, named.length ? named.length : 3)
  return picked.map(([key, value]) => `${key} = ${JSON.stringify(value)}`).join("；")
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/baseline-evidence-refresh.ts =====
import type { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"

export type BaselineEvidenceRunState = {
  id: number
  status: "running" | "completed" | "partial_failed" | "failed"
  workflow_runtime: { pending: number }
}

export async function refreshCompletedBaselineEvidence(
  queryClient: QueryClient,
  run: BaselineEvidenceRunState,
) {
  if (run.status === "running" || run.workflow_runtime.pending > 0) return false

  await Promise.all([
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-field-metrics", run.id] }),
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-semantic-metrics", run.id] }),
  ])
  return true
}
