import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import type { BaselineRunMetrics, BaselineTruthFieldMetric } from "@/lib/types"

import { baselineRunMetricsView } from "./baseline-run-metrics"
import { LevelPerformanceSummary } from "./level-performance-summary"
import { structuredFieldLabel } from "./structured-truth"

const percentFormatter = new Intl.NumberFormat("zh-CN", {
  style: "percent",
  maximumFractionDigits: 2,
})

export function BaselinePerformanceSummary({
  metrics,
  actions,
}: {
  metrics: BaselineRunMetrics
  actions?: ReactNode
}) {
  const view = baselineRunMetricsView(metrics)
  const imageMetrics = "image_grade_metrics" in metrics ? metrics.image_grade_metrics : null
  if (imageMetrics) {
    const expected = imageMetrics.compared + imageMetrics.missing
    return (
      <section className="space-y-5" aria-label="图片维度评测表现">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3"><h3 className="visual-card-title">图片维度评测表现</h3>{actions}</div>
        <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">准确率仅统计已有预测且按图片身份对齐的图片；缺失图片不进入准确率分母。覆盖率按本轮已纳入比对的真值图片计算。</p>
        <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
          <StructuredMetric label="逐图档位准确率" value={imageMetrics.compared ? formatPercent(imageMetrics.accuracy) : "—"} hint={imageMetrics.compared ? `${imageMetrics.correct}/${imageMetrics.compared} 张已对齐图片档位一致` : "暂无可比较图片"} />
          <StructuredMetric label="图片预测覆盖率" value={expected ? formatPercent(imageMetrics.compared / expected) : "—"} hint={`${imageMetrics.compared}/${expected} 张真值图片有可比较预测；缺失 ${imageMetrics.missing} 张`} />
          <StructuredMetric label="档位高估 / 低估" value={`${imageMetrics.over_graded} / ${imageMetrics.under_graded}`} hint="高估：模型档位优于人工；低估：模型档位低于人工" />
          <StructuredMetric label="缺失 / 额外图片" value={`${imageMetrics.missing} / ${imageMetrics.unexpected}`} hint={`本轮 ${view.validPredictions}/${view.total} 个案例产生可用结果`} />
        </div>
        <div className="baseline-matrix-frame overflow-x-auto rounded-md border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
          <table className="w-full text-left text-xs"><caption className="p-3 text-left font-bold">逐图档位混淆矩阵（行：人工真值；列：模型预测）</caption>
            <thead><tr><th className="p-3">人工 / 模型</th>{["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"].map((level) => <th key={level} className="p-3">{level}</th>)}</tr></thead>
            <tbody>{["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"].map((level) => <tr key={level} className="border-t border-[var(--line)]"><th className="p-3">{level}</th>{["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"].map((predicted) => <td key={predicted} className="p-3">{imageMetrics.confusion[level]?.[predicted] ?? 0}</td>)}</tr>)}</tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>
    )
  }
  if (view.levelMetrics) {
    return <LevelPerformanceSummary metrics={view.levelMetrics} actions={actions} />
  }
  // 等级回归但还没有任何成功结果：如实说明指标待生成，不要退回字段看板——
  // 那是知识图谱的口径，摆在灵感图页面上只会让人以为选错了类目。
  if (view.kind === "level") {
    return (
      <section className="space-y-5" aria-label="等级评测表现">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">当前回归任务</p>
            <h3 className="mt-1 visual-card-title">等级评测表现</h3>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-2">{actions}<p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">按 L1–L5 等级矩阵核对</p></div>
        </div>
        <div className="visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white px-5 py-8 text-center">
          <p className="text-sm font-bold">本轮尚无可用结果，等级指标暂未生成</p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            已完成 {view.validPredictions}/{view.total} 条
            {view.failed > 0 ? `，其中 ${view.failed} 条失败` : ""}
            。等级准确率与混淆矩阵需要至少一条成功结果才能计算；请先到下方逐条结果查看失败原因。
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>
    )
  }

  const aggregate = view.fieldAggregate
  const identity = "identity_aggregate" in metrics ? metrics.identity_aggregate : null
  const noSemanticTruth = !aggregate?.compared
  return (
    <section className="space-y-5" aria-label="字段评测表现">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">当前回归任务</p>
          <h3 className="mt-1 visual-card-title">字段评测表现</h3>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">{actions}<p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">知识图谱按字段核对，不套用 L1–L5 等级矩阵</p></div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        <StructuredMetric label="标签字段准确率" value={noSemanticTruth ? "待标注" : formatPercent(aggregate?.accuracy)} hint={noSemanticTruth ? "没有语义标签真值，无法评估标签质量" : `${aggregate!.correct}/${aggregate!.compared} 个标签字段完全一致`} />
        <StructuredMetric label="字段精确率" value={formatPercent(aggregate?.precision)} hint="预测出的标签中，有多少与真值一致" />
        <StructuredMetric label="字段召回率" value={formatPercent(aggregate?.recall)} hint="人工真值中的标签，有多少被成功找出" />
        <StructuredMetric label="有效覆盖率" value={formatPercent(view.coverage)} hint={`${view.validPredictions}/${view.total} 条已产生可用结果`} />
      </div>

      {identity && identity.compared > 0 && <p className="text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">身份与路线一致率 {formatPercent(identity.accuracy)}（{identity.correct}/{identity.compared} 个字段），仅核对 ID / 整体与单体路线，不计入标签质量。</p>}

      <section className="visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white" aria-label="逐字段表现">
        <div className="border-b border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-4">
          <p className="text-sm font-bold">逐字段表现</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">先看准确率定位需要纠偏的字段，再进入下方逐条结果查看具体差异。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
          {Object.entries(view.fieldMetrics).map(([fieldKey, field]) => (
            <FieldMetric key={fieldKey} fieldKey={fieldKey} metric={field} />
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
    </section>
  )
}

function StructuredMetric({
  label,
  value,
  hint,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  hint: string
}) {
  return (
    <div className="baseline-metric min-w-0 rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-5 py-4">
      <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p>
      <p className="font-data mt-2 text-2xl font-bold">{value}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{hint}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

function FieldMetric({
  fieldKey,
  metric,
}: {
  fieldKey: string
  metric: BaselineTruthFieldMetric
}) {
  return (
    <div className="border-b border-r border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-4">
      <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3">
        <p className="text-sm font-bold">{structuredFieldLabel(fieldKey)}</p>
        <p className="font-data text-lg font-bold">{formatPercent(metric.accuracy)}</p>
      </div>
      <p className="font-data mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
        {metric.correct}/{metric.compared} 条一致
      </p>
    </div>
  )
}

function formatPercent(value: number | null | undefined) {
  return typeof value === "number" ? percentFormatter.format(value) : "—"
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/baseline-regression-contract.ts =====
export type BaselineAcceptanceRow = {
  status: "queued" | "completed" | "failed"
  evaluation?: {
    human_review?: {
      decision?: "approved" | "corrected" | "rejected" | null
    } | null
  } | null
}

export type BaselineAcceptanceProgress = {
  reviewed: number
  total: number
  complete: boolean
}

import type { BaselineV3Revision, PromptVersion } from "@/lib/types"

export type BaselineV3Mode = "active" | "candidate"

export type CandidatePromptBindingResolution = {
  status: "available" | "unavailable" | "unbound"
  promptId: number | null
  requestedVersion: string | null
  reason?: "missing" | "stage" | "archived" | "pipeline_scope"
}

type CandidatePrompt = Pick<
  PromptVersion,
  "id" | "stage" | "version" | "status" | "pipeline_scope"
>

export function resolveCandidatePromptBinding(
  prompts: CandidatePrompt[],
  stage: "A" | "B",
  requestedVersion: string | null,
): CandidatePromptBindingResolution {
  const normalizedVersion = requestedVersion?.trim() || null
  if (!normalizedVersion) {
    return {
      status: "unbound",
      promptId: null,
      requestedVersion: null,
    }
  }

  const exactVersion = prompts.find(
    (prompt) => prompt.version === normalizedVersion && prompt.stage === stage,
  ) ?? prompts.find((prompt) => prompt.version === normalizedVersion)
  if (!exactVersion) {
    return {
      status: "unavailable",
      promptId: null,
      requestedVersion: normalizedVersion,
      reason: "missing",
    }
  }
  if (exactVersion.stage !== stage) {
    return {
      status: "unavailable",
      promptId: exactVersion.id,
      requestedVersion: normalizedVersion,
      reason: "stage",
    }
  }
  if (exactVersion.status === "archived") {
    return {
      status: "unavailable",
      promptId: exactVersion.id,
      requestedVersion: normalizedVersion,
      reason: "archived",
    }
  }
  if (exactVersion.pipeline_scope !== "baseline_regression" && exactVersion.pipeline_scope !== "shared") {
    return {
      status: "unavailable",
      promptId: exactVersion.id,
      requestedVersion: normalizedVersion,
      reason: "pipeline_scope",
    }
  }
  return {
    status: "available",
    promptId: exactVersion.id,
    requestedVersion: normalizedVersion,
  }
}

export function v3RevisionGroup(
  revision: Pick<BaselineV3Revision, "status" | "id">,
  projectedRevisionId: number,
): "active" | "candidate" | "history" {
  if (revision.id === projectedRevisionId || revision.status === "active") return "active"
  if (revision.status === "candidate") return "candidate"
  return "history"
}

export type V3CandidateLineage = "on_active_chain" | "diverged"

type LineageRevision = Pick<
  BaselineV3Revision,
  "id" | "status" | "parent_revision_id" | "category_key"
>

function ancestorIdsOf(
  startId: number,
  byId: Map<number, LineageRevision>,
): Set<number> {
  const chain = new Set<number>()
  let cursor = byId.get(startId)
  while (cursor && !chain.has(cursor.id)) {
    chain.add(cursor.id)
    if (cursor.parent_revision_id == null) break
    cursor = byId.get(cursor.parent_revision_id)
  }
  return chain
}

/**
 * Revisions are linked child -> parent (a new revision points at the one it
 * replaced), so the active revision sits at the *tip* of its chain and is never
 * an ancestor of a candidate. A candidate is therefore "on the active chain"
 * when it branches off any revision in the active revision's own ancestry.
 */
export function v3CandidateLineage(
  revision: LineageRevision,
  revisions: LineageRevision[],
  projectedRevisionId: number,
): V3CandidateLineage {
  const byId = new Map(revisions.map((item) => [item.id, item]))
  const activeChain = ancestorIdsOf(projectedRevisionId, byId)
  const seen = new Set<number>()
  let cursor: LineageRevision | undefined = byId.get(revision.id) ?? revision
  while (cursor) {
    if (activeChain.has(cursor.id)) return "on_active_chain"
    if (seen.has(cursor.id) || cursor.parent_revision_id == null) break
    seen.add(cursor.id)
    cursor = byId.get(cursor.parent_revision_id)
  }
  return "diverged"
}

/**
 * Selectability is intentionally limited to category + lifecycle status. Lineage
 * drift is surfaced as a note via {@link v3CandidateLineage} instead of blocking
 * the run, so operators keep full manual control over the graded matcher.
 */
export function isSelectableV3Candidate(
  revision: LineageRevision,
  _revisions: LineageRevision[],
  _projectedRevisionId: number,
): boolean {
  return revision.status === "candidate"
}

export function resolveV3PromptBinding(
  revision: Pick<BaselineV3Revision, "contract">,
  stage: "A" | "B",
): string | null {
  const bindings = revision.contract.prompt_bindings
  if (!bindings || typeof bindings !== "object") return null
  const key = stage === "A" ? "call_a_version" : "call_b_version"
  const value = (bindings as Record<string, unknown>)[key]
  return typeof value === "string" && value.trim() ? value : null
}

export type BaselineRunPayload = {
  workflow_version_id: number
  execution_mode?: "freeform" | "structured"
  model_execution_mode: "simulated" | "real"
  inference_mode: "online" | "batch"
  real_execution_confirmed: boolean
  category_context?: {
    source: "baseline_set"
    category_key: string
  }
}

export function baselineExecutionModeHelp(
  truthKind: "level" | "structured_fields",
  executionMode: "freeform" | "structured",
) {
  if (executionMode === "freeform") {
    return "自由实验不会要求固定 JSON、范围字段或八维输出；系统完整保留原始回答，能安全识别时自动计分，否则进入人工判断。"
  }
  if (truthKind === "structured_fields") {
    return "标准字段合同会逐项校验知识图谱字段、类型与当前路线；缺少必填字段或字段格式不合同时会停止并提示人工处理。"
  }
  return "标准评分合同沿用现有结构化协议；缺少范围、等级、分数或所选维度时会按合同失败。"
}

export function buildBaselineRunPayload(selection: {
  workflowVersionId: number
  executionMode?: "freeform" | "structured"
  categoryKey?: string | null
  modelExecutionMode?: "simulated" | "real"
  inferenceMode: "online" | "batch"
  realExecutionConfirmed?: boolean
}): BaselineRunPayload {
  const payload: BaselineRunPayload = {
    workflow_version_id: selection.workflowVersionId,
    model_execution_mode: selection.modelExecutionMode ?? "simulated",
    real_execution_confirmed: selection.realExecutionConfirmed ?? false,
    // 界面只有两种选择，且必须显式下发：省略等同于批量，但把它写清楚，
    // 服务端日志与冻结快照里就能直接看到这一轮是谁选的。
    inference_mode: selection.inferenceMode,
  }
  if (selection.executionMode) payload.execution_mode = selection.executionMode
  if (selection.categoryKey) {
    payload.category_context = {
      source: "baseline_set",
      category_key: selection.categoryKey,
    }
  }
  return payload
}

/**
 * The run the address pins, unless the page has just left that run.
 *
 * Picking another baseline set (or category) clears the run in local state at once,
 * but React Router applies the address change a render later. In that one render the
 * address still names the run that was left; treating it as pinned would select it
 * again, and its detail would then pull the page back to its own baseline set.
 *
 * ``leftRunId`` is the run of the address the page left, and only while that same
 * address is still the current one (the page compares the location key). Any later
 * navigation, to the same run included, is a new pin.
 */
export function baselinePinnedRunId(urlRunId: number, leftRunId: number): number {
  return urlRunId > 0 && urlRunId !== leftRunId ? urlRunId : 0
}

export function baselineRunIdAfterSetLoad(
  currentRunId: number,
  runs: Array<{ id: number }> | null,
  pinnedRunId = 0,
): number {
  if (pinnedRunId > 0) return pinnedRunId
  if (runs === null) return currentRunId
  if (runs.some((run) => run.id === currentRunId)) return currentRunId
  return runs[0]?.id ?? 0
}

export function baselineSetIdAfterListLoad(
  currentSetId: number,
  sets: Array<{ id: number }> | null,
  pinnedRunId = 0,
  awaitingListSetId = 0,
): number {
  // The run response selects its owning set; a category list must not clear
  // that run while its URL/category navigation is still settling.
  if (pinnedRunId > 0) return currentSetId
  if (sets === null) return currentSetId
  if (sets.some((set) => set.id === currentSetId)) return currentSetId
  // Chosen explicitly after this list was read: a set that was just created is
  // not in it yet. Missing from a list read before the choice does not mean the
  // set is gone; the next list read decides.
  if (awaitingListSetId > 0 && awaitingListSetId === currentSetId) return currentSetId
  return sets[0]?.id ?? 0
}

export function loadedBaselineSelectionIsCurrent(
  currentSelectionId: number,
  loadedSelectionId: number | null | undefined,
): boolean {
  return currentSelectionId > 0 && loadedSelectionId === currentSelectionId
}

export function loadedBaselineSetsMatchCategory(
  categoryKey: string,
  sets: Array<{ category_key: string }> | null,
): boolean {
  return sets !== null && sets.every((set) => set.category_key === categoryKey)
}

export function baselineRouteSearch(
  categoryKey: string,
  correction?: { runId: number; itemId: number },
): Record<string, string> {
  const params = { category_key: categoryKey }
  if (!correction) return params
  return {
    ...params,
    run: String(correction.runId),
    item: String(correction.itemId),
    mode: "correction",
  }
}

/** An explicit history selection replaces the old URL pin and its item. */
export function baselineSelectedRunSearch(categoryKey: string, runId: number): Record<string, string> {
  return { category_key: categoryKey, run: String(runId) }
}

export function baselineRunContextPatch(
  currentCategoryKey: string,
  currentBaselineSetId: number,
  target: { categoryKey: string; baselineSetId: number },
): { categoryKey?: string; baselineSetId?: number } {
  if (target.categoryKey !== currentCategoryKey) {
    return { categoryKey: target.categoryKey }
  }
  if (target.baselineSetId !== currentBaselineSetId) {
    return { baselineSetId: target.baselineSetId }
  }
  return {}
}

/** A loaded, URL-pinned run is authoritative; retain correction item/mode. */
export function baselineRunRouteSearch(
  search: URLSearchParams,
  selectedRunId: number,
  run: { id: number; category_key: string } | undefined,
): URLSearchParams | null {
  if (!run || run.id !== selectedRunId || Number(search.get("run")) !== run.id
    || !run.category_key || search.get("category_key") === run.category_key) return null
  const next = new URLSearchParams(search)
  next.set("category_key", run.category_key)
  return next
}

export function baselineAcceptanceProgressFromPages(
  pages: BaselineAcceptanceRow[][],
  runTerminal = true,
): BaselineAcceptanceProgress {
  return baselineAcceptanceProgress(pages.flat(), runTerminal)
}

export function baselineAcceptanceProgress(
  rows: BaselineAcceptanceRow[],
  runTerminal = true,
): BaselineAcceptanceProgress {
  const evaluable = rows.filter((row) => row.status === "completed" && row.evaluation)
  const reviewed = evaluable.filter((row) => (
    row.evaluation?.human_review?.decision === "approved"
    || row.evaluation?.human_review?.decision === "corrected"
    || row.evaluation?.human_review?.decision === "rejected"
  )).length
  const failedOrUnscored = rows.some((row) => row.status !== "completed" || !row.evaluation)
  return {
    reviewed,
    total: evaluable.length,
    complete: runTerminal && !failedOrUnscored && evaluable.length > 0 && reviewed === evaluable.length,
  }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/baseline-run-metrics.ts =====
import type {
  BaselineLevelMetrics,
  BaselineRunMetrics,
  BaselineTruthFieldMetric,
} from "@/lib/types"

export type BaselineRunMetricsView = {
  kind: "level" | "structured_fields"
  levelMetrics: BaselineLevelMetrics | null
  fieldAggregate: BaselineTruthFieldMetric | null
  fieldMetrics: Record<string, BaselineTruthFieldMetric>
  total: number
  validPredictions: number
  failed: number
  coverage: number
}

export function baselineRunMetricsView(
  metrics: BaselineRunMetrics,
): BaselineRunMetricsView {
  if (metrics.schema_version === "baseline-truth-metrics-v1") {
    // 口径以基准集为准，不靠「有没有算出等级指标」倒推：一轮全失败时
    // level_metrics 也会是 null，但它仍然是等级回归，不能显示成字段看板。
    // 历史 run 的 metrics 是当时算好存库的、没有 truth_kind，就退一步看字段
    // 统计：一个字段都没比较过（compared=0）说明基准集本来就没有字段真值。
    const comparedFields = metrics.field_aggregate?.compared ?? 0
    const kind = metrics.truth_kind
      ?? (metrics.level_metrics || comparedFields === 0
        ? "level"
        : "structured_fields")
    return {
      kind,
      levelMetrics: metrics.level_metrics,
      fieldAggregate: kind === "level" ? null : metrics.field_aggregate,
      fieldMetrics: kind === "level" ? {} : metrics.semantic_field_metrics ?? metrics.field_metrics,
      total: metrics.total,
      validPredictions: metrics.valid_predictions,
      failed: metrics.failed,
      coverage: metrics.coverage,
    }
  }

  return {
    kind: "level",
    levelMetrics: metrics,
    fieldAggregate: null,
    fieldMetrics: {},
    total: metrics.total,
    validPredictions: metrics.valid_predictions,
    failed: metrics.failed,
    coverage: metrics.total ? metrics.valid_predictions / metrics.total : 0,
  }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/candidate-release-panels.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { ArrowClockwise, WarningCircle } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { ApiError, baselineRegressionApi } from "@/lib/api"
import type { BaselineCandidateRebasePlan, BaselineV3Revision } from "@/lib/types"

type GateRegression = {
  code?: string
  message?: string
  field_key?: string
  accuracy_delta?: number
  recall_delta?: number
  failure_sample_ids?: number[]
}

function signedPercent(value: unknown): string | null {
  if (typeof value !== "number" || Number.isNaN(value)) return null
  const percent = value * 100
  const sign = percent > 0 ? "+" : ""
  return `${sign}${percent.toFixed(1)}%`
}

/**
 * Render why the release gate refused a candidate.
 *
 * The backend already reports every blocking reason, but a bare toast throws it
 * all away and leaves the operator with "rejected" and nothing to act on.
 */
export function CandidateGateRejection({ error }: { error: ApiError | null }) {
  if (!error) return null
  const detail = error.detail ?? {}
  const code = typeof detail.code === "string" ? detail.code : undefined
  const regressions = Array.isArray(detail.regressions)
    ? (detail.regressions as GateRegression[])
    : []
  const conflicts = Array.isArray(detail.conflicts)
    ? (detail.conflicts as Array<{ path?: string; reason?: string }>)
    : []

  return (
    <div
      className="mt-3 border border-alias-edge-036 bg-alias-surface-067 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-alias-content-041 rounded-md"
      data-testid="candidate-gate-rejection"
    >
      <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
        <WarningCircle className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="font-semibold">{error.message || "候选启用被拒绝"}</div>
          {code ? <div className="font-data mt-0.5 text-xs">{code}</div> : null}

          {code === "candidate_ancestry_conflict" ? (
            <div className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5">
              该候选不是现役版本的直接子代，直接启用会丢弃现役版本引入的改动。
              可用下方「变基到现役版本」生成一个挂在现役之上的等效候选，再重新回归。
            </div>
          ) : null}

          {regressions.length ? (
            <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1.5 text-xs leading-5">
              {regressions.map((item, index) => {
                const accuracy = signedPercent(item.accuracy_delta)
                const recall = signedPercent(item.recall_delta)
                return (
                  <li key={`${item.code ?? "regression"}-${item.field_key ?? index}`}>
                    <span className="font-semibold">{item.message ?? item.code}</span>
                    {accuracy || recall ? (
                      <span className="font-data ml-2">
                        {accuracy ? `准确率 ${accuracy}` : ""}
                        {accuracy && recall ? " · " : ""}
                        {recall ? `召回率 ${recall}` : ""}
                      </span>
                    ) : null}
                    {item.failure_sample_ids?.length ? (
                      <span className="ml-2">失败样本 {item.failure_sample_ids.length} 个</span>
                    ) : null}
                  </li>
                )
              })}
            </ul>
          ) : null}

          {conflicts.length ? (
            <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1 text-xs leading-5">
              {conflicts.map((item) => (
                <li key={item.path}>
                  <span className="font-data break-all">{item.path}</span>
                  <span className="ml-2">{item.reason}</span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

/**
 * Offer a rebase for a candidate that branched away from the active revision.
 *
 * Preview first: the operator sees which of their changes carry over and any
 * conflicts before anything is written.  Rebasing appends a new candidate and
 * leaves the diverged one untouched.
 */
export function CandidateRebasePanel({
  categoryKey,
  candidate,
  activeRevision,
  onRebased,
}: {
  categoryKey: string
  candidate: BaselineV3Revision
  activeRevision: BaselineV3Revision
  onRebased?: (revision: BaselineV3Revision) => void
}) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false)

  const preview = useQuery<BaselineCandidateRebasePlan>({
    queryKey: ["candidate-rebase-preview", categoryKey, candidate.revision, activeRevision.revision],
    queryFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.previewV3Rebase(categoryKey, candidate.revision),
    enabled: expanded,
  })

  const rebase = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.rebaseV3Revision(categoryKey, candidate.revision, {
      expected_projected_revision: activeRevision.revision,
      expected_projected_contract_hash: activeRevision.contract_hash,
    }),
    onSuccess: async (created) => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-v3-revisions", categoryKey] })
      toast.success(`已生成 Revision ${created.revision}，挂在现役 Revision ${activeRevision.revision} 之上`)
      onRebased?.(created)
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const plan = preview.data
  const conflicts = plan?.conflicts ?? []
  const rebaseError = rebase.error instanceof ApiError ? rebase.error : null

  return (
    <div className="mt-3 border border-alias-edge-039 bg-alias-surface-062 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-alias-content-030 rounded-md" data-testid="candidate-rebase-panel">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="font-semibold">该候选与现役版本已分叉，无法直接启用</div>
          <div className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5">
            Revision {candidate.revision} 挂在 Revision <span className="visual-code">{candidate.parent_revision_id ?? "—"}</span> 之上，
            而现役已是 Revision {activeRevision.revision}。变基会生成一个把本候选改动重放到现役之上的新候选，
            原候选保持不变。
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => setExpanded((value) => !value)}>
          <ArrowClockwise />{expanded ? "收起" : "查看变基影响"}
        </Button>
      </div>

      {expanded ? (
        <div className="mt-3 border-t border-alias-edge-054 pt-3">
          {preview.isLoading ? <div className="h-16 animate-pulse bg-alias-surface-034" /> : null}
          {preview.error ? (
            <div className="text-xs text-alias-content-041">变基预览失败：{preview.error.message}</div>
          ) : null}
          {plan && !plan.needed ? (
            <div className="text-xs">{plan.reason ?? "该候选无需变基。"}</div>
          ) : null}
          {plan && plan.needed ? (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <div className="text-xs leading-5">
                共同祖先 Revision <span className="visual-code">{plan.base_revision ?? plan.base_revision_id}</span>；
                将把本候选的 {plan.adopted_changes.length} 处改动重放到 Revision {plan.onto_revision ?? activeRevision.revision} 之上。
              </div>
              {plan.adopted_changes.length ? (
                <details className="visual-native-details text-xs">
                  <summary className="font-semibold">带过来的改动</summary>
                  <ul className="font-data mt-1.5 space-y-0.5 break-all">
                    {plan.adopted_changes.map((change) => <li key={change}>{change}</li>)}
                  </ul>
                </details>
              ) : null}
              {conflicts.length ? (
                <div className="border border-alias-edge-036 bg-alias-surface-067 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-alias-content-041 rounded-md">
                  <div className="font-semibold">存在 {conflicts.length} 处冲突，需人工决定取舍后才能变基</div>
                  <ul className="mt-1 space-y-0.5">
                    {conflicts.map((conflict) => (
                      <li key={conflict.path}>
                        <span className="font-data break-all">{conflict.path}</span>
                        <span className="ml-2">{conflict.reason}</span>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <Button
                  size="sm"
                  disabled={rebase.isPending}
                  onClick={() => {
                    if (window.confirm(`确认生成变基候选？将基于现役 Revision ${activeRevision.revision} 新建一个候选，原候选保持不变。`)) {
                      rebase.mutate()
                    }
                  }}
                >
                  <ArrowClockwise />{rebase.isPending ? "正在变基" : `变基到 Revision ${activeRevision.revision}`}
                </Button>
              )}
              <CandidateGateRejection error={rebaseError} />
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-analysis-panel.tsx =====
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react"
import { ArrowClockwise, Check, CheckSquare, File, Play, Square } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { ImagePreviewButton, type ImagePreview } from "@/components/image-lightbox"
import { baselineRegressionApi } from "@/lib/api"
import { useAccess } from "@/lib/access"
import type { BaselineRegressionItem, BaselineRegressionRun } from "@/lib/types"
import { candidateRefreshPlan } from "@/features/correction-contract/candidate-refresh"
import { isImageMimeType } from "@/features/baseline-regression/asset-presentation"
import { correctionLevelDisplay } from "@/features/baseline-regression/correction-level-display"
import { correctionStageClassName, correctionStageLabel, correctionStageRunningMessage, correctionStageState, correctionStageStateName, correctionStages, correctionStatusName, correctionStatusTone } from "@/features/baseline-regression/correction-stage-meta"
import { levelExplanationSummary } from "@/features/baseline-regression/level-explanation"
import { Metric, percent } from "@/features/baseline-regression/regression-page-shared"
import { baselineRunMetricsView } from "@/features/baseline-regression/baseline-run-metrics"
import { nativeWorkflowCorrectionSummary } from "@/features/baseline-regression/native-correction-summary"
import { structuredFieldLabel } from "@/features/baseline-regression/structured-truth"
import { realExecutionCanStart } from "@/features/baseline-regression/real-model-execution"

export function CorrectionAnalysisPanel({
  run,
  items,
  loading,
  onPreview,
  canDecide,
  onContinueCorrection,
}: {
  run: BaselineRegressionRun
  items: BaselineRegressionItem[]
  loading: boolean
  onPreview: (preview: ImagePreview) => void
  canDecide: boolean
  onContinueCorrection: (itemId: number) => void
}) {
  const canDevelop = useAccess().can("drafts:write")
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const deviations = useMemo(
    () => items.filter((item) => (
      item.status === "completed"
      && (item.deviation || hasHumanNodeCorrection(item))
    )),
    [items],
  )
  const deviationIds = useMemo(
    () => deviations
      .filter((item) => !run.selection.workflow || hasHumanNodeCorrection(item))
      .map((item) => item.id),
    [deviations, run.selection.workflow],
  )
  const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set())
  const [realExecutionConfirmed, setRealExecutionConfirmed] = useState(false)
  const [retryRealExecutionConfirmed, setRetryRealExecutionConfirmed] = useState(false)
  const sourceUsesRealModel = run.selection.model_execution_mode === "real"
  const correctionRuns = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["baseline-correction-runs", run.id],
    queryFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.listCorrectionRuns(run.id),
    refetchInterval: (query) => (
      query.state.data?.items.some((item) => item.status === "processing") ? 2500 : false
    ),
  })
  const latest = correctionRuns.data?.items[0]

  useEffect(() => {
    setSelectedIds(new Set(deviationIds))
  }, [run.id, deviationIds.join(",")])

  useEffect(() => {
    setRealExecutionConfirmed(false)
    setRetryRealExecutionConfirmed(false)
  }, [run.id])

  useEffect(() => {
    setRetryRealExecutionConfirmed(false)
  }, [latest?.id, latest?.attempt_count])

  const createCorrection = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.createCorrectionRun(
      run.id,
      Array.from(selectedIds),
      `baseline-correction-${run.id}-${Date.now()}`,
      realExecutionConfirmed,
    ),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      setRealExecutionConfirmed(false)
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-correction-runs", run.id] })
      toast.success("纠偏分析已启动；系统会推荐组件版本并执行一次候选回归")
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })
  const retryCorrection = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({
      correctionRunId,
      realExecutionConfirmed,
    }: {
      correctionRunId: number
      realExecutionConfirmed: boolean
    }) => baselineRegressionApi.retryCorrectionRun(
      correctionRunId,
      realExecutionConfirmed,
    ),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      setRetryRealExecutionConfirmed(false)
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-correction-runs", run.id] })
      toast.success("已重新启动纠偏分析")
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })
  const decideCorrection = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({
      correctionRunId,
      decision,
      note,
    }: {
      correctionRunId: number
      decision: "approved" | "rejected"
      note: string
    }) => baselineRegressionApi.decideCorrectionRun(correctionRunId, decision, note),
    onSuccess: async (result) => {
      const refreshPlan = result.mechanism_refresh
        ? candidateRefreshPlan(result.mechanism_refresh, run.id)
        : null
      await Promise.all([
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-correction-runs", run.id] }),
        ...(refreshPlan?.invalidate ?? [
          ["evaluation-categories"],
          ["prompts", run.category_key],
          ["baseline-v3-revisions", run.category_key],
        ]).map((queryKey) => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey })),
      ])
      toast.success(result.status === "approved" ? "候选结论已采纳" : "本轮候选已放弃")
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const allSelected = deviationIds.length > 0 && selectedIds.size === deviationIds.length
  const report = latest?.report ?? {}
  const runMetricsView = baselineRunMetricsView(run.metrics)
  const runPrimaryAccuracy = runMetricsView.levelMetrics?.exact_accuracy
    ?? runMetricsView.fieldAggregate?.accuracy
  const nativeSummary = latest ? nativeWorkflowCorrectionSummary(latest) : null
  const promptSuggestions = recordArray(report.prompt_suggestions)
  const dimensionSuggestions = recordArray(report.dimension_suggestions)
  const risks = stringArray(report.risks)
  const candidateRegression = recordValue(report.candidate_regression)
  const baselineMetrics = recordValue(candidateRegression.baseline_metrics)
  const candidateMetrics = recordValue(candidateRegression.candidate_metrics)
  const regressions = recordArray(candidateRegression.regressions)
  const approvalAllowed = nativeSummary?.approvalAllowed ?? candidateRegression.approval_allowed === true
  const recommendation = nativeSummary?.recommendation ?? stringValue(candidateRegression.recommendation)
  const blockers = (latest?.blockers ?? []).map((blocker) => (
    typeof blocker === "string" ? blocker : readableRecord(blocker)
  )).filter(Boolean)
  const latestLocked = latest?.status === "processing" || latest?.status === "awaiting_decision"

  const requestDecision = (decision: "approved" | "rejected") => {
    if (!latest || latest.status !== "awaiting_decision") return
    const approved = decision === "approved"
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      approved
        ? nativeSummary
          ? "确认采纳本轮候选结论？候选完整工作流与回归证据会保留，但不会自动切换该类目的增量评测版本。"
          : "确认采纳本轮候选结论？旧链路会按既有规则应用候选，但不会发布标签事实。"
        : "确认放弃本轮候选？本次候选与回归证据会保留，结论提交后不可修改。",
    )
    if (!confirmed) return
    decideCorrection.mutate({
      correctionRunId: latest.id,
      decision,
      note: approved ? "人工采纳自动纠偏候选结论" : "人工放弃自动纠偏候选",
    })
  }

  const continueCorrection = () => {
    if (!latest || latest.status !== "awaiting_decision") return
    const nextItemId = Array.from(selectedIds)[0] ?? deviations[0]?.id
    if (!nextItemId) return
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      "继续逐条纠偏会结束并保留本轮候选，再打开样本纠偏页。完成新的人工纠偏后，可重新发起下一轮分析与候选回归。",
    )
    if (!confirmed) return
    decideCorrection.mutate(
      {
        correctionRunId: latest.id,
        decision: "rejected",
        note: "运营选择继续逐条纠偏，保留并结束本轮候选",
      },
      { onSuccess: () => onContinueCorrection(nextItemId) },
    )
  }

  return (
    <section
      id="baseline-correction-panel"
      className="mt-6 visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white"
      role="tabpanel"
      aria-labelledby="baseline-correction-tab"
    >
      <div className="grid gap-5 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-5 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto] lg:items-end">
        <div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <h3 className="visual-card-title">纠偏分析与版本推荐</h3>
            <Badge tone="active">分析、推荐、试跑自动完成</Badge>
            <Badge tone="neutral">最终人工决策</Badge>
          </div>
          <p className="mt-2 max-w-3xl text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            启动后，系统会按人工证据定位受影响的步骤，推荐新的提示词或规则模块版本，再用推荐版本自动试跑一次。最后由运营选择采纳、继续纠偏或放弃。
          </p>
          <div className="mt-3 grid gap-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)] sm:grid-cols-2">
            <p className="border-l-2 border-[var(--line-strong)] pl-3">
              结果都在当前页：先逐条纠偏，再进入“纠偏分析与版本推荐”。分析报告、版本建议、试跑指标和风险提示都会在这里展示。
            </p>
            <p className="border-l-2 border-primary pl-3">
              推荐版本试跑完成后，本区域会进入“等待人工决策”。采纳只记录本轮结论，不会自动切换增量评测版本。
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Button
            variant="secondary"
            onClick={() => correctionRuns.refetch()}
            disabled={correctionRuns.isFetching}
          >
            <ArrowClockwise />刷新状态
          </Button>
          <Button
            onClick={() => createCorrection.mutate()}
            disabled={
              !canDevelop || run.status === "running"
              || !selectedIds.size
              || createCorrection.isPending
              || latestLocked
              || !realExecutionCanStart(
                sourceUsesRealModel ? "real" : "simulated",
                realExecutionConfirmed,
              )
            }
          >
            <Play weight="fill" />
            {createCorrection.isPending ? "正在启动" : `启动纠偏分析 (${selectedIds.size})`}
          </Button>
        </div>
        {sourceUsesRealModel && (
          <label className="visual-native-choice rounded-md lg:col-span-2 flex items-start gap-3 border border-alias-edge-049 bg-alias-surface-077 px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-022">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              className="visual-native-checkbox mt-0.5 size-4"
              checked={realExecutionConfirmed}
              onChange={(event) => setRealExecutionConfirmed(event.target.checked)}
            />
            <span>
              <strong className="block text-alias-content-018">真实模型候选回归会再次产生费用</strong>
              我确认使用推荐后的候选提示词版本重新调用真实模型，并知晓所选素材会再次发送至火山方舟。
            </span>
          </label>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="grid min-w-0 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(320px,0.88fr)_minmax(0,1.12fr)]">
        <div className="min-w-0 border-b border-[var(--line)] lg:border-r lg:border-b-0">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 px-4 py-3">
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-bold">选择已完成人工纠偏的样本</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">可分析 {deviationIds.length} / 共 {deviations.length} · 已选 {selectedIds.size}</p>
            </div>
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              size="sm"
              disabled={!deviationIds.length || latestLocked}
              onClick={() => setSelectedIds(allSelected ? new Set() : new Set(deviationIds))}
            >
              {allSelected ? <CheckSquare weight="fill" /> : <Square />}
              {allSelected ? "取消全选" : "全选偏差"}
            </Button>
          </div>
          <div className="max-h-[620px] overflow-auto">
            {loading ? (
              <div className="h-64 animate-pulse bg-white" />
            ) : deviations.length ? (
              <div className="divide-y divide-[var(--line)]">
                {deviations.map((item) => {
                  const levelDisplay = correctionLevelDisplay(item)
                  const structuredTruth = item.truth_kind === "structured_fields"
                  const analysisReady = !run.selection.workflow || hasHumanNodeCorrection(item)
                  return (
                  <div key={item.id} className="grid grid-cols-[auto_52px_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-3 px-4 py-3 hover:bg-alias-surface-051">
                    <label className="visual-native-choice shrink-0 self-start"><input
                      type="checkbox"
                      aria-label={`选择偏差样本：${item.asset.name}`}
                      className="visual-native-checkbox mt-4 size-4"
                      checked={selectedIds.has(item.id)}
                      disabled={latestLocked || !analysisReady}
                      onChange={(event) => setSelectedIds((current) => {
                        const next = new Set(current)
                        if (event.target.checked) next.add(item.id)
                        else next.delete(item.id)
                        return next
                      })}
                    /></label>
                    {isImageMimeType(item.asset.mime_type) ? (
                      <ImagePreviewButton
                        src={item.image_url}
                        alt={item.asset.name}
                        imageClassName="size-12"
                        onPreview={onPreview}
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <div className="flex size-12 items-center justify-center border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-029 text-[var(--muted)] rounded-md"><File size={21} /></div>
                    )}
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                        <p className="file-name min-w-0 truncate text-sm">{item.asset.name}</p>
                        {structuredTruth ? (
                          <><Badge tone={item.deviation ? "danger" : "warning"}>{item.deviation ? `${item.field_deviations?.length ?? 0} 个字段有偏差` : "已保存人工纠偏"}</Badge><Badge tone="neutral">结构化字段真值</Badge></>
                        ) : (
                          <><Badge tone="danger">{levelDisplay.level ?? "—"} → {item.predicted_level ?? "—"}</Badge><Badge tone={levelDisplay.source === "human_correction" ? "warning" : "neutral"}>{levelDisplay.source === "human_correction" ? "人工纠偏等级" : "冻结预期等级"}</Badge></>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      {!structuredTruth && levelDisplay.source === "human_correction" && (
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-4 text-alias-content-033">
                          原冻结预期 {item.expected_level} · 当前展示以已完成人工纠偏为准
                        </p>
                      )}
                      {!structuredTruth && levelDisplay.source === "frozen_expected" && (
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-4 text-[var(--muted)]">
                          尚未保存人工纠偏等级 · 当前仅用于筛选偏差样本
                        </p>
                      )}
                      <p className="mt-1 line-clamp-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{structuredTruth ? `待确认字段：${(item.field_deviations ?? []).map(structuredFieldLabel).join("、") || "请查看节点明细"}` : levelExplanationSummary(item)}</p>
                      {!analysisReady && (
                        <Button className="mt-2" size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => onContinueCorrection(item.id)}>
                          先逐条纠偏
                        </Button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  )
                })}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p className="px-5 py-12 text-center text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">
                当前运行没有偏差样本或已保存的人工纠偏证据。
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="min-w-0 px-5 py-5">
          {!latest ? (
            <div className="border-y border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-12 text-center">
              <p className="text-sm font-bold">尚未启动纠偏分析</p>
              <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">选择左侧偏差样本后启动，系统会完成节点分析、组件版本推荐和一次候选回归，最后等待人工决策。</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-[var(--line-strong)] pb-4">
                <div>
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <h4 className="text-base font-bold">纠偏分析 #<span className="visual-code">{latest.id}</span></h4>
                    <Badge tone={correctionStatusTone(latest)}>{correctionStatusName(latest)}</Badge>
                    <Badge>第 {latest.attempt_count} 次尝试</Badge>
                  </div>
                  <p className="font-data mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                    {latest.selected_item_ids.length} 个冻结样本 · {latest.updated_at}
                  </p>
                </div>
                {latest.status === "failed" && latest.error?.retryable && (
                  <div className="flex max-w-md flex-col items-end gap-2">
                    {sourceUsesRealModel && (
                      <label className="visual-native-choice rounded-md flex items-start gap-2 border border-alias-edge-049 bg-alias-surface-077 px-3 py-2 text-left text-xs leading-4 text-alias-content-022">
                        <input
                          type="checkbox"
                          className="visual-native-checkbox mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0"
                          checked={retryRealExecutionConfirmed}
                          onChange={(event) => setRetryRealExecutionConfirmed(event.target.checked)}
                        />
                        <span>
                          <strong className="block">本次重新执行会再次产生费用</strong>
                          我确认再次调用真实模型，并知晓冻结素材会再次发送至火山方舟。
                        </span>
                      </label>
                    )}
                    <Button
                      variant="secondary"
                      size="sm"
                      disabled={
                        !canDevelop || retryCorrection.isPending
                        || (sourceUsesRealModel && !retryRealExecutionConfirmed)
                      }
                      onClick={() => retryCorrection.mutate({
                        correctionRunId: latest.id,
                        realExecutionConfirmed: retryRealExecutionConfirmed,
                      })}
                    >
                      <ArrowClockwise />{retryCorrection.isPending ? "正在重新执行" : "重新执行"}
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 border-y border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 2xl:grid-cols-5">
                {correctionStages.map((stage, index) => {
                  const state = correctionStageState(latest, stage.key)
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={stage.key}
                      className="min-w-0 border-r border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-3 last:border-r-0"
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className={`flex size-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md border text-xs font-bold ${correctionStageClassName(state)}`}>
                          {state === "completed" ? <Check weight="bold" /> : index + 1}
                        </span>
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold leading-4">{stage.label}</p>
                      </div>
                      <p className="mt-1 pl-8 text-xs leading-4 text-[var(--muted)]">
                        {correctionStageStateName(state)}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  )
                })}
              </div>

              {latest.status === "processing" && (
                <div className="mt-4 border border-alias-edge-035 bg-alias-surface-044 px-4 py-3 rounded-md">
                  <div className="flex justify-between gap-4 text-xs font-bold">
                    <span>{correctionStageRunningMessage(latest.stage)}</span>
                    <span className="font-data">{latest.progress}%</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-2 h-1.5 overflow-hidden bg-white">
                    <div className="h-full w-full origin-left bg-brand transition-transform duration-[var(--visual-ease-out-ms)] ease-[var(--visual-ease-out)]" style={{ transform: `scaleX(${latest.progress / 100})` }} />
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {latest.status === "failed" && (
                <div className="mt-4 border border-alias-edge-048 bg-alias-surface-066 px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-041 rounded-md">
                  <p className="font-bold">{correctionStageLabel(latest.stage)}失败{latest.error?.code ? ` · ${latest.error.code}` : ""}</p>
                  <p className="mt-1">{latest.error?.message || "未返回具体失败原因，可重新执行本次冻结样本。"}</p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-alias-content-024">
                    重新执行会沿用本次冻结样本；真实模型重试仍需再次确认费用和素材外发。
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              {(blockers.length > 0) && (
                <div className="mt-4 border border-alias-edge-049 bg-alias-surface-077 px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-033 rounded-md">
                  <p className="font-bold">自动处理提示</p>
                  {blockers.map((blocker, index) => <p key={`${blocker}-${index}`} className="mt-1">{blocker}</p>)}
                </div>
              )}

              {(latest.status === "awaiting_decision"
                || latest.status === "approved"
                || latest.status === "rejected") && (
                <>
                  <div className="visual-panel-group mt-5 grid gap-px visual-panel border border-[var(--line)] bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
                    <Metric label={nativeSummary?.accuracyKind === "field" ? "基准字段准确率" : nativeSummary?.accuracyKind === "level" ? "基准等级准确率" : "基准准确率"} value={formatPercent(nativeSummary?.baselineAccuracy ?? numberValue(baselineMetrics.exact_accuracy) ?? runPrimaryAccuracy ?? null)} />
                    <Metric label={nativeSummary?.accuracyKind === "field" ? "候选字段准确率" : nativeSummary?.accuracyKind === "level" ? "候选等级准确率" : "候选准确率"} value={formatPercent(nativeSummary?.candidateAccuracy ?? numberValue(candidateMetrics.exact_accuracy))} />
                    <Metric label="准确率变化" value={formatSignedPercent(nativeSummary?.accuracyDelta ?? numberValue(candidateRegression.exact_accuracy_delta))} />
                    <Metric label={nativeSummary ? "增量版本" : "相邻准确率变化"} value={nativeSummary ? "保持不变" : formatSignedPercent(numberValue(candidateRegression.adjacent_accuracy_delta))} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="visual-panel-group mt-5 grid gap-px visual-panel border border-[var(--line)] bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
                    <ReportFact label={nativeSummary ? "原完整工作流" : "机制候选"} value={nativeSummary?.baselineWorkflowVersionId ? `Workflow #${nativeSummary.baselineWorkflowVersionId}` : latest.candidate_revision_id ? `Revision #${latest.candidate_revision_id}` : "—"} />
                    <ReportFact label={nativeSummary ? "候选完整工作流" : "候选提示词"} value={nativeSummary?.candidateWorkflowVersionId ? `Workflow #${nativeSummary.candidateWorkflowVersionId}` : latest.orchestration.candidate_prompt?.version ?? "—"} />
                    <ReportFact label="候选回归" value={latest.regression_run_id ? `Run #${latest.regression_run_id}` : "—"} />
                    <ReportFact label="回归建议" value={recommendation === "approve" ? "建议采纳" : "建议放弃"} />
                  </div>
                  {nativeSummary ? (
                    <div className="mt-6 space-y-5">
                      <section>
                        <h5 className="visual-item-title">受影响节点</h5>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">只根据本轮已保存的人工纠偏证据定位，不会修改未受影响路线。</p>
                        <div className="mt-3 divide-y divide-[var(--line)] border-y border-[var(--line)]">
                          {nativeSummary.affectedNodes.map((node) => (
                            <div key={node.nodeInstanceId} className="grid gap-2 py-3 text-xs sm:grid-cols-[minmax(150px,0.7fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
                              <span className="font-bold"><span className="visual-code">{node.nodeInstanceId}</span></span>
                              <span className="leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{node.componentKind === "prompt" ? "提示词节点" : "规则模块节点"} · {node.assetKey || "未记录调用标识"} · {node.fields.length ? `纠偏字段：${node.fields.map(structuredFieldLabel).join("、")}` : "按节点证据分析"} · {node.evidenceCount} 条证据</span>
                            </div>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </section>
                      <section>
                        <h5 className="visual-item-title">组件版本建议</h5>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">提示词与规则模块一视同仁，按节点选择新版本，再组成候选完整工作流。</p>
                        <div className="mt-3 divide-y divide-[var(--line)] border-y border-[var(--line)]">
                          {nativeSummary.recommendations.map((item) => (
                            <div key={`${item.nodeInstanceId}-${item.candidateVersionId}`} className="grid gap-2 py-3 text-xs sm:grid-cols-[minmax(150px,0.7fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
                              <span className="font-bold"><span className="visual-code">{item.nodeInstanceId}</span></span>
                              <span className="leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{item.assetKey || "组件"} · 版本 #<span className="visual-code">{item.sourceVersionId}</span> → 推荐版本 #<span className="visual-code">{item.candidateVersionId}</span>{item.mode === "generated" ? "（根据人工纠偏生成）" : "（复用已有独立版本）"}</span>
                            </div>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </section>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                  <div className="mt-6">
                    <h5 className="visual-item-title">分析与候选变更摘要</h5>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">系统已把以下分析落入统一机制候选，并使用同一基准集完成验证。</p>
                    <div className="mt-3 divide-y divide-[var(--line)] border-y border-[var(--line)]">
                      {[...promptSuggestions, ...dimensionSuggestions].map((suggestion, index) => (
                        <div key={`${stringValue(suggestion.code) || stringValue(suggestion.dimension_key) || "suggestion"}-${index}`} className="grid gap-2 py-3 text-xs sm:grid-cols-[120px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
                          <span className="font-bold">
                            {stringValue(suggestion.dimension_key) || "提示词建议"}
                            {stringValue(suggestion.priority) ? ` · ${priorityName(stringValue(suggestion.priority))}` : ""}
                          </span>
                          <span className="leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{stringValue(suggestion.message) || readableRecord(suggestion)}</span>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                      {!promptSuggestions.length && !dimensionSuggestions.length && (
                        <p className="py-5 text-center text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">本次未形成可展示的改进建议。</p>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  )}
                  {risks.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="mt-5 border border-alias-edge-049 bg-alias-surface-077 px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-033 rounded-md">
                      <p className="font-bold">报告风险提示</p>
                      {risks.map((risk) => <p key={risk} className="mt-1">{risk}</p>)}
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {regressions.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="mt-5 border border-alias-edge-048 bg-alias-surface-066 px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-041 rounded-md">
                      <p className="font-bold">候选回归未通过</p>
                      {regressions.map((regression, index) => (
                        <p key={`${stringValue(regression.code)}-${index}`} className="mt-1">
                          {stringValue(regression.message) || readableRecord(regression)}
                        </p>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {latest.status === "awaiting_decision" && canDecide && (
                    <div className="mt-5 border-l-2 border-primary bg-alias-surface-048 px-4 py-4">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-5">
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-sm font-bold">等待人工决策</p>
                          <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                            中间步骤已全部完成。采纳会保留候选完整工作流作为人工认可结论，但不会自动切换增量评测版本；正式增量仍需回到工作流管理器单独设置。
                          </p>
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex shrink-0 flex-wrap justify-end gap-2">
                          <Button
                            variant="danger"
                            disabled={decideCorrection.isPending}
                            onClick={() => requestDecision("rejected")}
                          >
                            放弃候选
                          </Button>
                          <Button
                            variant="secondary"
                            disabled={decideCorrection.isPending}
                            onClick={continueCorrection}
                          >
                            继续逐条纠偏
                          </Button>
                          <Button
                            disabled={!approvalAllowed || decideCorrection.isPending}
                            title={approvalAllowed ? undefined : "候选回归未通过，不能采纳"}
                            onClick={() => requestDecision("approved")}
                          >
                            <Check weight="bold" />采纳候选结论
                          </Button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {latest.status === "awaiting_decision" && !canDecide && (
                    <div className="mt-5 border-l-2 border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-051 px-4 py-4">
                      <p className="text-sm font-bold">等待系统管理员决策</p>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                        候选与回归证据已就绪。只有系统管理员可以采纳或放弃候选；运营仍可继续逐条纠偏。
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {(latest.status === "approved" || latest.status === "rejected") && (
                    <div className={`mt-5 border-l-2 px-4 py-4 ${latest.status === "approved" ? "border-primary bg-alias-surface-048" : "border-danger bg-alias-surface-066"}`}>
                      <p className="text-sm font-bold">
                        人工结论：{latest.status === "approved" ? "已采纳候选结论" : "已放弃候选"}
                      </p>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                        {latest.decided_by || "管理员"} · {latest.decided_at || latest.updated_at}
                        {latest.decision_note ? ` · ${latest.decision_note}` : ""}
                      </p>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">该人工结论不可修改，候选、回归与决策证据均已保留。</p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </>
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

export function recordValue(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
    ? value as Record<string, unknown>
    : {}
}

function hasHumanNodeCorrection(item: BaselineRegressionItem) {
  return (item.evaluation?.correction_history ?? []).some((event) => {
    const metadata = event as unknown as {
      corrector_confidence?: unknown
      corrector_policy?: unknown
    }
    return metadata.corrector_confidence == null && !metadata.corrector_policy
  })
}

export function recordArray(value: unknown): Array<Record<string, unknown>> {
  return Array.isArray(value) ? value.map(recordValue).filter((item) => Object.keys(item).length) : []
}

export function stringArray(value: unknown): string[] {
  return Array.isArray(value) ? value.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string") : []
}

export function stringValue(value: unknown): string {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : ""
}

export function numberValue(value: unknown): number | null {
  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null
}

export function readableRecord(value: unknown): string {
  const record = recordValue(value)
  return stringValue(record.message) || stringValue(record.title) || stringValue(record.code)
}

export function formatSignedPercent(value: number | null) {
  if (value === null) return "—"
  const sign = value > 0 ? "+" : ""
  return `${sign}${(value * 100).toFixed(1)}%`
}

export function formatPercent(value: number | null) {
  return value === null ? "—" : percent(value)
}

export function priorityName(priority: string) {
  if (priority === "high") return "高优先"
  if (priority === "medium") return "中优先"
  if (priority === "low") return "低优先"
  return priority
}

export function ReportFact({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="border-y border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-3">
      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p>
      <p className="font-data mt-1 text-lg font-bold">{value}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-autosave.ts =====
import { createIdempotencyToken } from "../../lib/idempotency-token.ts"
import { correctionDraftFromView, correctionNodeChanged, correctionSubmissionPayload, updateCorrectionDraft } from "../correction-contract/correction-view-state.ts"
import type { CorrectionDraft, CorrectionSubmissionRequest, CorrectionView } from "../correction-contract/types.ts"

type PendingEdit = { key: string; value: unknown; payload?: CorrectionSubmissionRequest }
const same = (a: unknown, b: unknown) => JSON.stringify(a) === JSON.stringify(b)

/** One serial queue per material; retries retain the original idempotency key. */
export class CorrectionAutosave {
  view: CorrectionView
  draft: CorrectionDraft
  queue: PendingEdit[] = []
  busy = false
  error = ""
  failedStatus = 0
  saved = new Set<string>()
  private timers = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>()
  private composing = new Set<string>()
  private notify: () => void
  private send: (payload: CorrectionSubmissionRequest) => Promise<CorrectionView>
  private accept: (view: CorrectionView) => void
  private confirmRerun: () => boolean

  constructor(view: CorrectionView, options: {
    notify: () => void; send: (payload: CorrectionSubmissionRequest) => Promise<CorrectionView>
    accept: (view: CorrectionView) => void; confirmRerun: () => boolean
  }) {
    this.view = view
    this.draft = correctionDraftFromView(view)
    this.notify = options.notify; this.send = options.send; this.accept = options.accept
    this.confirmRerun = options.confirmRerun
  }

  get dirty() { return this.busy || this.queue.length > 0 || Object.values(this.draft).some(node => node.dirty) }

  edit(key: string, value: unknown) {
    this.draft = updateCorrectionDraft(this.draft, key, { value })
    this.saved.delete(key)
    this.notify()
  }

  schedule(key: string, delay = 800) {
    const timer = this.timers.get(key)
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer)
    this.timers.delete(key)
    if (!this.composing.has(key)) this.timers.set(key, setTimeout(() => this.commit(key), delay))
  }

  composition(key: string, active: boolean) {
    if (active) this.composing.add(key)
    else this.composing.delete(key)
    this.schedule(key)
  }

  cancelScheduled() {
    this.timers.forEach(timer => clearTimeout(timer))
    this.timers.clear()
  }

  commit(key: string) {
    const timer = this.timers.get(key)
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer)
    this.timers.delete(key)
    if (this.composing.has(key)) return
    const node = this.view.nodes.find(node => node.node_key === key)
    const edited = this.draft[key]
    if (!node || !edited?.dirty) return
    const last = this.queue.filter(task => task.key === key).at(-1)
    if (last && same(last.value, edited.value)) return
    if (!last && !correctionNodeChanged(node, edited)) {
      this.draft[key] = { ...edited, dirty: false }; this.notify(); return
    }
    // A rejected input did not commit: replace it with the user's corrected input.
    if (this.error && this.failedStatus === 422 && this.queue[0]?.key === key) {
      this.queue.shift(); this.error = ""; this.failedStatus = 0
    }
    this.queue.push({ key, value: structuredClone(edited.value) })
    this.notify()
    void this.flush()
  }

  retry() { this.error = ""; this.failedStatus = 0; void this.flush() }

  /** Explicit conflict resolution only; preserve edits while taking a fresh revision. */
  refresh(view: CorrectionView) {
    this.view = view
    for (const task of this.queue) delete task.payload
    this.retry()
  }

  private async flush() {
    if (this.busy || this.error || !this.queue.length) return
    this.busy = true; this.notify()
    try {
      while (this.queue.length) {
        const task = this.queue[0]
        if (!task.payload) {
          const node = this.view.nodes.find(node => node.node_key === task.key)
          if (!node || this.view.read_only) throw new Error("字段当前不可编辑，请刷新核对")
          const paid = node.metadata?.paid_rerun_required === true
          if (paid && !this.confirmRerun()) {
            this.queue.shift()
            this.draft[task.key] = correctionDraftFromView(this.view)[task.key]
            continue
          }
          const reason = "运营在表格中手动修正字段（未补充人工备注）"
          const change = updateCorrectionDraft(correctionDraftFromView(this.view), task.key, {
            value: task.value, reason,
            evidence: [{ source: "operator_table_edit", text: "人工直接填写的字段修正值", field: task.key, value: task.value }],
          })
          task.payload = correctionSubmissionPayload(change, this.view,
            `table:${this.view.run_id}:${this.view.item_id}:${createIdempotencyToken()}`, { realExecutionConfirmed: paid })
        }
        const response = await this.send(task.payload)
        this.view = response
        const fresh = correctionDraftFromView(response)
        for (const [key, edited] of Object.entries(this.draft)) {
          if (edited.dirty && (key !== task.key || !same(edited.value, task.value))) fresh[key] = edited
        }
        this.draft = fresh
        this.queue.shift()
        this.saved.add(task.key)
        this.accept(response)
        this.notify()
      }
    } catch (error) {
      this.error = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "保存失败，请重试"
      this.failedStatus = Number((error as { status?: number })?.status ?? 0)
    } finally {
      this.busy = false; this.notify()
    }
  }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-cache.ts =====
import type { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import type { BaselineRegressionDetail, BaselineRegressionItem } from "../../lib/types"

/** Refresh the saved material, preserving every unrelated row and page. */
export async function refreshCorrectionItem(
  client: QueryClient, runId: number, itemId: number, revision: number,
  fetchItem: () => Promise<BaselineRegressionItem>,
) {
  const item = await fetchItem()
  if (item.id !== itemId || (item.evaluation?.review_revision ?? 0) < revision) return
  const replace = (rows: BaselineRegressionItem[]) => rows.map(previous =>
    previous.id === itemId && (previous.evaluation?.review_revision ?? 0) <= (item.evaluation?.review_revision ?? 0)
      ? item : previous)
  client.setQueriesData<BaselineRegressionDetail>({
    queryKey: ["baseline-regression", runId],
    predicate: query => typeof query.queryKey[2] === "number",
  }, old => old ? { ...old, items: replace(old.items) } : old)
  client.setQueriesData<BaselineRegressionItem[][]>({ queryKey: ["baseline-acceptance", runId] },
    old => old?.map(replace))
  void client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-acceptance-summary", runId] })
  // Full evidence remains a distinct payload and is fetched when reopened.
  await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-regression", runId, "item", itemId], refetchType: "none" })
  await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-correction-view", runId, itemId], exact: true, refetchType: "none" })
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-draft-store.ts =====
import type { CorrectionAutosave } from "./correction-autosave"

type DraftEntry = {
  editor: CorrectionAutosave | null
  note: string
  noteBase: string
  noteOpen: boolean
  noteRequest: { text: string; idempotency_key: string } | null
  noteBusy: boolean
  active: boolean
  listeners: Set<() => void>
}
const drafts = new Map<string, DraftEntry>()
let account: number | null = null

export function correctionDraftEntry(runId: number, itemId: number): DraftEntry {
  const key = `${runId}:${itemId}`
  let entry = drafts.get(key)
  if (!entry) {
    entry = { editor: null, note: "", noteBase: "", noteOpen: false, noteRequest: null, noteBusy: false, active: true, listeners: new Set() }
    drafts.set(key, entry)
  }
  return entry
}
export function correctionNoteDirty(entry: Pick<DraftEntry, "note" | "noteBase">) {
  return entry.note.trim() !== entry.noteBase.trim()
}
export function openCorrectionNote(entry: DraftEntry, notes: { id: number; text: string }[]) {
  if (!entry.noteOpen && !correctionNoteDirty(entry)) {
    entry.noteBase = notes.reduce((latest, note) => note.id > latest.id ? note : latest, { id: -1, text: "" }).text
    entry.note = entry.noteBase
  }
  entry.noteOpen = true
  entry.listeners.forEach(notify => notify())
}
export function resetCorrectionNote(entry: DraftEntry) {
  entry.note = ""; entry.noteBase = ""; entry.noteOpen = false; entry.noteRequest = null
  entry.listeners.forEach(notify => notify())
}
export function hasUnsavedCorrectionDrafts() {
  return [...drafts.values()].some(entry => entry.editor?.dirty || correctionNoteDirty(entry))
}
export function correctionDraftsBusy() {
  return [...drafts.values()].some(entry => entry.editor?.busy || entry.noteBusy)
}
export function scopeCorrectionDrafts(userId: number | null) {
  if (account === userId) return
  for (const entry of drafts.values()) { entry.active = false; entry.editor?.cancelScheduled() }
  drafts.clear()
  account = userId
}
export function releaseCleanDraft(runId: number, itemId: number) {
  const key = `${runId}:${itemId}`
  const entry = drafts.get(key)
  // StrictMode replays mount effects. Only release after an actual unmount,
  // when no subscriber reattached and no in-flight note can still update it.
  queueMicrotask(() => {
    if (entry && drafts.get(key) === entry && !entry.listeners.size && !entry.editor?.dirty && !correctionNoteDirty(entry) && !entry.noteBusy) drafts.delete(key)
  })
}
// Keep the guard alive while another page is open. Draft values are confined
// to this signed-in tab; no credentials or correction evidence go into storage.
if (typeof window !== "undefined") window.addEventListener("beforeunload", event => {
  if (hasUnsavedCorrectionDrafts()) { event.preventDefault(); event.returnValue = "" }
})

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-level-display.ts =====
import type { BaselineLevel, BaselineRegressionItem } from "@/lib/types"
import type { NodeCorrectionHistoryItem } from "@/lib/node-correction"

const BASELINE_LEVELS = new Set<BaselineLevel>(["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"])

function isBaselineLevel(value: string | null | undefined): value is BaselineLevel {
  return typeof value === "string" && BASELINE_LEVELS.has(value as BaselineLevel)
}

export type CorrectionLevelDisplay = {
  level: BaselineLevel | null
  source: "human_correction" | "frozen_expected"
}

function isHumanNodeCorrection(event: NodeCorrectionHistoryItem): boolean {
  const metadata = event as typeof event & {
    corrector_confidence?: unknown
    corrector_policy?: unknown
  }
  return metadata.corrector_confidence == null && !metadata.corrector_policy
}

export function humanNodeCorrectionCount(
  item: Pick<BaselineRegressionItem, "evaluation">,
): number {
  return (item.evaluation?.correction_history ?? []).filter(isHumanNodeCorrection).length
}

function latestHumanCorrectedLevel(
  item: Pick<BaselineRegressionItem, "evaluation">,
): BaselineLevel | null {
  const history = item.evaluation?.correction_history ?? []
  for (let index = history.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
    const event = history[index]
    if (!event || !isHumanNodeCorrection(event)) continue
    const finalLevelNode = (
      event.node_type === "final_level"
      || (
        event.module_type_key === "score_level_matcher"
        && event.module_field_key === "level"
      )
    )
    if (finalLevelNode && isBaselineLevel(event.new_value as string | null | undefined)) {
      return event.new_value as BaselineLevel
    }
  }
  return null
}

/**
 * The frozen expected level remains the regression truth. This helper only
 * chooses which level the correction-analysis UI should show as its human
 * reference after a completed manual correction.
 */
export function correctionLevelDisplay(
  item: Pick<BaselineRegressionItem, "expected_level" | "evaluation">,
): CorrectionLevelDisplay {
  const evaluation = item.evaluation
  const humanReview = evaluation?.human_review
  const nativeHumanLevel = latestHumanCorrectedLevel(item)
  if (nativeHumanLevel) {
    return { level: nativeHumanLevel, source: "human_correction" }
  }
  const humanLevel = humanReview?.corrected_level
  if (
    isBaselineLevel(humanLevel)
    && evaluation?.review_truth_status === "completed"
    && humanReview?.decision === "corrected"
  ) {
    return { level: humanLevel, source: "human_correction" }
  }
  return { level: item.expected_level, source: "frozen_expected" }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-navigation.ts =====
export type CorrectionNavigationItem = {
  id: number
  status: "queued" | "completed" | "failed"
  evaluation: {
    review_stage: string | null
  } | null
}

/** Keep only rows that have a finished model result and can be opened in correction. */
export function correctionQueueItems<T extends CorrectionNavigationItem>(
  items: readonly T[],
): T[] {
  return items.filter((item) => item.status === "completed" && item.evaluation)
}

/** Return the next item after current that still needs an initial decision. */
export function nextPendingCorrectionId(
  items: readonly CorrectionNavigationItem[],
  currentId: number,
): number | null {
  const currentIndex = items.findIndex((item) => item.id === currentId)
  const candidates = currentIndex >= 0 ? items.slice(currentIndex + 1) : items
  return candidates.find((item) => (
    item.status === "completed"
    && item.evaluation
    && item.evaluation.review_stage !== "completed"
  ))?.id ?? null
}

/** Return the immediately preceding item in the current correction list. */
export function previousCorrectionId(
  items: readonly CorrectionNavigationItem[],
  currentId: number,
): number | null {
  const currentIndex = items.findIndex((item) => item.id === currentId)
  if (currentIndex <= 0) return null
  return items[currentIndex - 1]?.id ?? null
}

/**
 * Save a dirty node draft before approving the item, and navigate only after
 * both writes succeed. Injected callbacks keep failure ordering testable.
 */
export async function runStructuredCorrectionApproval<Saved>({
  hasDirtyDraft,
  saveDraft,
  submitReview,
  navigateNext,
}: {
  hasDirtyDraft: boolean
  saveDraft: () => Promise<Saved>
  submitReview: (saved: Saved | null) => Promise<void>
  navigateNext?: () => void
}): Promise<void> {
  const saved = hasDirtyDraft ? await saveDraft() : null
  await submitReview(saved)
  navigateNext?.()
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-pagination.tsx =====
import { useEffect, useId, useState } from "react"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { pageRange } from "@/lib/paging"

export function CorrectionPagination({ page, pageSize, total, loading, onPageChange, onPageSizeChange }: {
  page: number; pageSize: number; total: number; loading: boolean
  onPageChange: (page: number) => void
  onPageSizeChange: (size: number) => void
}) {
  const id = useId()
  const pageCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize))
  const [jump, setJump] = useState(String(page + 1))
  useEffect(() => setJump(String(page + 1)), [page])
  const range = pageRange({ total, offset: page * pageSize, limit: pageSize })
  const pages = [...new Set([0, page - 1, page, page + 1, pageCount - 1])]
    .filter(value => value >= 0 && value < pageCount).sort((a, b) => a - b)
  return <nav aria-label="纠偏分页" className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-y border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-3 py-3">
    <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]" aria-live="polite">
      {loading ? "正在加载素材…" : `第 ${range.from}–${range.to} 张 / 共 ${total} 张`} · 第 {page + 1}/{pageCount} 页
    </p>
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">每页
        <select aria-label="每页素材数" value={pageSize} disabled={loading}
          className="visual-native-select" onChange={event => onPageSizeChange(Number(event.target.value))}>
          {[10, 25, 50].map(size => <option key={size} value={size}>{size} 张</option>)}
        </select>
      </label>
      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={loading || page <= 0} onClick={() => onPageChange(page - 1)}>上一页</Button>
      {pages.map((value, index) => <span key={value} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
        {index > 0 && value > pages[index - 1] + 1 && <span aria-hidden="true" className="text-[var(--muted)]">…</span>}
