        <Button size="sm" variant={value === page ? "primary" : "secondary"} aria-label={`第 ${value + 1} 页`}
          aria-current={value === page ? "page" : undefined} disabled={loading || value === page}
          onClick={() => onPageChange(value)}>{value + 1}</Button>
      </span>)}
      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={loading || page + 1 >= pageCount} onClick={() => onPageChange(page + 1)}>下一页</Button>
      <form className="flex items-center gap-2" onSubmit={event => {
        event.preventDefault()
        const target = Number(jump)
        if (!loading && Number.isInteger(target) && target >= 1 && target <= pageCount) onPageChange(target - 1)
      }}>
        <label htmlFor={id} className="text-xs">跳至</label>
        <Input id={id} aria-label="跳至页码" type="number" min={1} max={pageCount} step={1} required
          value={jump} onChange={event => setJump(event.target.value)} disabled={loading} className="w-20"/>
        <Button type="submit" size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={loading || total === 0}>跳转</Button>
      </form>
    </div>
  </nav>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-presentation.ts =====
import { scopeFromIsSingle } from "./kg-route-codes.ts"

export type CorrectionDetailMode = "aesthetic_level" | "structured_fields"

export type CorrectionWorkspaceLayout = {
  panelOrder: Array<"queue" | "context" | "correction">
  queueWeight: number
  contextWeight: number
  correctionWeight: number
  gridTemplateColumns: string
}

export type CorrectionQueueState = {
  key: "completed" | "different" | "pending" | "ready"
  label: "已完成" | "有差异" | "待填写" | "待确认"
  tone: "success" | "danger" | "neutral" | "warning"
}

export type BaselineWorkspaceLayout = {
  gridClassName: string
  showBaselineSetSidebar: boolean
}

export function baselineWorkspaceLayout(
  correctionItemId: number,
): BaselineWorkspaceLayout {
  if (correctionItemId > 0) {
    return {
      gridClassName: "mx-auto grid max-w-[1920px] grid-cols-1",
      showBaselineSetSidebar: false,
    }
  }
  return {
    gridClassName: "mx-auto grid max-w-[1720px] lg:grid-cols-[310px_minmax(0,1fr)]",
    showBaselineSetSidebar: true,
  }
}

export type KnowledgeGraphTruthRoute = {
  source: "国内" | "海外"
  scope: "单体" | "整体"
  label: string
}

export function correctionDetailMode(
  truthKind: "level" | "structured_fields",
): CorrectionDetailMode {
  return truthKind === "structured_fields" ? "structured_fields" : "aesthetic_level"
}

export function correctionWorkspaceLayout(
  mode: CorrectionDetailMode,
): CorrectionWorkspaceLayout {
  if (mode === "structured_fields") {
    return {
      panelOrder: ["queue", "context", "correction"],
      queueWeight: 0.45,
      contextWeight: 0.95,
      correctionWeight: 1.3,
      gridTemplateColumns: "minmax(200px, 0.45fr) minmax(280px, 0.95fr) minmax(360px, 1.3fr)",
    }
  }
  return {
    panelOrder: ["context", "correction"],
    queueWeight: 0,
    contextWeight: 0.85,
    correctionWeight: 1.15,
    gridTemplateColumns: "minmax(320px, 0.85fr) minmax(0, 1.15fr)",
  }
}

export function correctionQueueItemState({
  reviewStage,
  deviationCount,
  pendingTruthCount,
}: {
  reviewStage: string | null | undefined
  deviationCount: number
  pendingTruthCount: number
}): CorrectionQueueState {
  if (reviewStage === "completed") {
    return { key: "completed", label: "已完成", tone: "success" }
  }
  if (deviationCount > 0) {
    return { key: "different", label: "有差异", tone: "danger" }
  }
  if (pendingTruthCount > 0) {
    return { key: "pending", label: "待填写", tone: "neutral" }
  }
  return { key: "ready", label: "待确认", tone: "warning" }
}

export function structuredCorrectionPrimaryAction(
  hasNext: boolean,
  pending: boolean,
): string {
  if (pending) return "正在保存"
  return hasNext ? "保存纠偏并查看下一条" : "保存本条纠偏"
}

export function knowledgeGraphTruthRoute(
  workflowInput: Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined,
): KnowledgeGraphTruthRoute | null {
  if (!workflowInput) return null
  const source = workflowInput.source === "domestic"
    ? "国内"
    : workflowInput.source === "overseas"
      ? "海外"
      : null
  const scopeValue = scopeFromIsSingle(workflowInput.source as string, workflowInput.is_single)
  const scope = scopeValue === "single" ? "单体" : scopeValue === "whole" ? "整体" : null
  if (!source || !scope) return null
  return { source, scope, label: `${source} · ${scope}` }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-stage-meta.tsx =====
import type { BaselineCorrectionRun, BaselineRegressionItem } from "@/lib/types"

export const correctionStages: Array<{
  key: BaselineCorrectionRun["stage"]
  label: string
}> = [
  { key: "analysis", label: "自动分析纠偏样本" },
  { key: "candidate_generation", label: "生成统一机制候选" },
  { key: "candidate_validation", label: "校验并冻结候选" },
  { key: "regression", label: "执行候选回归" },
  { key: "decision", label: "等待人工决策" },
]

export type CorrectionStageState = "pending" | "active" | "completed" | "failed"

export function correctionStageState(
  run: BaselineCorrectionRun,
  stage: BaselineCorrectionRun["stage"],
): CorrectionStageState {
  const currentIndex = correctionStages.findIndex((item) => item.key === run.stage)
  const stageIndex = correctionStages.findIndex((item) => item.key === stage)
  if (run.status === "approved" || run.status === "rejected") return "completed"
  if (stageIndex < currentIndex) return "completed"
  if (stageIndex > currentIndex) return "pending"
  if (run.status === "failed") return "failed"
  return "active"
}

export function correctionStageClassName(state: CorrectionStageState) {
  if (state === "completed") return "border-alias-control-002 bg-primary text-active-text"
  if (state === "active") return "border-alias-control-002 bg-active-surface text-active-text"
  if (state === "failed") return "border-danger bg-alias-surface-063 text-alias-content-041"
  return "border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white text-[var(--muted)]"
}

export function correctionStageStateName(state: CorrectionStageState) {
  if (state === "completed") return "已完成"
  if (state === "active") return "进行中"
  if (state === "failed") return "执行失败"
  return "等待自动执行"
}

export function correctionStageLabel(stage: BaselineCorrectionRun["stage"]) {
  return correctionStages.find((item) => item.key === stage)?.label ?? "自动纠偏"
}

export function correctionStageRunningMessage(stage: BaselineCorrectionRun["stage"]) {
  if (stage === "analysis") return "AI 正在分析纠偏样本与偏差方向"
  if (stage === "candidate_generation") return "AI 正在生成统一机制候选"
  if (stage === "candidate_validation") return "系统正在校验并冻结候选版本"
  if (stage === "regression") return "系统正在执行候选回归"
  return "系统正在整理最终决策证据"
}

export function correctionStatusName(run: BaselineCorrectionRun) {
  if (run.status === "processing") return correctionStageLabel(run.stage)
  if (run.status === "awaiting_decision") return "等待人工决策"
  if (run.status === "approved") return "已启用候选"
  if (run.status === "rejected") return "已拒绝候选"
  return "执行失败"
}

export function correctionStatusTone(run: BaselineCorrectionRun): "active" | "success" | "danger" {
  if (run.status === "processing") return "active"
  if (run.status === "awaiting_decision" || run.status === "approved") return "success"
  return "danger"
}

export function reviewStatus(evaluation: BaselineRegressionItem["evaluation"]) {
  if (!evaluation) return null
  if (evaluation.review_panel && evaluation.review_panel.status !== "completed") {
    return {
      label: `审核中 ${evaluation.review_panel.submitted_count}/${evaluation.review_panel.required_reviewers}`,
      tone: "active" as const,
    }
  }
  const decision = evaluation.human_review?.decision
  if (decision === "approved") return { label: "已确认", tone: "success" as const }
  if (decision === "corrected") {
    return {
      label: `已纠偏 · 人工 ${evaluation.final_level ?? "维度"}`,
      tone: "warning" as const,
    }
  }
  if (decision === "rejected") return { label: "已退回", tone: "danger" as const }
  if (evaluation.review_panel) {
    return {
      label: `审核中 ${evaluation.review_panel.submitted_count}/${evaluation.review_panel.required_reviewers}`,
      tone: "active" as const,
    }
  }
  return { label: "待审核", tone: "neutral" as const }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-table.tsx =====
import { createIdempotencyToken } from "@/lib/idempotency-token"
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQueries, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { baselineRegressionApi } from "@/lib/api"
import type { BaselineRegressionItem } from "@/lib/types"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { TablePreferences, orderedColumns } from "@/components/table-preferences"
import { useRememberedPage } from "@/lib/preferences"
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea"
import { ImagePreviewButton, type ImagePreview } from "@/components/image-lightbox"
import { renderCorrectionNode } from "@/features/correction-contract/contract-renderer.tsx"
import { correctionNodeEditable } from "@/features/correction-contract/contract-renderer"
import { correctionValueFromNode } from "@/features/correction-contract/correction-view-state"
import type { CorrectionContractNode, CorrectionView } from "@/features/correction-contract/types"
import { CorrectionAutosave } from "./correction-autosave"
import { refreshCorrectionItem } from "./correction-cache"
import { correctionDraftEntry, correctionNoteDirty, openCorrectionNote, resetCorrectionNote, releaseCleanDraft } from "./correction-draft-store"

const fieldKey = (node: CorrectionContractNode) => node.metadata?.field_identity?.field_path ?? node.node_key

export function CorrectionTable({ runId, items, disabled, onPreview, onDirtyChange }: {
  runId: number; items: BaselineRegressionItem[]; disabled: boolean
  onPreview: (preview: ImagePreview) => void; onDirtyChange: (dirty: boolean) => void
}) {
  const remembered = useRememberedPage("baseline-regression")
  const [field, setField] = useState("")
  const [dirtyRows, setDirtyRows] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set())
  const headerScroll = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const bodyScroll = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const client = useQueryClient()
  const completedIds = items.filter(item => item.status === "completed").map(item => item.id)
  const batch = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["baseline-correction-page", runId, "table", completedIds],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const response = await baselineRegressionApi.getCorrectionViews(runId, completedIds, "table")
      response.items.forEach(view => {
        const key = ["baseline-correction-view", runId, view.item_id, "table"]
        const current = client.getQueryData<CorrectionView>(key)
        if (!current || current.review_revision <= view.review_revision) {
          view.notes = [...new Map([...(view.notes ?? []), ...(current?.notes ?? [])].map(note => [note.id, note])).values()].sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id)
          client.setQueryData(key, view)
        }
      })
      return response
    },
    enabled: completedIds.length > 0, staleTime: 60_000,
  })
  const views = useQueries({ queries: items.map(item => ({
    queryKey: ["baseline-correction-view", runId, item.id, "table"],
    queryFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.getCorrectionView(runId, item.id, "table"),
    enabled: false, staleTime: 60_000,
  })) })
  const columns = useMemo(() => {
    const fields = new Map<string, string>()
    views.forEach(query => query.data?.nodes.filter(correctionNodeEditable).forEach(node => fields.set(fieldKey(node), node.label)))
    return [...fields].map(([key, label]) => ({ key, label }))
  }, [views])
  const catalog = [{ key: "material", label: "素材", width: 176, fixed: true },
    ...columns.map((column) => ({ ...column, key: `field:${column.key}`, width: 260 })),
    { key: "notes", label: "保存状态与人工备注", width: 288, fixed: true }]
  const ordered = orderedColumns(catalog, remembered.table)
  const visible = ordered.filter(column => column.key.startsWith("field:") && column.visible && (!field || column.key === `field:${field}`))
    .map(column => ({ ...column, key: column.key.slice(6) }))
  const widths = [ordered.find(column => column.key === "material")?.width ?? 176, ...visible.map(column => column.width), ordered.find(column => column.key === "notes")?.width ?? 288]
  const tableStyle = { minWidth: widths.reduce((sum, width) => sum + width, 0) }
  const colgroup = <colgroup>{widths.map((width, index) => <col key={index} style={{ width }} />)}</colgroup>
  useEffect(() => { onDirtyChange(dirtyRows.size > 0) }, [dirtyRows, onDirtyChange])
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!dirtyRows.size) return
    const warn = (event: BeforeUnloadEvent) => { event.preventDefault(); event.returnValue = "" }
    window.addEventListener("beforeunload", warn)
    return () => window.removeEventListener("beforeunload", warn)
  }, [dirtyRows])
  return <section className={`mt-5 ${remembered.table.density === "compact" ? "[&_td]:py-2 [&_th]:py-2" : ""}`} aria-label="素材字段纠偏表格">
    <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
      <h3 className="text-xl font-bold">表格纠偏</h3>
      <label className="text-sm">查看字段 <select aria-label="查看字段" value={field} onChange={event => setField(event.target.value)} className="visual-native-select ml-2">
        <option value="">全部字段</option>{columns.map(column => <option key={column.key} value={column.key}>{column.label}</option>)}
      </select></label>
      <TablePreferences columns={catalog} value={remembered.table} onChange={remembered.setTable} disabled={!remembered.ready} />
      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">字段停止输入后自动保存，离开单元格或按 Enter 也会立即保存；无需逐条确认。人工备注可基于上次内容反复补充或修改，单独提交；图片可点击放大。切换页面或轮次会保留未保存草稿，返回该素材可继续。</p>
    </div>
    <div className="sticky top-28 z-10 rounded-t-md border border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-029 lg:top-12" data-testid="correction-table-sticky-header">
      <div ref={headerScroll} className="overflow-x-auto rounded-t-md" onScroll={event => {
        if (bodyScroll.current) bodyScroll.current.scrollLeft = event.currentTarget.scrollLeft
      }}>
        <table className="w-full table-fixed border-collapse text-sm" style={tableStyle}>{colgroup}
          <thead><tr><th className="sticky left-0 z-10 bg-alias-surface-029 p-3 text-left">素材</th>
            {visible.map(column => <th key={column.key} className="p-3 text-left">{column.label}</th>)}
            <th className="p-3 text-left">保存状态与人工备注</th></tr></thead>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div ref={bodyScroll} className="overflow-x-auto rounded-b-md border-x border-b border-[var(--line)]" onScroll={event => {
      if (headerScroll.current) headerScroll.current.scrollLeft = event.currentTarget.scrollLeft
    }}>
      <table className="w-full table-fixed border-collapse text-sm" style={tableStyle} aria-label="素材字段纠偏内容">{colgroup}
        <thead className="sr-only"><tr><th>素材</th>{visible.map(column => <th key={column.key}>{column.label}</th>)}<th>保存状态与人工备注</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>{items.map((item, index) => <CorrectionRow key={`${runId}:${item.id}`} runId={runId} item={item}
          view={views[index].data} error={batch.error?.message ?? views[index].error?.message} columns={visible} disabled={disabled}
          onPreview={onPreview} onDirtyChange={dirty => setDirtyRows(previous => {
            if (previous.has(item.id) === dirty) return previous
            const next = new Set(previous); if (dirty) next.add(item.id); else next.delete(item.id); return next
          })} />)}</tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  </section>
}

function CorrectionRow({ runId, item, view: latestView, error, columns, disabled, onPreview, onDirtyChange }: {
  runId: number; item: BaselineRegressionItem; view?: CorrectionView; error?: string
  columns: {key: string; label: string}[]; disabled: boolean
  onPreview: (preview: ImagePreview) => void; onDirtyChange: (dirty: boolean) => void
}) {
  const client = useQueryClient()
  const [, render] = useState(0)
  const entry = useMemo(() => correctionDraftEntry(runId, item.id), [runId, item.id])
  const composingFields = useRef(new Set<string>())
  const editor = { get current() { return entry.editor }, set current(value: CorrectionAutosave | null) { entry.editor = value } }
  const note = entry.note
  const setNote = (value: string) => { entry.note = value; entry.listeners.forEach(notify => notify()) }
  const noteOpen = entry.noteOpen
  const noteRequest = { get current() { return entry.noteRequest }, set current(value: typeof entry.noteRequest) { entry.noteRequest = value } }
  useEffect(() => {
    const notify = () => render(version => version + 1)
    entry.listeners.add(notify)
    return () => { entry.listeners.delete(notify); releaseCleanDraft(runId, item.id) }
  }, [entry, runId, item.id])
  const view = editor.current?.dirty ? editor.current.view : latestView ?? editor.current?.view
  const edit = (nodeKey: string, value: unknown) => {
    if (!view || disabled) return
    if (!editor.current || !editor.current.dirty) editor.current = new CorrectionAutosave(latestView ?? view, {
      notify: () => entry.listeners.forEach(notify => notify()),
      send: payload => entry.active ? baselineRegressionApi.submitCorrectionNodes(runId, item.id, payload, "table") : Promise.reject(new Error("登录账号已变化，请重新打开纠偏页面")),
      accept: response => {
        if (!entry.active) return
        const current = client.getQueryData<CorrectionView>(["baseline-correction-view", runId, item.id, "table"])
        response.notes = [...new Map([...(response.notes ?? []), ...(current?.notes ?? [])].map(note => [note.id, note])).values()].sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id)
        client.setQueryData(["baseline-correction-view", runId, item.id, "table"], response)
        void refreshCorrectionItem(client, runId, item.id, response.review_revision,
          () => baselineRegressionApi.getRunItem(runId, item.id, "list"))
          .catch(() => toast.error("字段已保存，结果摘要刷新失败；可稍后刷新页面"))
      },
      confirmRerun: () => window.confirm("此字段修改需要重跑后续模型节点并消耗调用额度，是否继续？"),
    })
    editor.current.edit(nodeKey, value)
    editor.current.composition(nodeKey, composingFields.current.has(nodeKey))
  }
  const pending = Boolean(editor.current?.busy)
  const dirty = Boolean(editor.current?.dirty || correctionNoteDirty(entry))
  useEffect(() => { onDirtyChange(dirty) }, [dirty, onDirtyChange])
  const saveNote = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => {
      if (disabled) return Promise.reject(new Error("当前角色或类目仅可查看"))
      if (!entry.active) return Promise.reject(new Error("登录账号已变化，请重新打开纠偏页面"))
      if (noteRequest.current?.text !== note.trim()) noteRequest.current = {
        text: note.trim(), idempotency_key: `note:${runId}:${item.id}:${createIdempotencyToken()}`,
      }
      entry.noteBusy = true
      entry.listeners.forEach(notify => notify())
      return baselineRegressionApi.addCorrectionNote(runId, item.id, noteRequest.current!).finally(() => {
        entry.noteBusy = false; entry.listeners.forEach(notify => notify())
      })
    },
    onSuccess: response => {
      if (!entry.active) return
      client.setQueryData(["baseline-correction-view", runId, item.id, "table"], (current: CorrectionView | undefined) => current ? { ...current, notes: response.notes } : current)
      if (editor.current) editor.current.view = { ...editor.current.view, notes: response.notes }
      resetCorrectionNote(entry)
      toast.success("人工备注已保存")
    },
    onError: error => toast.error(error.message),
  })
  const locked = disabled || view?.read_only
  return <tr className="border-t border-[var(--line)] align-top">
    <td className="sticky left-0 z-[1] bg-white p-3"><ImagePreviewButton src={item.image_url} alt={item.asset.name} imageClassName="h-24 w-32 object-contain bg-white" onPreview={onPreview}/>
      <p className="mt-2 break-all text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{item.asset.name}</p><p className="text-xs">素材 #<span className="visual-code">{item.asset.asset_id}</span></p>
      {item.status !== "completed" && <p className="text-xs text-red-700">未完成评测，请先处理失败</p>}</td>
    {columns.map(column => {
      const node = view?.nodes.find(node => fieldKey(node) === column.key && correctionNodeEditable(node))
      const savedValue = node ? correctionValueFromNode(node) : undefined
      const draft = node ? editor.current?.draft[node.node_key] : undefined
      return <td key={column.key} className="p-3" onCompositionStart={() => { if (node) { composingFields.current.add(node.node_key); editor.current?.composition(node.node_key, true) } }} onCompositionEnd={() => { if (node) { composingFields.current.delete(node.node_key); editor.current?.composition(node.node_key, false) } }} onBlur={event => {
        if (node && !event.currentTarget.contains(event.relatedTarget as Node | null)) editor.current?.commit(node.node_key)
      }} onKeyDown={event => {
        if (event.key === "Enter" && !event.nativeEvent.isComposing && event.target instanceof HTMLInputElement) event.target.blur()
      }}>{node ? <>
        <p className="mb-2 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{view?.route_field_confirmation ? node.human_value !== undefined ? "新路线人工真值" : "新路线待确认" : node.human_value !== undefined ? "人工纠偏" : "当前结果"}：{savedValue === null ? "已清空" : JSON.stringify(savedValue ?? "")}</p>
        {view?.route_field_confirmation && <p className="mb-2 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">旧路线模型：{JSON.stringify(node.model_value ?? "")}</p>}
        {renderCorrectionNode({...node, node_key: `${item.id}.${node.node_key}`}, draft ? draft.value : savedValue,
          value => edit(node.node_key, value), Boolean(locked))}
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]" aria-live="polite">{editor.current?.queue.some(task => task.key === node.node_key)
          ? editor.current.error ? "保存失败，修改仍保留" : "保存中…" : editor.current?.draft[node.node_key]?.dirty ? "等待自动保存…" : editor.current?.saved.has(node.node_key) ? "已保存" : ""}</p>
      </> : <span className="text-[var(--muted)]">—</span>}</td>
    })}
    <td className="p-3">{error ? <p className="text-red-700">{error}</p> : !view && item.status === "completed" ? <p>加载字段中…</p> : <>
      <p role="status" className="mb-2 text-xs">{pending ? "正在保存字段…" : editor.current?.error ? "字段保存失败" : editor.current?.dirty ? "有待保存字段" : editor.current?.saved.size ? view?.route_field_confirmation ? "已保存为新路线人工真值" : "字段纠偏已保存，可用于分析" : !view ? "素材完成评测后可纠偏" : locked ? "当前结果只读，不能修改字段或备注" : !view.nodes.some(correctionNodeEditable) ? "当前结果没有可编辑字段，可补充人工备注" : "编辑字段即可纠偏"}</p>
      {view?.route_field_confirmation && <p className="mb-3 text-xs text-amber-800">整体/单体已修改，可继续纠偏其他字段。人工真值供新路线使用；本轮模型结果仍是旧路线，请重新回归后分析模型表现。</p>}
      {editor.current?.error && <div className="mb-3 text-xs text-red-700"><p>{editor.current.error}</p>
        <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={async () => {
          if (editor.current?.failedStatus === 409) {
            if (!window.confirm("审核版本已变化。保留你的字段修改，读取最新结果后重新提交？")) return
            try { editor.current.refresh(await baselineRegressionApi.getCorrectionView(runId, item.id, "table")) } catch (error) { toast.error((error as Error).message) }
          } else editor.current?.retry()
        }}>{editor.current.failedStatus === 409 ? "刷新后重试" : "重试保存"}</Button>
      </div>}
      {dirty && <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" disabled={pending || entry.noteBusy} onClick={async () => {
        if (!window.confirm("放弃此素材未保存的字段与备注？已经保存的纠偏不会撤销。")) return
        entry.editor?.cancelScheduled(); entry.editor = null; resetCorrectionNote(entry)
        render(version => version + 1)
        try { client.setQueryData(["baseline-correction-view", runId, item.id, "table"], await baselineRegressionApi.getCorrectionView(runId, item.id, "table")) }
        catch (error) { toast.error((error as Error).message) }
      }}>放弃未保存修改</Button>}
      {(view?.notes ?? []).slice(-1).map(note => <p key={note.id} className="mb-2 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-xs">{note.text}<span className="ml-2 text-[var(--muted)]">{note.actor}</span></p>)}
      {(view?.notes?.length ?? 0) > 1 && <details className="visual-native-details mb-2 text-xs"><summary className="">历史备注（{view!.notes!.length - 1}）</summary>{view!.notes!.slice(0, -1).map(note => <p key={note.id} className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">{note.text}<span className="ml-2 text-[var(--muted)]">{note.actor}</span></p>)}</details>}
      {noteOpen ? <>
        <Textarea rows={3} aria-label={`素材 ${item.asset.asset_id} 人工备注`} value={note} maxLength={2000} onChange={event => setNote(event.target.value)} placeholder="在上次备注基础上补充或修改（选填）" disabled={Boolean(locked) || entry.noteBusy}/>
        <Button className="mt-2" size="sm" disabled={Boolean(locked) || entry.noteBusy || !note.trim() || !correctionNoteDirty(entry)} onClick={() => saveNote.mutate()}>{entry.noteBusy ? "保存备注中…" : "提交人工备注"}</Button>
      </> : <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" disabled={Boolean(locked) || !view} onClick={() => openCorrectionNote(entry, view?.notes ?? [])}>补充人工备注</Button>}
    </>}</td>
  </tr>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-workbench.tsx =====
import { scopeFromIsSingle, scopeLabel } from "./kg-route-codes"
import { type CSSProperties, type ReactNode } from "react"
import { ArrowLeft, CaretLeft, CaretRight } from "@phosphor-icons/react"

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { ImagePreviewButton } from "@/components/image-lightbox"
import { NodeCorrectionEditor } from "@/pages/node-correction-editor"
import type { BaselineRegressionItem, Evaluation } from "@/lib/types"
import { CorrectionContractRenderer } from "@/features/correction-contract/contract-renderer.tsx"
import { NativeEvaluationDetail } from "@/features/correction-contract/native-evaluation-detail"
import { nativeEvaluationPresentation } from "@/features/correction-contract/native-evaluation-presentation"
import type { CorrectionDraft, CorrectionView } from "@/features/correction-contract/types"
import { RealModelEvidence } from "@/features/baseline-regression/real-model-evidence"
import {
  TruthRevisionPanel,
  type KnowledgeGraphRouteRevision,
  type TruthFieldConfirmation,
} from "@/features/baseline-regression/truth-revision-panel"

  correctionQueueItemState,
  correctionWorkspaceLayout,
