import type { BaselineRuleDiagnostics } from "@/lib/types"
import { SecondaryDrawer } from "@/components/workspace-page"
import type { ReactNode } from "react"

export function RuleDiagnosticsDrawer({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  children,
}: {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  children: ReactNode
}) {
  return (
    <SecondaryDrawer
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
      title="扣分规则命中诊断"
      description="查看本轮回归中每条规则实际命中多少次；规则不命中时，调整扣分值与红线阈值不会改变结果。"
    >
      {children}
    </SecondaryDrawer>
  )
}

function Stat({ label, value, hint }: { label: string; value: ReactNode; hint?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="border border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-2 rounded-md">
      <div className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</div>
      <div className="font-data mt-1 text-lg font-semibold">{value}</div>
      {hint ? <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{hint}</div> : null}
    </div>
  )
}

export function RuleDiagnosticsEvidence({
  data,
  loading,
  error,
}: {
  data?: BaselineRuleDiagnostics
  loading: boolean
  error: Error | null
}) {
  if (loading) {
    return <div className="h-52 animate-pulse bg-alias-surface-042" />
  }
  if (error) {
    return (
      <div className="border border-alias-edge-036 bg-alias-surface-067 px-4 py-4 text-sm text-alias-content-041 rounded-md">
        规则命中诊断加载失败：{error.message}
      </div>
    )
  }
  if (!data) {
    return <div className="px-4 py-6 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">暂无诊断数据。</div>
  }

  const hitRules = data.rules.filter((rule) => rule.hit_count > 0)
  const deadRules = data.rules.filter((rule) => rule.hit_count === 0)
  const noHitShare = data.scored_item_count
    ? Math.round((data.items_without_rule_hits / data.scored_item_count) * 100)
    : 0

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4" data-testid="baseline-rule-diagnostics">
      {/* 规则层几乎不参与打分时，先把结论说清楚：继续调阈值不会有效果。 */}
      {noHitShare >= 50 ? (
        <div
          className="border border-alias-edge-039 bg-alias-surface-062 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-alias-content-030 rounded-md"
          data-testid="baseline-rule-layer-warning"
        >
          <div className="font-semibold">
            {data.rule_layer_inert
              ? "规则层完全未参与打分"
              : `${noHitShare}% 的样本没有命中任何规则`}
          </div>
          <div className="mt-1">
            这些样本的分数就是调用 B 给出的原始分，未经规则层增减
            {data.unpenalised_level
              ? `，落在 ${data.unpenalised_level} 档`
              : ""}
            。此时提高扣分值或下调红线阈值都不会改变判级，应先检查调用 B 是否报出了缺陷。
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 md:grid-cols-4">
        <Stat label="声明规则数" value={data.declared_rule_count} />
        <Stat
          label="从未命中"
          value={data.never_hit_rule_count}
          hint={data.declared_rule_count ? `占 ${Math.round((data.never_hit_rule_count / data.declared_rule_count) * 100)}%` : undefined}
        />
        <Stat
          label="零命中样本"
          value={`${data.items_without_rule_hits}/${data.scored_item_count}`}
          hint={`${noHitShare}%`}
        />
        <Stat label="触发分数上限" value={data.items_with_score_caps} hint="红线等策略生效次数" />
      </div>

      {Object.keys(data.redline_policy).length ? (
        <div className="border border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-3 text-sm rounded-md">
          <div className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">红线策略</div>
          <div className="font-data mt-1 break-all">
            {Object.entries(data.redline_policy)
              .map(([key, value]) => `${key}=${String(value)}`)
              .join("  ")}
            {`  规则数=${data.redline_rule_count}`}
          </div>
          {data.redline_rule_count > 0 && data.items_with_score_caps === 0 ? (
            <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-alias-content-030">
              红线已配置但本轮一次未触发。
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {data.undeclared_hits.length ? (
        <div className="border border-alias-edge-036 bg-alias-surface-067 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-alias-content-041 rounded-md">
          <div className="font-semibold">模型报出了合同未声明的规则</div>
          <div className="font-data mt-1 break-all">
            {data.undeclared_hits
              .map((hit) => `${hit.dimension_key}/${hit.rule_id} × ${hit.hit_count}`)
              .join("，")}
          </div>
          <div className="mt-1 text-xs">这些命中不会参与扣分，通常说明提示词与合同的规则 id 不一致。</div>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <section className="border border-[var(--line)] rounded-md">
        <header className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(180px,1fr)_minmax(120px,180px)_80px_70px] gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-042 px-4 py-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
          <span>规则</span>
          <span>维度</span>
          <span>分值</span>
          <span>命中</span>
        </header>
        {data.rules.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="px-4 py-4 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">该冻结合同未声明任何扣分或加分规则。</div>
        ) : (
          [...hitRules, ...deadRules].map((rule) => (
            <div
              key={`${rule.dimension_key}:${rule.rule_id}:${rule.kind}`}
              className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(180px,1fr)_minmax(120px,180px)_80px_70px] gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-2 text-sm last:border-0"
              data-testid={`baseline-rule-coverage-${rule.dimension_key}-${rule.rule_id}`}
            >
              <span className="font-data break-all">
                <span className="visual-code">{rule.rule_id}</span>
                <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                  {rule.kind === "bonus" ? "加分" : "扣分"}
                </span>
                {rule.description ? (
                  <span className="mt-0.5 block text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{rule.description}</span>
                ) : null}
              </span>
              <span className="break-all text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                {rule.dimension_label || rule.dimension_key}
              </span>
              <span className="font-data">{rule.points ?? "—"}</span>
              <span className={rule.hit_count ? "font-data font-semibold" : "font-data text-alias-content-041"}>
                {rule.hit_count}
              </span>
            </div>
          ))
        )}
      </section>

      {Object.keys(data.score_buckets).length ? (
        <div className="border border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-3 text-sm rounded-md">
          <div className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">分数分布</div>
          <div className="font-data mt-1 break-all">
            {Object.entries(data.score_buckets)
              .map(([bucket, count]) => `${bucket}: ${count}`)
              .join("　")}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/run-config-drawer.tsx =====
import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import { SecondaryDrawer } from "@/components/workspace-page"
import { formatMicrosAsCny, microsToYuanPer1k } from "@/features/model-registry/pricing-contract"
import { expectedOutputBasisLabel, expectedOutputSourceLabel } from "./real-model-execution"
import type { RealExecutionPreview } from "./real-model-execution"

export function RunConfigDrawer({ open, onOpenChange, children, footer }: { open: boolean; onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void; children: ReactNode; footer?: ReactNode }) {
  return <SecondaryDrawer open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange} size="wide" title="运行配置" description="选择一个完整评测机制与执行方式；启动后会冻结本轮所有节点和版本。" footer={footer}>{children}</SecondaryDrawer>
}

export function RunPricingEstimate({ preview }: { preview: RealExecutionPreview }) {
  const reserve = preview.retry_reserve
  const plannedWithRetries = preview.expected.cost_micros + (reserve?.cost_micros ?? 0)
  const tiers = Array.from(new Set((preview.node_pricing ?? []).flatMap((item) =>
    item.expected_tier_buckets?.map((bucket) => bucket.pricing_tier_key)
      ?? (item.expected_pricing_tier_key ? [item.expected_pricing_tier_key] : []),
  )))
  return (
    <section className="border border-alias-edge-049 bg-white px-3 py-3 rounded-md" aria-label="真实调用计价预估">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div><p className="text-xs font-bold text-alias-content-033">本轮未命中费用上界</p><p className="font-data mt-1 text-lg font-bold text-alias-content-033">{formatMicrosAsCny(preview.expected.cost_micros)}</p></div>
        <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">预计计价档位：{tiers.join("、") || "旧版平价"}</p>
      </div>
      {preview.inference_route && <p className="mt-2 text-sm font-semibold">本轮推理：{preview.inference_route.mode === "batch" ? "批量推理 · 等待时间较长，按批量价格估算" : "在线推理"}</p>}
      {preview.doubao_endpoint && (
        <p className={`mt-2 text-xs leading-5 ${preview.doubao_endpoint.blocker ? "border-l-2 border-danger pl-3 text-alias-content-041" : "text-[var(--muted)]"}`}>
          {preview.doubao_endpoint.blocker
            ? preview.doubao_endpoint.blocker
            : preview.doubao_endpoint.binding
              ? `豆包接入点：${preview.doubao_endpoint.binding.display_name}（${preview.doubao_endpoint.binding.endpoint_id}）· ${preview.doubao_endpoint.binding.verification_status === "verified" ? "已验收" : "未验收"}，费用按该接入点归因`
              : preview.doubao_endpoint.required
                ? "该机制使用豆包 2.0，但尚未绑定推理接入点；当前按模型 ID 直连，费用无法按渠道归因"
                : "该机制不使用豆包 2.0 接入点路由"}
        </p>
      )}
      <dl className="mt-3 grid gap-2 text-xs sm:grid-cols-2">
        <CostFact label="调用次数" value={`${preview.expected.requests.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} 次`} />
        <CostFact label="输入 token" value={preview.expected.input_tokens.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} />
        <CostFact label="输出 token" value={preview.expected.output_tokens.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} />
        <CostFact label="输出 token 上限" value={(preview.expected.output_tokens + (reserve?.output_tokens ?? 0)).toLocaleString("zh-CN")} />
        <CostFact label="图片 token" value={preview.expected.image_tokens == null ? "结算后拆分（已含在输入 token）" : preview.expected.image_tokens.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} />
        <CostFact label="重试预留" value={reserve ? `${reserve.requests.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} 次 · ${formatMicrosAsCny(reserve.cost_micros)}` : "未单独预留"} />
        <CostFact label="含重试未命中上界" value={formatMicrosAsCny(plannedWithRetries)} />
        <CostFact label="服务端费用上限" value={formatMicrosAsCny(preview.caps.max_cost_micros)} />
      </dl>
      {(preview.node_pricing ?? []).length > 0 && (
        <div className="mt-3 divide-y divide-[var(--line)] border-y border-[var(--line)]">
          {preview.node_pricing?.map((node) => (
            <NodePricingFact key={node.node_instance_id} node={node} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-4 text-[var(--muted)]">输出 token {expectedOutputSourceLabel(preview.expected_output_source)}。输入 token 已包含图片 token。缓存命中无法在启动前可靠预估；最终费用以供应商返回的 cached_tokens 为准。</p>
    </section>
  )
}

function NodePricingFact({ node }: { node: NonNullable<RealExecutionPreview["node_pricing"]>[number] }) {
  const buckets = node.expected_tier_buckets ?? []
  const retryReserve = node.retry_reserve_request_count
  const expectedTierKeys = new Set(
    buckets.length > 0
      ? buckets.map((bucket) => bucket.pricing_tier_key)
      : [node.expected_pricing_tier_key ?? node.pricing_tier_key],
  )
  const expectedRates = (node.pricing_tiers ?? []).filter((tier) =>
    expectedTierKeys.has(tier.tier_key),
  )
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-1 py-2 text-xs sm:grid-cols-[minmax(10rem,1fr)_minmax(18rem,2fr)]">
      <span className="font-semibold"><span className="visual-code">{node.node_instance_id}</span> · <span className="visual-code">{node.model_id}</span></span>
      <div className="space-y-1 text-[var(--muted)] sm:text-right">
        <p>
          预计 {(node.expected_request_count ?? 0).toLocaleString("zh-CN")} 次 · 输入 {(node.expected_input_tokens ?? 0).toLocaleString("zh-CN")} · 输出 {(node.expected_output_tokens ?? 0).toLocaleString("zh-CN")} · 未命中上界 {node.expected_cost_micros == null ? "待按输入结算" : formatMicrosAsCny(node.expected_cost_micros)}
        </p>
        {expectedRates.map((tier) => (
          <p key={tier.tier_key}>
            {tier.tier_key}：普通输入价 {formatRate(tier.input_micros_per_1k)} · 缓存命中价 {tier.cached_input_micros_per_1k == null ? "合同未提供" : formatRate(tier.cached_input_micros_per_1k)} · 输出价 {formatRate(tier.output_micros_per_1k)}
          </p>
        ))}
        <p>
          逐档：{buckets.length > 0
            ? buckets.map((bucket) => `${bucket.pricing_tier_key} × ${bucket.request_count}（${formatMicrosAsCny(bucket.cost_micros)}）`).join("；")
            : (node.expected_pricing_tier_key ?? "旧版平价")}
        </p>
        <p>{expectedOutputBasisLabel(node.expected_output_basis)}</p>
        <p>
          重试预留：{retryReserve == null ? "旧工作流按全局预算" : `${retryReserve.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} 次`}；最大单次预留 {node.pricing_tier_key} · {formatMicrosAsCny(node.estimated_cost_micros_per_request)}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function formatRate(microsPer1k: number): string {
  return `${microsToYuanPer1k(microsPer1k)} 元 / 1k tokens`
}

function CostFact({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return <div className="flex justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] py-1"><dt className="text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</dt><dd className="font-data text-right font-semibold">{value}</dd></div>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/run-history-drawer.tsx =====
import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import { SecondaryDrawer } from "@/components/workspace-page"
import { formatMicrosAsCny } from "@/features/model-registry/pricing-contract"
import type { BaselineModelCost } from "@/lib/types"

export function RunHistoryDrawer({ open, onOpenChange, children }: { open: boolean; onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <SecondaryDrawer open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange} title="运行历史" description="查看本基准集的历史回归轮次与冻结版本。">{children}</SecondaryDrawer>
}

export function RunCostEvidence({ cost }: { cost?: BaselineModelCost | null }) {
  if (!cost) return null
  const variance = cost.variance_micros
  const actualCost = cost.actual_cost_known
    ? formatMicrosAsCny(cost.actual_cost_micros)
    : cost.unknown_invocation_count > 0
      ? `账单未知（${cost.unknown_invocation_count} 次调用）`
      : "结算中"
  const cache = cost.cache_observation
  const cachedRatio = cache?.cached_token_ratio == null
    ? "—"
    : `${(cache.cached_token_ratio * 100).toLocaleString("zh-CN", { maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}%`
  const cacheSavings = cache?.cache_adjusted_cost_exact
    ? formatMicrosAsCny(cache.cache_savings_micros ?? 0)
    : "保守结算（含未上报、无效或旧合同）"
  return (
    <dl className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-3 text-xs">
      <CostFact label="规划预估" value={formatMicrosAsCny(cost.estimated_cost_micros)} />
      <CostFact label="最大预留" value={formatMicrosAsCny(cost.reserved_cost_micros)} />
      <CostFact label="实际费用" value={actualCost} />
      <CostFact label="实际档位" value={cost.tiers.map((tier) => `${tier.tier_key}×${tier.count}`).join("、") || "—"} />
      <CostFact label="费用偏差" value={variance == null ? "—" : `${variance >= 0 ? "+" : "−"}${formatMicrosAsCny(Math.abs(variance))}`} />
      <CostFact label="缓存 token" value={cache ? `${cache.cached_input_tokens.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} · ${cachedRatio}` : "旧记录无缓存观测"} />
      <CostFact label="命中 / 未命中" value={cache ? `${cache.hit_count} / ${cache.miss_count}` : "—"} />
      <CostFact label="未上报 / 无效" value={cache ? `${cache.unreported_count} / ${cache.invalid_count}` : "—"} />
      <CostFact label="缓存节省" value={cache ? cacheSavings : "—"} />
      <CostFact label="结算精度" value={cache?.cache_adjusted_cost_exact ? "缓存调整后精确" : "保守结算"} />
    </dl>
  )
}

function CostFact({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return <div><dt className="text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</dt><dd className="font-data mt-1 font-semibold">{value}</dd></div>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/run-page-placeholder.ts =====
/** Keep the editor mounted while paging within a run, never across runs. */
export function sameRunPage<T extends { summary: { id: number } }>(
  selectedRunId: number,
  previous: T | undefined,
): T | undefined {
  return selectedRunId > 0 && previous?.summary.id === selectedRunId ? previous : undefined
}

import type { BaselineRegressionRun } from "../../lib/types"

export function workflowRuntimePending(run: BaselineRegressionRun | null | undefined) {
  return Boolean(run && !["failed", "partial_failed"].includes(run.status)
    && run.workflow_runtime.selected && run.workflow_runtime.pending > 0)
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/structured-baseline-truth.ts =====
import type {
  BaselineTruthContract,
  FilenameKnowledgeGraphTruthSuggestion,
} from "@/lib/types"

export type StructuredTruthDraft = {
  contractKey: string
  values: Record<string, string | number>
  workflowInput?: Record<string, unknown>
  source?: "filename" | "manual"
}

const ROUTE_FIELDS = new Set(["ll_id", "res_id", "is_single"])

export function createStructuredTruthDraft(
  contract?: BaselineTruthContract,
): StructuredTruthDraft {
  if (!contract) return { contractKey: "", values: {} }
  const values: Record<string, string | number> = {}
  for (const field of contract.fields) {
    values[field.key] = field.fixed_value ?? ""
  }
  return { contractKey: contract.contract_key, values }
}

export function switchStructuredTruthContract(
  current: StructuredTruthDraft,
  contract: BaselineTruthContract,
): StructuredTruthDraft {
  const next = createStructuredTruthDraft(contract)
  for (const field of contract.fields) {
    if (ROUTE_FIELDS.has(field.key) || field.fixed_value !== null) continue
    const previous = current.values[field.key]
    if (typeof previous === "string") next.values[field.key] = previous
  }
  return next
}

export function structuredTruthContract(
  draft: StructuredTruthDraft | undefined,
  contracts: readonly BaselineTruthContract[],
) {
  return contracts.find((item) => item.contract_key === draft?.contractKey)
}

export function structuredTruthReady(
  draft: StructuredTruthDraft | undefined,
  contracts: readonly BaselineTruthContract[],
) {
  const contract = structuredTruthContract(draft, contracts)
  if (!draft || !contract) return false
  const identity = draft.values[contract.identity_field]
  if (typeof identity !== "string" || !identity.trim()) return false
  if (contract.contract_kind === "design_case") {
    if (!contract.fields.every((field) => (
      !field.required
      || field.fixed_value !== null
      || String(draft.values[field.key] ?? "").trim().length > 0
    ))) return false
    const workflowInput = structuredTruthWorkflowInput(draft)
    const caseInput = workflowInput?.case
    if (!caseInput || typeof caseInput !== "object" || Array.isArray(caseInput)) return false
    const frozenCase = caseInput as Record<string, unknown>
    if (frozenCase.cloud_id !== identity) return false
    for (const field of ["project_id", "case_name", "case_text"] as const) {
      if (typeof frozenCase[field] !== "string" || !frozenCase[field].trim()) return false
    }
    for (const field of ["quality_score", "time_score", "filter_threshold"] as const) {
      if (typeof frozenCase[field] !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(frozenCase[field])) return false
    }
    const images = frozenCase.images
    if (!Array.isArray(images) || images.length < 1) return false
    if (images.some((image) => {
      if (!image || typeof image !== "object" || Array.isArray(image)) return true
      const frozen = image as Record<string, unknown>
      return typeof frozen.cloud_id !== "string" || !frozen.cloud_id
        || typeof frozen.oss_url !== "string" || !frozen.oss_url
        || typeof frozen.asset_id !== "number" || !Number.isInteger(frozen.asset_id)
        || typeof frozen.asset_sha256 !== "string"
        || !/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(frozen.asset_sha256)
    })) return false
  }
  return contract.fields.every((field) => (
    field.fixed_value === null
    || draft.values[field.key] === field.fixed_value
  ))
}

export function structuredTruthExpectedResult(
  draft: StructuredTruthDraft,
  contract: BaselineTruthContract,
) {
  const result: Record<string, unknown> = {}
  for (const field of contract.fields) {
    const raw = field.fixed_value ?? draft.values[field.key] ?? ""
    // 案例类合同的非必填字段留空就不提交：空串会被合同当成「填了一个空值」，
    // 素材维度更严重——空的文字描述会把已有统计判成冲突。
    if (
      (contract.contract_kind === "design_case"
        || contract.contract_kind === "design_case_aggregate")
      && !field.required
      && String(raw).trim() === ""
    ) continue
    let value: unknown = raw
    if (field.type === "array") {
      value = String(raw).split(",").map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean)
    } else if (field.type === "number" || field.type === "integer") {
      const parsed = Number(raw)
      value = Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : raw
    }
    const path = field.key.split(".")
    let cursor = result
    for (const segment of path.slice(0, -1)) {
      const child = cursor[segment]
      if (!child || typeof child !== "object" || Array.isArray(child)) {
        cursor[segment] = {}
      }
      cursor = cursor[segment] as Record<string, unknown>
    }
    cursor[path[path.length - 1]] = value
  }
  return result
}

type DesignCaseAsset = {
  id: number
  name: string
  sha256?: string
  source_uri?: string | null
  image_url: string
}

export function syncDesignCaseWorkflowInput(
  draft: StructuredTruthDraft,
  assets: readonly DesignCaseAsset[],
): StructuredTruthDraft {
  const previousCase = (
    draft.workflowInput?.case
    && typeof draft.workflowInput.case === "object"
    && !Array.isArray(draft.workflowInput.case)
  ) ? draft.workflowInput.case as Record<string, unknown> : {}
  const previousImages = Array.isArray(previousCase.images)
    ? previousCase.images.filter((item): item is Record<string, unknown> => (
        Boolean(item) && typeof item === "object" && !Array.isArray(item)
      ))
    : []
  const previousByAsset = new Map(previousImages.map((item) => [item.asset_id, item]))
  const images = assets.map((asset) => {
    const previous = previousByAsset.get(asset.id)
    return {
      cloud_id: typeof previous?.cloud_id === "string" && previous.cloud_id
        ? previous.cloud_id
        : `asset-${asset.id}`,
      oss_url: typeof previous?.oss_url === "string" && previous.oss_url
        ? previous.oss_url
        : asset.source_uri || asset.image_url,
      asset_id: asset.id,
      asset_sha256: asset.sha256 ?? "",
    }
  })
  return {
    ...draft,
    workflowInput: {
      case: {
        cloud_id: previousCase.cloud_id ?? "",
        project_id: previousCase.project_id ?? "",
        case_name: previousCase.case_name ?? "",
        case_text: previousCase.case_text ?? "",
        quality_score: previousCase.quality_score ?? "",
        time_score: previousCase.time_score ?? "",
        filter_threshold: previousCase.filter_threshold ?? "",
        images,
      },
    },
  }
}

export function structuredTruthWorkflowInput(
  draft: StructuredTruthDraft,
): Record<string, unknown> | undefined {
  const rawCase = draft.workflowInput?.case
  if (!rawCase || typeof rawCase !== "object" || Array.isArray(rawCase)) return undefined
  const caseInput = { ...(rawCase as Record<string, unknown>) }
  for (const field of ["quality_score", "time_score", "filter_threshold"] as const) {
    const parsed = Number(caseInput[field])
    if (!Number.isFinite(parsed) || String(caseInput[field]).trim() === "") return undefined
    caseInput[field] = parsed
  }
  return { case: caseInput }
}

export function applyFilenameTruthSuggestion(
  current: StructuredTruthDraft | undefined,
  suggestion: FilenameKnowledgeGraphTruthSuggestion | null | undefined,
  contracts: readonly BaselineTruthContract[],
): StructuredTruthDraft | undefined {
  if (current || suggestion?.status !== "matched") return current
  const contract = contracts.find((item) => (
    item.contract_key === suggestion.contract_key
    && item.identity_field === suggestion.identity_field
    && item.is_single === suggestion.is_single
  ))
  // 赛道能从文件名判定就先把合同选上，运营不必再手选；素材 ID 没带出来时
  // 留空等人工补，比整条都要手选好得多。
  if (!contract || !suggestion.identity_field) {
    return current
  }
  const draft = createStructuredTruthDraft(contract)
  if (suggestion.identity_value) {
    draft.values[suggestion.identity_field] = suggestion.identity_value
  }
  draft.source = "filename"
  return draft
}

export function prefillStructuredTruthDrafts(
  current: Record<number, StructuredTruthDraft>,
  assets: ReadonlyArray<{
    id: number
    knowledge_graph_truth_suggestion?: FilenameKnowledgeGraphTruthSuggestion | null
  }>,
  contracts: readonly BaselineTruthContract[],
): Record<number, StructuredTruthDraft> {
  let next = current
  for (const asset of assets) {
    const draft = applyFilenameTruthSuggestion(
      current[asset.id],
      asset.knowledge_graph_truth_suggestion,
      contracts,
    )
    if (!draft || draft === current[asset.id]) continue
    if (next === current) next = { ...current }
    next[asset.id] = draft
  }
  return next
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/structured-truth-drawer.tsx =====
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { SecondaryDrawer } from "@/components/workspace-page"
import {
  switchStructuredTruthContract,
  type StructuredTruthDraft,
} from "@/features/baseline-regression/structured-baseline-truth"
import type { BaselineTruthContract } from "@/lib/types"
import type { Asset } from "@/lib/types"

export function StructuredTruthDrawer({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  assetName,
  draft,
  contracts,
  selectedAssets,
  onChange,
}: {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  assetName: string
  draft: StructuredTruthDraft
  contracts: readonly BaselineTruthContract[]
  selectedAssets: readonly Asset[]
  onChange: (draft: StructuredTruthDraft) => void
}) {
  const contract = contracts.find((item) => item.contract_key === draft.contractKey)
  const designCase = contract?.contract_kind === "design_case"
  const materialAggregate = contract?.contract_kind === "design_case_aggregate"
  // 案例类真值的路线由基准集冻结时决定，改不了：换路线等于换了一个案例。
  const routeLocked = designCase || materialAggregate
  const caseInput = (
    draft.workflowInput?.case
    && typeof draft.workflowInput.case === "object"
    && !Array.isArray(draft.workflowInput.case)
  ) ? draft.workflowInput.case as Record<string, unknown> : {}

  function updateCaseField(key: string, value: string) {
    onChange({
      ...draft,
      values: key === "cloud_id"
        ? { ...draft.values, case_cloud_id: value }
        : draft.values,
      workflowInput: {
        case: { ...caseInput, [key]: value },
      },
    })
  }

  function updateCaseImage(index: number, key: "cloud_id" | "oss_url", value: string) {
    const images = Array.isArray(caseInput.images)
      ? caseInput.images.map((item) => ({ ...(item as Record<string, unknown>) }))
      : []
    images[index] = { ...images[index], [key]: value }
    onChange({
      ...draft,
      workflowInput: { case: { ...caseInput, images } },
    })
  }

  return (
    <SecondaryDrawer
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
      size="wide"
      title="填写字段真值"
      description={materialAggregate
        ? `案例：${assetName || "当前案例"}。按样本表原话填三行统计，保存后作为素材维度回归的真值。`
        : designCase
        ? `案例代表素材：${assetName || "当前素材"}。填写案例输入，并为全部已选图片冻结身份。`
        : `素材：${assetName || "当前素材"}。先选它属于哪条路线，再填写人工确认的字段；看不出的字段留空。`}
      footer={(
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            {materialAggregate
              ? "三行统计与反解出的计数一起追加为新一版真值，下一轮回归生效。"
              : designCase
              ? "案例、全部图片身份与终态字段真值会一起冻结，创建后不可覆盖。"
              : "路线会自动固定国内/海外、整体/单体和素材标识字段，创建后随基准集冻结。"}
          </p>
          <Button disabled={!contract} onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>完成</Button>
        </div>
      )}
    >
      <div className="space-y-6">
        <section className="border-y border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-051 p-4">
          <label>
            <span className="mb-2 block text-sm font-bold">字段路线</span>
            <select
              className="visual-native-select w-full font-semibold disabled:bg-alias-surface-018 disabled:text-[var(--muted)]"
              value={draft.contractKey}
              disabled={routeLocked}
              onChange={(event) => {
                const next = contracts.find((item) => item.contract_key === event.target.value)
                if (next) onChange(switchStructuredTruthContract(draft, next))
              }}
            >
              <option value="">请选择字段合同</option>
              {contracts.map((item) => (
                <option key={item.contract_key} value={item.contract_key}>{item.display_name}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </label>
          {routeLocked && (
            <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
              案例路线在基准集冻结时已确定，不能在这里更换。
            </p>
          )}
          {contract && (
            <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {contract.site_label && <Badge tone="active">{contract.site_label}</Badge>}
              {contract.asset_label && <Badge>{contract.asset_label}</Badge>}
              <Badge>{contract.identity_label}</Badge>
            </div>
          )}
        </section>

        {designCase && (
          <section className="space-y-4 border-y border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-051 p-4">
            <div>
              <h3 className="visual-card-title">案例工作流输入</h3>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                这些是运行输入，不是黄金答案；质量分、时效分和过滤阈值只从这里读取。
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">
              {[
                ["cloud_id", "案例 Cloud ID"],
                ["project_id", "项目 ID"],
                ["case_name", "案例名称"],
                ["case_text", "案例说明"],
                ["quality_score", "quality_score"],
                ["time_score", "time_score"],
                ["filter_threshold", "filter_threshold"],
              ].map(([key, label]) => (
                <label key={key} className={key === "case_text" ? "md:col-span-2" : ""}>
                  <span className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold">{label}</span>
                  <Input
                    aria-label={label}
                    value={String(caseInput[key] ?? "")}
                    placeholder={`填写${label}`}
                    onChange={(event) => updateCaseField(key, event.target.value)}
                  />
                </label>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <h4 className="text-sm font-bold">全部已选图片</h4>
                <Badge tone="active">{selectedAssets.length} 张</Badge>
              </div>
              <div className="mt-3 space-y-3">
                {(Array.isArray(caseInput.images) ? caseInput.images : []).map((raw, index) => {
                  const image = raw as Record<string, unknown>
                  return (
                    <div key={String(image.asset_id ?? index)} className="grid gap-2 border border-[var(--line)] bg-white p-3 md:grid-cols-2 rounded-md">
                      <p className="md:col-span-2 text-xs font-semibold">
                        图片 #<span className="visual-code">{String(image.asset_id ?? "-")}</span> · {selectedAssets[index]?.name ?? "已选素材"}
                      </p>
                      <Input
                        aria-label={`图片${index + 1} Cloud ID`}
                        value={String(image.cloud_id ?? "")}
                        placeholder="图片 Cloud ID"
                        onChange={(event) => updateCaseImage(index, "cloud_id", event.target.value)}
                      />
                      <Input
                        aria-label={`图片${index + 1}来源地址`}
                        value={String(image.oss_url ?? "")}
                        placeholder="OSS URL 或平台素材地址"
                        onChange={(event) => updateCaseImage(index, "oss_url", event.target.value)}
                      />
                      <p className="md:col-span-2 break-all font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                        SHA256 {String(image.asset_sha256 ?? "未冻结")}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  )
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        {!contract ? (
          <p className="border-y border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-10 text-center text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">
            选择字段路线后，系统会展示该路线需要人工确认的字段。
          </p>
        ) : (
          <section>
            <div className="mb-4">
              <h3 className="visual-card-title">人工确认字段</h3>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                {materialAggregate
                  ? "按素材维度样本表的原话填，例如「82分有3张,88分有4张」；系统会按导入时同一套规则反解成统计，各项合计不得超过图片总张数。留空表示这项不作为真值。"
                  : "素材 ID 必填；其他字段无法判断时可留空。多值请使用英文逗号连接，不加空格。"}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
              {contract.fields.map((field) => {
                const fixed = field.fixed_value !== null
                return (
                  <label
                    key={field.key}
                    className={`border rounded-md p-4 ${field.key === contract.identity_field ? "border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-044" : "border-[var(--line)] bg-white"}`}
                  >
                    <span className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-sm font-bold">
                      {field.label}
                      {field.key === contract.identity_field && <Badge tone="active">必填</Badge>}
                      {fixed && <Badge>路线固定</Badge>}
                    </span>
                    <span className="mb-3 mt-1 block text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{field.description}</span>
                    {field.key === "level" ? (
                      <select
                        aria-label={field.label}
                        className="visual-native-select w-full"
                        value={String(draft.values[field.key] ?? "")}
                        onChange={(event) => onChange({
                          ...draft,
                          values: { ...draft.values, [field.key]: event.target.value },
                        })}
                      >
                        <option value="">请选择等级</option>
                        {["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"].map((level) => <option key={level} value={level}>{level}</option>)}
                      </select>
                    ) : (
                      <Input
                        aria-label={field.label}
                        disabled={fixed || (designCase && field.key === contract.identity_field)}
                        value={String(draft.values[field.key] ?? "")}
                        placeholder={field.type === "array" ? "多值用英文逗号分隔" : field.key === contract.identity_field ? `填写${field.label}` : "看不出可留空"}
                        onChange={(event) => onChange({
                          ...draft,
                          values: { ...draft.values, [field.key]: event.target.value },
                        })}
                      />
                    )}
                  </label>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}
      </div>
    </SecondaryDrawer>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/structured-truth.ts =====
export function structuredValuesMatch(left: unknown, right: unknown) {
  return canonicalStructuredValue(left) === canonicalStructuredValue(right)
}

const FIELD_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  ll_id: "国内素材 ID",
  res_id: "海外素材 ID",
  is_single: "是否单体",
  title: "标题",
  space: "空间",
  object: "物体",
  style: "风格",
  material: "材质",
  structural_features: "结构特征",
  architectural_element: "建筑元素",
  soft_decoration: "软装",
  hard_decoration: "硬装",
  color: "颜色",
  form: "形态",
  structure: "结构关系",
  view_angle: "视角",
  // 素材（案例）维度聚合摘要，与运营样本表的三行说法一致
  image_total: "图片总张图",
  score_distribution: "所有表现图的分数",
  feature_counts: "所有图片的类型分布",
  scored_images: "已打分张数",
}

export function structuredFieldLabel(fieldPath: string) {
  const fieldKey = fieldPath.split(".").at(-1) ?? fieldPath
  return FIELD_LABELS[fieldKey] ?? fieldPath
}

export function structuredTruthRunSummary(item: {
  status: string
  field_deviations?: string[]
}) {
  if (item.status !== "completed") {
    return item.status === "failed"
      ? "本轮工作流执行失败，请查看失败原因"
      : "等待工作流完成后再核对字段"
  }
  const deviations = item.field_deviations ?? []
  if (!deviations.length) return "已有真值的字段核对一致；未标注字段尚未评估"
  return `需要纠偏的字段：${deviations.map(structuredFieldLabel).join("、")}`
}

function canonicalStructuredValue(value: unknown): string {
  if (value === undefined) return "undefined"
  return JSON.stringify(normalizeStructuredValue(value))
}

function normalizeStructuredValue(value: unknown): unknown {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(normalizeStructuredValue)
  if (value && typeof value === "object") {
    return Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)
        .sort(([left], [right]) => left.localeCompare(right))
        .map(([key, child]) => [key, normalizeStructuredValue(child)]),
    )
  }
  return value
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/truth-edit.ts =====
/**
 * 基准集人工真值的直接编辑（不经回归纠偏）。
 *
 * 条目本体不可改；这里每次保存都是追加一版 BaselineTruthRevision，下一轮回归生效。
 * 纯函数集中在这里，方便契约脚本验证数据形状。
 */
import type { BaselineSetItem, BaselineTruthContract } from "@/lib/types"

import {
  createStructuredTruthDraft,
  structuredTruthExpectedResult,
  type StructuredTruthDraft,
} from "./structured-baseline-truth.ts"

export type BaselineTruthEditRequest = {
  edit_key: string
  reason: string
  expected_level?: "L1" | "L2" | "L3" | "L4" | "L5"
  expected_result?: Record<string, unknown>
  truth_contract_key?: string
}

/** 把条目当前生效的结构化真值还原成可编辑草稿（选中它现在的合同，填入已有字段值）。 */
export function truthDraftFromItem(
  item: Pick<BaselineSetItem, "expected_result" | "truth_contract_key">,
  contracts: readonly BaselineTruthContract[],
): StructuredTruthDraft {
  const contract = contracts.find((c) => c.contract_key === item.truth_contract_key)
  const draft = createStructuredTruthDraft(contract)
  const current = item.expected_result ?? {}
  for (const key of Object.keys(draft.values)) {
    // 案例合同的字段键是带点的路径（case_review.score），真值里是嵌套对象。
    // 只按顶层键取会读不到，保存时这些字段被当成空值丢掉，等于静默改坏真值。
    const value = resolveTruthPath(current, key)
    if (typeof value === "string" || typeof value === "number") draft.values[key] = value
    else if (Array.isArray(value)) draft.values[key] = value.join(",")
  }
  draft.source = "manual"
  return draft
}

function resolveTruthPath(source: Record<string, unknown>, path: string): unknown {
  let cursor: unknown = source
  for (const segment of path.split(".")) {
    if (!cursor || typeof cursor !== "object" || Array.isArray(cursor)) return undefined
    cursor = (cursor as Record<string, unknown>)[segment]
  }
  return cursor
}

/** 结构化真值提交载荷：按合同展开全部字段（缺失填空串、固定值原样），路线跟随草稿所选合同。 */
export function structuredTruthEditPayload(
  draft: StructuredTruthDraft,
  contracts: readonly BaselineTruthContract[],
  reason: string,
  editKey: string,
): BaselineTruthEditRequest | null {
  const contract = contracts.find((c) => c.contract_key === draft.contractKey)
  if (!contract) return null
  return {
    edit_key: editKey,
    reason: reason.trim(),
    truth_contract_key: contract.contract_key,
    expected_result: structuredTruthExpectedResult(draft, contract),
  }
}

export const DIMENSION_GRADE_CONTRACT_KEY = "design_case.dimension_grade.v1"
export const IMAGE_GRADE_LEVELS = ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"] as const
export type ImageGradeLevel = (typeof IMAGE_GRADE_LEVELS)[number]

export type ImageGradeDraft = {
  /** 案例整体档位，五档——过滤档只在案例级存在。 */
  caseLevel: "L1" | "L2" | "L3" | "L4" | "L5" | ""
  /** 逐图人工档位，键是图片 cloud_id，顺序跟随冻结清单。 */
  images: { cloudId: string; level: ImageGradeLevel | ""; imageUrl?: string }[]
}

/**
 * 把图片维度真值还原成逐图草稿。
 *
 * 逐图档位是几十个平级取值，通用的「字段 → 文本」编辑器装不下，也不该把它
 * 压成逗号串——那样保存时顺序或漏项都无从校验。冻结清单的顺序为准，真值里
 * 没有的图片留空，保存时后端会拒绝，不会静默写进一份缺图的真值。
 */
export function imageGradeDraftFromItem(
  item: Pick<BaselineSetItem, "expected_result"> & { workflow_input?: Record<string, unknown> | null },
): ImageGradeDraft {
  const current = item.expected_result ?? {}
  const rawLevel = current.level
  const rawImages = Array.isArray(current.images) ? current.images : []
  const caseInput = item.workflow_input?.case as { images?: Record<string, unknown>[] } | undefined
  const frozen = Array.isArray(caseInput?.images) ? caseInput.images : []
  const truthById = new Map(rawImages.filter((image) => image && typeof image === "object").map((image) => [image.cloud_id, image.level]))
  const source = frozen.length ? frozen : rawImages
  return {
    caseLevel: typeof rawLevel === "string" && /^L[1-5]$/.test(rawLevel)
      ? rawLevel as ImageGradeDraft["caseLevel"]
      : "",
    images: source.flatMap((raw) => {
      if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object" || Array.isArray(raw)) return []
      const image = raw as Record<string, unknown>
      const cloudId = image.cloud_id
      if (typeof cloudId !== "string" || !cloudId) return []
      const level = truthById.get(cloudId)
      return [{
        cloudId,
        ...(typeof image.asset_id === "number" && image.asset_id > 0
          ? { imageUrl: `/api/assets/${image.asset_id}/file` }
          : {}),
        level: (IMAGE_GRADE_LEVELS as readonly string[]).includes(String(level))
          ? level as ImageGradeLevel
          : "",
      }]
    }),
  }
}

/** 图片维度真值提交载荷；案例档位或任一图片档位未选时返回 null。 */
export function imageGradeEditPayload(
  draft: ImageGradeDraft,
  reason: string,
  editKey: string,
): BaselineTruthEditRequest | null {
  if (!draft.caseLevel || !draft.images.length) return null
  if (draft.images.some((image) => !image.level)) return null
  return {
    edit_key: editKey,
    reason: reason.trim(),
    truth_contract_key: DIMENSION_GRADE_CONTRACT_KEY,
    expected_result: {
      level: draft.caseLevel,
      images: draft.images.map((image) => ({
        cloud_id: image.cloudId,
        level: image.level,
      })),
    },
  }
}

export function levelTruthEditPayload(
  level: "L1" | "L2" | "L3" | "L4" | "L5",
  reason: string,
  editKey: string,
): BaselineTruthEditRequest {
  return { edit_key: editKey, reason: reason.trim(), expected_level: level }
}

/** 条目行上的一句话状态：等级 / 已填字段数，和它现在是第几版人工真值。 */
export function truthRowSummary(item: BaselineSetItem): string {
  const revision = item.truth_revision ?? 0
  const suffix = revision > 0 ? ` · 人工修订 ${revision} 版` : " · 导入原值"
  if (item.truth_kind === "level") return `等级 ${item.expected_level ?? "—"}${suffix}`
  const known = item.known_field_count ?? 0
  const pending = item.pending_truth_field_count ?? 0
  return `已填 ${known} / ${known + pending} 个字段${suffix}`
}

export function newEditKey(itemId: number): string {
  const rand = typeof crypto !== "undefined" && "randomUUID" in crypto
    ? crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)
    : Math.random().toString(16).slice(2, 10)
  return `ui-${itemId}-${Date.now()}-${rand}`
}

/** 案例资产是文本容器，审图只能使用导入时冻结的图片身份。 */
export function truthPreviewUrl(item: BaselineSetItem): string | null {
  if (item.truth_contract_key === DIMENSION_GRADE_CONTRACT_KEY) {
    const caseInput = item.workflow_input?.case as { images?: unknown[] } | undefined
    if (!Array.isArray(caseInput?.images) || !caseInput.images.length) return null
    return imageGradeDraftFromItem(item).images.find((image) => image.imageUrl)?.imageUrl ?? null
  }
  return item.asset.mime_type.startsWith("image/") ? item.image_url : null
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/truth-import-button.tsx =====
import { useRef, useState } from "react"
import { UploadSimple } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { api } from "@/lib/api"
import type { TruthImportPreview, TruthImportResult } from "@/lib/types"

/**
 * 回填运营改过的导出表：只认真值列、留空不改、先预览后提交。
 *
 * 真值是平台里最敏感的数据，所以这里强制两步：先看清「改哪条、哪个字段、
 * 从什么到什么」，填了原因才允许提交。写进去的是追加式修订，不覆盖历史。
 */
export function BaselineTruthImportButton({
  runId,
  onApplied,
}: {
  runId: number
  onApplied: () => Promise<void> | void
}) {
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null)
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<TruthImportPreview | null>(null)
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("")

  function body(picked: File, withReason = false) {
    const form = new FormData()
    form.append("file", picked)
    if (withReason) form.append("reason", reason.trim())
    return form
  }

  const doPreview = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (picked: File) => api<TruthImportPreview>(
      `/api/baseline-regressions/${runId}/truth-import/preview`,
      { method: "POST", body: body(picked) },
    ),
    onSuccess: (data, picked) => {
      setFile(picked)
      setPreview(data)
      if (!data.summary.changed_items) {
        toast.info("表格里没有检测到真值改动")
      }
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => {
      setFile(null); setPreview(null); toast.error(error.message)
    },
  })

  const [result, setResult] = useState<TruthImportResult | null>(null)

  const apply = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (picked: File) => api<TruthImportResult>(
      `/api/baseline-regressions/${runId}/truth-import`,
      { method: "POST", body: body(picked, true) },
    ),
    onSuccess: async (data) => {
      setFile(null); setPreview(null); setReason("")
      setResult(data)
      await onApplied()
      // 失败的条目不能只报个数字：运营看不到原因就只能重试同一份表格。
      if (data.summary.failed_items) {
        toast.warning(
          `已写入 ${data.summary.applied_items} 条，${data.summary.failed_items} 条未写入`,
          { description: "下方列出了每条失败的原因；成功的那些已经落库，不受失败条目影响。" },
        )
      } else {
        toast.success(`已写入 ${data.summary.applied_items} 条真值修订`)
      }
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const busy = doPreview.isPending || apply.isPending
  const changed = preview?.summary.changed_items ?? 0

  return (
    <>
      <input
        ref={inputRef}
        type="file"
        accept=".xlsx"
        className="sr-only"
        onChange={(event) => {
          const picked = event.target.files?.[0]
          event.target.value = ""
          if (picked) doPreview.mutate(picked)
        }}
      />
      <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" disabled={busy} onClick={() => inputRef.current?.click()}>
        <UploadSimple />回填真值
      </Button>
      {preview && file && (
        <div className="col-span-full mt-2 w-full rounded-sm border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-045 p-4 text-sm">
          <p className="font-semibold">
            {file.name}：将修改 {changed} 条、共 {preview.summary.changed_fields} 个字段
            {preview.summary.unchanged_rows ? `；${preview.summary.unchanged_rows} 行无改动` : ""}
            {preview.summary.skipped_rows ? `；${preview.summary.skipped_rows} 行跳过` : ""}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
            只读「（真值）」列，模型列一律忽略；留空表示不修改，要清空请填 <code>-</code>
          </p>
          {preview.items.length > 0 && (
            <ul className="mt-3 max-h-56 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto text-xs">
              {preview.items.map((item) => (
                <li key={item.item_id}>
                  条目 <span className="visual-code">{item.item_id}</span>：
                  {item.changes.map((c) => (
                    <span key={c.field} className="ml-1">
                      {c.label} <span className="text-[var(--muted)]">{String(c.before ?? "") || "（空）"}</span>
                      {" → "}<strong>{String(c.after ?? "") || "（空）"}</strong>；
                    </span>
                  ))}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
          {preview.skipped.length > 0 && (
            <ul className="mt-2 max-h-32 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto text-xs text-[var(--danger)]">
              {preview.skipped.map((s) => (
                <li key={s.row_number}>第 {s.row_number} 行：{s.reason}</li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
          <label className="mt-3 block">
            <span className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold">修改原因（会记入真值修订历史）</span>
            <Input
              value={reason}
              maxLength={2000}
              placeholder="例如：核对海外素材英文标题后批量修正"
              onChange={(event) => setReason(event.target.value)}
            />
          </label>
          <div className="mt-3 flex gap-2">
            <Button
              disabled={busy || !changed || !reason.trim()}
              onClick={() => apply.mutate(file)}
            >
              {apply.isPending ? "正在写入" : `确认写入 ${changed} 条真值修订`}
            </Button>
            <Button variant="secondary" disabled={busy} onClick={() => { setFile(null); setPreview(null) }}>
              取消
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {result && (
        <div className="col-span-full mt-2 w-full rounded-sm border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white p-4 text-sm">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <p className="font-semibold">
              回填结果：已写入 {result.summary.applied_items} 条 · {result.summary.applied_fields} 个字段
              {result.summary.failed_items ? `；${result.summary.failed_items} 条未写入` : ""}
              {result.summary.unchanged_rows ? `；${result.summary.unchanged_rows} 行无改动` : ""}
              {result.summary.skipped_rows ? `；${result.summary.skipped_rows} 行跳过` : ""}
            </p>
            <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => setResult(null)}>关闭</Button>
          </div>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
            写入的是追加式真值修订，下一轮回归才生效；已跑完轮次的指标按当轮冻结的真值算，不会被改写。
          </p>
          {result.failed.length > 0 && (
            <>
              <p className="mt-3 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--danger)]">未写入的条目与原因</p>
              <ul className="mt-1 max-h-56 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto text-xs text-[var(--danger)]">
                {result.failed.map((item) => (
                  <li key={item.item_id}>条目 <span className="visual-code">{item.item_id}</span>：{item.reason}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </>
          )}
          {result.skipped.length > 0 && (
            <ul className="mt-2 max-h-32 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
              {result.skipped.map((s) => (
                <li key={s.row_number}>第 {s.row_number} 行跳过：{s.reason}</li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/truth-revision-panel.tsx =====
import { isSingleChangeHint, isSingleOptions, remapIsSingle, scopeFromIsSingle, scopeLabel } from "./kg-route-codes"
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react"
import { ArrowClockwise, CheckCircle, GitBranch, WarningCircle } from "@phosphor-icons/react"

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea"
import {
  correctionNodeEditable,
  correctionNodeInputValue,
  parseCorrectionNodeInput,
} from "@/features/correction-contract/contract-renderer"
import type {
  CorrectionNodeValue,
  CorrectionView,
} from "@/features/correction-contract/types"
import {
  knowledgeGraphRouteDraft,
  needsAttention,
  needsHumanReview,
  reconcileTruthFieldInputs,
  routeRevisionReady,
  sortTruthFieldRows,
  truthFieldRows,
  truthNodeForField,
  truthReviewProgress,
  truthViewIdentity,
  type KnowledgeGraphRouteDraft,
  type TruthFieldRow,
} from "@/features/baseline-regression/truth-revision-state"

export type TruthFieldConfirmation = {
  nodeKey: string
  fieldPath: string
  value: CorrectionNodeValue
  reason: string
}

/** 采纳模型输出时的默认理由：不让运营为「确认正确」重复打字，但仍留下可审计的来源。 */
export const ACCEPT_MODEL_REASON = "人工核对后采纳模型输出"

export type KnowledgeGraphRouteRevision = KnowledgeGraphRouteDraft

export function TruthRevisionPanel({
  view,
  onConfirmField,
  onReviseRoute,
  fieldPending = false,
  routePending = false,
  disabled = false,
  frozenTruthContractKey,
}: {
  view: CorrectionView
  onConfirmField?: (confirmation: TruthFieldConfirmation) => Promise<void> | void
  onReviseRoute?: (revision: KnowledgeGraphRouteRevision) => Promise<void> | void
  fieldPending?: boolean
  routePending?: boolean
  disabled?: boolean
  frozenTruthContractKey?: string | null
}) {
  const truth = view.effective_truth
  const [showAll, setShowAll] = useState(false)
  const [bulkPending, setBulkPending] = useState(false)
  const fieldRows = useMemo(() => sortTruthFieldRows(truthFieldRows(view)), [view])
  const progress = useMemo(() => truthReviewProgress(fieldRows), [fieldRows])
  // 用户已手动改过、尚未提交的字段：数据刷新不覆盖，一键采纳也跳过
  const [dirtyFields, setDirtyFields] = useState<ReadonlySet<string>>(() => new Set())
  const dirtyFieldsRef = useRef(dirtyFields)
  dirtyFieldsRef.current = dirtyFields
  // 一键采纳的范围：要人看、且模型给了建议、且与当前真值不同——一致的本就不用动
  const acceptable = useMemo(
    () => fieldRows.filter((r: TruthFieldRow) => (
      needsAttention(r) && r.suggestion !== undefined && r.nodeKey && !dirtyFields.has(r.fieldPath)
    )),
    [dirtyFields, fieldRows],
  )
  const visibleRows = useMemo(
    () => (showAll ? fieldRows : fieldRows.filter((r: TruthFieldRow) => needsAttention(r) || r.review?.emphasis === "critical")),
    [fieldRows, showAll],
  )
  const initialRoute = useMemo(
    () => knowledgeGraphRouteDraft(view),
    [view.category_key, view.truth_hash, view.truth_revision],
  )
  const [fieldValues, setFieldValues] = useState<Record<string, string>>({})
  const [fieldReasons, setFieldReasons] = useState<Record<string, string>>({})
  const [route, setRoute] = useState<KnowledgeGraphRouteDraft | null>(initialRoute)

  const identity = truthViewIdentity(view)
  const initialFieldValues = useMemo(() => Object.fromEntries(fieldRows.map((row) => {
    const node = truthNodeForField(view.nodes, row.fieldPath)
    return [
      row.fieldPath,
      node
        ? correctionNodeInputValue(node, view.route_field_confirmation ? row.truthValue ?? "" : row.suggestion ?? row.truthValue)
        : String(row.suggestion ?? row.truthValue ?? ""),
    ]
  })), [fieldRows, view.nodes, view.route_field_confirmation])
  const initializedIdentity = useRef<string | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    const identityChanged = initializedIdentity.current !== identity
    initializedIdentity.current = identity
    if (identityChanged) {
      setDirtyFields(new Set())
      setFieldReasons({})
    }
    setFieldValues((current) => reconcileTruthFieldInputs(
      current,
      initialFieldValues,
      identityChanged ? new Set() : dirtyFieldsRef.current,
      identityChanged,
    ))
  }, [identity, initialFieldValues])

  const markFieldSubmitted = (fieldPath: string) => {
    setDirtyFields((current) => {
      if (!current.has(fieldPath)) return current
      const next = new Set(current)
      next.delete(fieldPath)
      return next
    })
    setFieldReasons((current) => {
      if (!(fieldPath in current)) return current
      const { [fieldPath]: _submitted, ...rest } = current
      return rest
    })
  }

  useEffect(() => setRoute(initialRoute), [initialRoute])

  if (view.truth_status === undefined) return null
  if (view.truth_status !== "available" || !truth) {
    return (
      <section className="border-y border-alias-edge-053 bg-alias-surface-068 px-5 py-4" aria-label="人工真值不可用">
        <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-alias-content-033"><WarningCircle />当前历史记录没有可演进的人工真值</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-033">仍可查看本轮冻结结果，但不能把这次纠偏带入下一轮。</p>
      </section>
    )
  }

  const revision = Number(view.truth_revision ?? 0)
  const revisionLabel = revision > 0 ? `人工真值 v${revision}` : "导入真值 v0"
  const knownCount = view.known_truth_fields?.length ?? 0
  const pendingCount = view.pending_truth_fields?.length ?? 0
  const levelTruth = truth.truth_kind === "level"
  const interactionDisabled = disabled || view.read_only
  const routeChangedSinceRun = Boolean(
    truth.truth_kind === "structured_fields"
    && frozenTruthContractKey
    && frozenTruthContractKey !== truth.truth_contract_key,
  )

  return (
    <section className="border-y border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-048 px-5 py-5" aria-label="人工真值与下一轮">
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <p className="text-sm font-bold">人工真值与下一轮</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            人工只确认自己核对过的内容；未确认的模型输出不会自动变成标准答案。
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <Badge tone={revision > 0 ? "success" : "neutral"}>{revisionLabel}</Badge>
          {view.effective_next_run && <Badge tone="active"><ArrowClockwise />下一轮生效</Badge>}
        </div>
      </header>

      {levelTruth ? (
        <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-4 sm:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto] sm:items-center">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">当前最终等级真值</p>
            <p className="mt-1 font-data text-2xl font-bold">{truth.expected_level || "待人工确认"}</p>
          </div>
          <p className="max-w-md text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            只有“最终等级”纠偏会更新下一轮等级真值；提示词和规则内部判断仍作为分析与版本推荐证据。
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="mt-4 space-y-4 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-4">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold">字段真值覆盖</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                本条需人工看 {progress.toReview} 项（其中重点 {progress.critical} 项）·
                按抽检规则免检 {progress.exempt} 项 · 待你处理 {progress.attention} 项
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <p className="font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">已确认 {knownCount} · 待补充 {pendingCount}</p>
              <Button
                size="sm"
                variant="secondary"
                disabled={interactionDisabled || fieldPending || bulkPending || routeChangedSinceRun || !acceptable.length}
                onClick={async () => {
                  setBulkPending(true)
                  try {
                    for (const row of acceptable) {
                      const node = truthNodeForField(view.nodes, row.fieldPath)
                      if (!node || !row.nodeKey) continue
                      await onConfirmField?.({
                        nodeKey: row.nodeKey,
                        fieldPath: row.fieldPath,
                        value: parseCorrectionNodeInput(node, correctionNodeInputValue(node, row.suggestion ?? row.truthValue)),
                        reason: ACCEPT_MODEL_REASON,
                      })
                    }
                  } finally { setBulkPending(false) }
                }}
              >
                <CheckCircle />{bulkPending ? "正在采纳" : `一键采纳 ${acceptable.length} 项模型输出`}
              </Button>
              <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setShowAll((v) => !v)}>
                {showAll ? "只看待处理" : `显示全部 ${fieldRows.length} 项`}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
          {routeChangedSinceRun && (
            <p className="border-l-2 border-alias-edge-033 bg-alias-surface-068 px-3 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-025">
              {view.route_field_confirmation ? "整体/单体已修改，可以继续手动填写新路线的人工真值。本轮模型结果仍属于旧路线，不能一键采纳；请按新路线回归后分析模型表现。" : "素材路线已经更新。当前页面仍是旧路线的模型结果，请先按新路线重新回归，再确认字段真值。"}
            </p>
          )}
          <div className="truth-confirmation-fields grid gap-3 lg:grid-cols-2">
            {visibleRows.map((row) => {
              const node = truthNodeForField(view.nodes, row.fieldPath)
              const actionable = Boolean(
                node
                && onConfirmField
                && correctionNodeEditable(node)
                && (!routeChangedSinceRun || view.route_field_confirmation?.fields.includes(row.fieldPath)),
              )
              const reason = fieldReasons[row.fieldPath] ?? ""
              // 值没改＝采纳模型输出，理由自动记；改了值才必须写清依据
              const baseline = node ? correctionNodeInputValue(node, row.suggestion ?? row.truthValue) : ""
              const valueChanged = routeChangedSinceRun || (fieldValues[row.fieldPath] ?? "") !== baseline
              return (
                <article key={row.fieldPath} className="space-y-3 border border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-4 py-4 rounded-md">
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-sm font-semibold">
                        {row.label}
                        {row.review?.emphasis === "critical" && <Badge tone="danger" className="ml-2">重点</Badge>}
                        {row.review?.state === "sampled" && <Badge tone="active" className="ml-2">抽检</Badge>}
                        {row.review?.state === "exempt" && <Badge tone="neutral" className="ml-2">本条免检</Badge>}
                      </p>
                      <p className="mt-1 font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                        {row.fieldPath}
                        {row.review && row.review.sample_rate < 1
                          && ` · 抽检 ${Math.round(row.review.sample_rate * 100)}%`}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <Badge tone={row.status === "pending" ? "warning" : row.status === "different" ? "danger" : "success"}>
                      {row.status === "pending" ? "待补充人工真值" : row.status === "different" ? "模型建议不同" : "已有人工真值"}
                    </Badge>
                  </div>
                  <div className="grid gap-2 text-xs sm:grid-cols-2">
                    <ValueBlock label="当前人工真值" value={row.status === "pending" ? "待补充人工真值" : displayValue(row.truthValue)} />
                    <ValueBlock label="本轮模型建议" value={row.suggestion === undefined ? "本轮未提供" : displayValue(row.suggestion)} />
                  </div>
                  {actionable && node && (
                    <div className="space-y-3 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-3">
                      {row.fieldPath === "is_single" && isSingleChangeHint(route?.source, row.truthValue) && (
                        <p className="border-l-2 border-alias-edge-014 bg-alias-surface-027 px-3 py-2 text-xs leading-5">
                          {isSingleChangeHint(route?.source, row.truthValue)}
                        </p>
                      )}
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold">
                        确认后的字段值
                        <Input
                          className="mt-2"
                          value={fieldValues[row.fieldPath] ?? ""}
                          disabled={interactionDisabled || fieldPending}
                          onChange={(event) => {
                            const value = event.target.value
                            setFieldValues((current) => ({ ...current, [row.fieldPath]: value }))
                            setDirtyFields((current) => current.has(row.fieldPath) ? current : new Set(current).add(row.fieldPath))
                          }}
                        />
                      </label>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold">
                        人工判断依据{valueChanged ? "（必填）" : "（改了值才必填）"}
                        <Textarea
                          className="mt-2 min-h-20"
                          value={reason}
                          disabled={interactionDisabled || fieldPending}
                          placeholder={valueChanged ? "用人话说明你从画面中看到了什么" : ACCEPT_MODEL_REASON}
                          onChange={(event) => setFieldReasons((current) => ({ ...current, [row.fieldPath]: event.target.value }))}
                        />
                      </label>
                      <Button
                        size="sm"
                        disabled={
                          interactionDisabled || fieldPending
                          || (valueChanged && !reason.trim())
                          || !(fieldValues[row.fieldPath] ?? "").trim()
                        }
                        onClick={async () => {
                          try {
                            await onConfirmField?.({
                              nodeKey: row.nodeKey as string,
                              fieldPath: row.fieldPath,
                              value: parseCorrectionNodeInput(node, fieldValues[row.fieldPath] ?? ""),
                              reason: reason.trim() || ACCEPT_MODEL_REASON,
                            })
                            markFieldSubmitted(row.fieldPath)
                          } catch {
                            // 失败提示由上层 mutation 负责；保留已编辑内容便于重试
                          }
                        }}
                      >
                        <CheckCircle />{fieldPending ? "正在保存" : "确认该字段为真值"}
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </article>
              )
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {view.category_key === "knowledge_graph" && route && initialRoute && (
        <details open className="visual-native-details mt-5 border-t border-[var(--line-strong)] pt-4">
          <summary className="list-none font-bold">
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2"><GitBranch />修改素材路线与身份</span>
            <span className="ml-2 text-xs font-normal text-[var(--muted)]">高影响操作，展开后再次确认</span>
          </summary>
          <div className="mt-4 space-y-4 border border-alias-edge-053 bg-alias-surface-081 p-4 rounded-md">
            <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-027">
              修改国内/海外或整体/单体后，系统会在下一轮自动选择新的提示词分支和字段合同；已经完成的本轮结果不会被改写。
            </p>
            <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold">
                来源范围
                <select
                  className="visual-native-select mt-2 w-full"
                  value={route.source}
                  disabled={interactionDisabled || routePending}
                  onChange={(event) => {
                    const source = event.target.value as KnowledgeGraphRouteDraft["source"]
                    setRoute({
                      ...route,
                      source,
                      // 换站点只换编码，整体/单体保持不变（国内 0/1，海外 1/2）
                      isSingle: remapIsSingle(route.source, source, route.isSingle),
                      identityValue: source === route.source ? route.identityValue : "",
                      confirmed: false,
                    })
                  }}
                >
                  <option value="domestic">国内</option>
                  <option value="overseas">海外</option>
                </select>
              </label>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold">
                素材范围
                <select
                  className="visual-native-select mt-2 w-full"
                  value={route.isSingle}
                  disabled={interactionDisabled || routePending}
                  onChange={(event) => setRoute({ ...route, isSingle: Number(event.target.value), confirmed: false })}
                >
                  {isSingleOptions(route.source).map((option) => (
                    <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>{option.label}</option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </label>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold">
                {route.source === "domestic" ? "国内素材 ID" : "海外素材 ID"}
                <Input
                  className="mt-2"
                  value={route.identityValue}
                  disabled={interactionDisabled || routePending}
                  placeholder={route.source === "domestic" ? "填写 ll_id" : "填写 res_id"}
                  onChange={(event) => setRoute({ ...route, identityValue: event.target.value, confirmed: false })}
                />
              </label>
            </div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold">
              修改原因（必填）
              <Textarea
                className="mt-2 min-h-20 bg-white"
                value={route.reason}
                disabled={interactionDisabled || routePending}
                placeholder="例如：人工核对后确认该图是海外单体，不是国内整体"
                onChange={(event) => setRoute({ ...route, reason: event.target.value, confirmed: false })}
              />
            </label>
            <label className="visual-native-choice flex items-start gap-3 border-y border-alias-edge-033 bg-white px-3 py-3 text-xs font-semibold leading-5 text-alias-content-025">
              <input
                className="visual-native-checkbox mt-1 size-4 shrink-0"
                type="checkbox"
                checked={route.confirmed}
                disabled={interactionDisabled || routePending}
                onChange={(event) => setRoute({ ...route, confirmed: event.target.checked })}
              />
              <span>我已再次核对来源、整体/单体和素材 ID；确认保存为新的人工真值，并在下一轮生效。</span>
            </label>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                当前：{routeLabel(initialRoute)}{routeChanged(initialRoute, route) ? ` → 将改为 ${routeLabel(route)}` : " · 尚未修改"}
              </p>
              <Button
                variant="secondary"
                disabled={interactionDisabled || routePending || !routeRevisionReady(route) || !routeChanged(initialRoute, route)}
                onClick={() => onReviseRoute?.(route)}
              >
                <GitBranch />{routePending ? "正在保存" : "保存路线，下一轮生效"}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </details>
      )}
    </section>
  )
}

function ValueBlock({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return <div className="min-w-0 border-l-2 border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-051 px-3 py-2"><p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p><p className="mt-1 break-words text-sm">{value}</p></div>
}

function displayValue(value: unknown): string {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") return "未填写"
  if (typeof value === "string" || typeof value === "number" || typeof value === "boolean") return String(value)
  return JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)
}

function routeLabel(route: KnowledgeGraphRouteDraft): string {
  return `${route.source === "domestic" ? "国内" : "海外"} · ${scopeLabel(scopeFromIsSingle(route.source, route.isSingle)) || "整体"} · ${route.identityValue || "未填写 ID"}`
}

function routeChanged(initial: KnowledgeGraphRouteDraft, current: KnowledgeGraphRouteDraft): boolean {
  return initial.source !== current.source
    || initial.isSingle !== current.isSingle
    || initial.identityValue.trim() !== current.identityValue.trim()
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/truth-revision-state.ts =====
import { isSingleCode, scopeFromIsSingle } from "./kg-route-codes.ts"
import type {
  CorrectionContractNode,
  CorrectionNodeValue,
  CorrectionView,
} from "../correction-contract/types.ts"
import { structuredFieldLabel } from "./structured-truth.ts"

export type TruthFieldStatus = "known" | "pending" | "different"

/** 人工校验强度（后端 field_review_policy 下发）：必看 / 抽检命中 / 本条免检 */
export type FieldReviewState = "required" | "sampled" | "exempt"

export type FieldReview = {
  state: FieldReviewState
  emphasis: "critical" | "normal"
  sample_rate: number
}

export type TruthFieldRow = {
  fieldPath: string
  label: string
  truthValue: unknown
  suggestion: CorrectionNodeValue
  status: TruthFieldStatus
  nodeKey: string | null
  review: FieldReview | null
}

/** 本条要人工看的字段：必看 + 抽检命中；免检字段直接采纳模型输出。 */
export function needsHumanReview(row: TruthFieldRow): boolean {
  return !row.review || row.review.state !== "exempt"
}

/** 需要运营真正动手的：待补充，或模型建议与当前真值不同。一致的不必逐条点。 */
export function needsAttention(row: TruthFieldRow): boolean {
  return needsHumanReview(row) && row.status !== "known"
}

export type TruthReviewProgress = {
  total: number
  toReview: number
  attention: number
  exempt: number
  critical: number
}

export function truthReviewProgress(rows: readonly TruthFieldRow[]): TruthReviewProgress {
  return {
    total: rows.length,
    toReview: rows.filter(needsHumanReview).length,
    attention: rows.filter(needsAttention).length,
    exempt: rows.filter((r) => r.review?.state === "exempt").length,
    critical: rows.filter((r) => r.review?.emphasis === "critical").length,
  }
}

/** 重点字段优先，其次待办，最后一致项；免检沉底。 */
export function sortTruthFieldRows(rows: readonly TruthFieldRow[]): TruthFieldRow[] {
  const rank = (r: TruthFieldRow) => {
    if (r.review?.state === "exempt") return 3
    if (r.review?.emphasis === "critical") return 0
    return r.status === "known" ? 2 : 1
  }
  return [...rows].sort((a, b) => rank(a) - rank(b) || a.fieldPath.localeCompare(b.fieldPath))
}

export type KnowledgeGraphRouteDraft = {
  source: "domestic" | "overseas"
  /** 站点编码：国内 0/1，海外 1/2（见 kg-route-codes.ts） */
  isSingle: number
  identityValue: string
  reason: string
  confirmed: boolean
}

export function truthRevisionLabel(view: CorrectionView): string {
  const revision = Number(view.truth_revision ?? 0)
  return view.truth_source === "revision" || revision > 0
    ? `人工真值 v${revision}`
    : "导入真值 v0"
}

export function truthFieldRows(view: CorrectionView): TruthFieldRow[] {
  const truth = view.effective_truth
  if (!truth || truth.truth_kind !== "structured_fields") return []
  const flattened = flattenObject(truth.expected_result)
  const known = new Set(view.known_truth_fields ?? [])
  const pending = new Set(view.pending_truth_fields ?? [])
  const paths = [...new Set([
    ...Object.keys(flattened),
    ...known,
    ...pending,
  ])].sort((left, right) => left.localeCompare(right))

  return paths.map((fieldPath) => {
    const node = truthNodeForField(view.nodes, fieldPath)
    const suggestion = node?.model_value
    const truthValue = flattened[fieldPath]
    const status: TruthFieldStatus = pending.has(fieldPath)
      ? "pending"
      : suggestion !== undefined && !sameValue(truthValue, suggestion)
        ? "different"
        : "known"
    return {
      fieldPath,
      label: node?.label || structuredFieldLabel(fieldPath),
      truthValue,
      suggestion,
      status,
      nodeKey: node?.node_key ?? null,
      review: (node as { field_review?: FieldReview } | undefined)?.field_review ?? null,
    }
  })
}

export function knowledgeGraphRouteDraft(
  view: CorrectionView,
): KnowledgeGraphRouteDraft | null {
  const truth = view.effective_truth
  if (
    view.category_key !== "knowledge_graph"
    || !truth
    || truth.truth_kind !== "structured_fields"
  ) return null
  const source = truth.workflow_input.source === "overseas"
    ? "overseas"
    : "domestic"
  const scope = scopeFromIsSingle(source, truth.workflow_input.is_single)
    ?? (truth.truth_contract_key?.includes(".single.") ? "single" : "whole")
  const isSingle = isSingleCode(source, scope)
  const identityKey = source === "domestic" ? "ll_id" : "res_id"
  const identityValue = truth.workflow_input[identityKey]
    ?? truth.expected_result[identityKey]
  return {
    source,
    isSingle,
    identityValue: typeof identityValue === "string" ? identityValue : "",
    reason: "",
    confirmed: false,
  }
}

export function routeRevisionReady(draft: KnowledgeGraphRouteDraft): boolean {
  return Boolean(
    draft.identityValue.trim()
    && draft.reason.trim()
    && draft.confirmed,
  )
}

export function truthNodeForField(
  nodes: readonly CorrectionContractNode[],
  fieldPath: string,
): CorrectionContractNode | undefined {
  const leaf = fieldPath.split(".").at(-1) ?? fieldPath
  return nodes.find((node) => {
    if (node.read_only_reason === "route_not_selected") return false
    const metadata = node.metadata ?? {}
    return metadata.output_field_key === fieldPath
      || metadata.output_field_key === leaf
      || node.path.endsWith(`.${fieldPath}`)
  })
}

function flattenObject(
  source: Record<string, unknown>,
  prefix = "",
): Record<string, unknown> {
  const flattened: Record<string, unknown> = {}
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(source)) {
    const path = prefix ? `${prefix}.${key}` : key
    if (isRecord(value)) Object.assign(flattened, flattenObject(value, path))
    else flattened[path] = value
  }
  return flattened
}

function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return Boolean(value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value))
}

function sameValue(left: unknown, right: unknown): boolean {
  return JSON.stringify(left) === JSON.stringify(right)
}

/** 真值面板的数据身份：同一条目的同一次评测。身份变了才整体重置输入。 */
export function truthViewIdentity(
  view: Pick<CorrectionView, "lane" | "run_id" | "item_id" | "evaluation_id">,
): string {
  return `${view.lane ?? ""}:${view.run_id ?? ""}:${view.item_id ?? ""}:${view.evaluation_id ?? ""}`
}

/**
 * 数据刷新后合并字段输入：
 * - 身份变化（换条目/换评测）→ 全部按新数据初始化；
 * - 同一身份 → 只刷新用户没动过的字段，已编辑未提交的保留原样，
 *   避免刷新把人工输入悄悄换回模型建议后被当作“采纳模型输出”提交。
 */
export function reconcileTruthFieldInputs(
  current: Record<string, string>,
  initial: Record<string, string>,
  dirtyFields: ReadonlySet<string>,
  identityChanged: boolean,
): Record<string, string> {
  if (identityChanged) return { ...initial }
  const next = { ...initial }
  for (const fieldPath of dirtyFields) {
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(current, fieldPath)) next[fieldPath] = current[fieldPath]
  }
  return next
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/use-real-execution-estimate.ts =====
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react"

import {
  estimateAcceptance,
  estimateModeMismatchMessage,
  type EstimateRequest,
  type EstimateState,
  type RegressionInferenceMode,
} from "./inference-mode"
import type { RealExecutionPreview } from "./real-model-execution"

export type RealExecutionEstimate = {
  state: EstimateState
  generation: number
  /** 当前世代已拿到可用报价时才有值；迟到的过期响应永远到不了这里。 */
  preview: RealExecutionPreview | null
  isPending: boolean
  errorMessage: string | null
}

/** 带请求世代的费用预估。
 *
 * 这里不走 react-query：预估的 key 里含推理方式，而批量→在线→批量的 ABA 下前后两次
 * 批量请求共用同一个 key，缓存层没法区分「这条响应属于哪一次点击」。世代号只增不减，
 * 过期响应一律丢弃，界面因此不会把上一次的报价当成当前模式的费用依据。
 */
export function useRealExecutionEstimate(options: {
  enabled: boolean
  baselineSetId: number
  workflowVersionId: number
  mode: RegressionInferenceMode
  fetchEstimate: (request: EstimateRequest) => Promise<RealExecutionPreview>
}): RealExecutionEstimate {
