===== FILE frontend/src/features/correction-contract/candidate-refresh.ts =====
import type { CorrectionView } from "./types"

export type MechanismRefresh = {
  category_key: string
  prompt_version_ids: number[]
  v3_revision_id: number
  contract_hash: string
}

export type CandidateRefreshPlan = {
  invalidate: ReadonlyArray<readonly unknown[]>
  preserve: ReadonlyArray<readonly unknown[]>
}

export function candidateRefreshPlan(
  refresh: MechanismRefresh,
  existingRunId: number,
): CandidateRefreshPlan {
  return {
    invalidate: [
      ["evaluation-categories"],
      ["prompts", refresh.category_key],
      ["baseline-v3-revisions", refresh.category_key],
    ],
    preserve: [
      ["baseline-regression", existingRunId],
      ["baseline-correction-view", existingRunId],
    ],
  }
}

export function preservesFrozenCorrectionView(
  view: CorrectionView,
  existingRunId: number,
): boolean {
  return view.run_id === existingRunId && view.snapshot_status !== "legacy_read_only"
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/correction-contract/contract-renderer.test.tsx =====
import {
  correctionFieldsFromOutputContract,
  correctionNodeOptions,
  correctionNodeValueType,
  groupCorrectionNodes,
} from "./contract-renderer"
import type { CorrectionContractNode } from "./types"

function equal<T>(actual: T, expected: T, message: string) {
  if (JSON.stringify(actual) !== JSON.stringify(expected)) {
    throw new Error(`${message}: ${JSON.stringify(actual)} !== ${JSON.stringify(expected)}`)
  }
}

function truthy(value: unknown, message: string) {
  if (!value) throw new Error(message)
}

const nodes: CorrectionContractNode[] = [
  {
    node_key: "call_a.title",
    layer: "A",
    path: "call_a.title",
    label: "素材标题",
    description: "冻结素材标题",
    type: "text",
    semantic_version: "1",
    compatibility_key: "title",
    required: true,
    evidence: { description: "需要图片证据", required: true },
  },
  {
    node_key: "call_b.composition.subject_offset",
    layer: "B",
    path: "dimension.composition.hit_rules.subject_offset",
    label: "构图秩序：主体偏移",
    description: "主体明显偏移",
    type: "rule_hit",
    semantic_version: "1",
    compatibility_key: "subject-offset",
    required: false,
    evidence: { description: "需要规则证据" },
  },
  {
    node_key: "v3.level_thresholds",
    layer: "V3",
    path: "scoring.level_thresholds",
    label: "等级分数阈值",
    description: "冻结阈值",
    type: "list",
    semantic_version: "1",
    compatibility_key: "thresholds",
    required: true,
    evidence: { description: "仅供查看" },
    metadata: { editable: false, frozen_value: [{ min_score: 90, level: "L1" }] },
  },
]

const grouped = groupCorrectionNodes(nodes)
equal(grouped.A.map((node) => node.node_key), ["call_a.title"], "A 节点分组")
equal(grouped.B.map((node) => node.node_key), ["call_b.composition.subject_offset"], "B 节点分组")
equal(grouped.V3.map((node) => node.node_key), ["v3.level_thresholds"], "V3 节点分组")
equal(correctionNodeValueType(nodes[0]), "text", "文本类型")
equal(correctionNodeValueType(nodes[1]), "json", "规则类型")
equal(correctionNodeOptions({ ...nodes[0], type: "enum", options: ["L1", "L2"] }), ["L1", "L2"], "枚举选项")
equal(correctionNodeOptions(nodes[2]), [], "只读节点没有选项")
const dynamicFields = correctionFieldsFromOutputContract(
  {
    mode: "json_schema",
    name: "unified_aesthetic",
    strict: true,
    schema: {
      type: "object",
      properties: {
        composition: { type: "string", title: "构图", description: "构图判断" },
        color: { type: "string", title: "色彩" },
        evidence: { type: "array", title: "证据", items: { type: "string" } },
      },
      required: ["composition", "color", "evidence"],
      additionalProperties: false,
    },
  },
  { composition: "主体居中", color: "冷暖平衡", evidence: ["主体清晰"] },
)
equal(dynamicFields.map((field) => field.key), ["composition", "color", "evidence"], "单提示词动态字段")
equal(dynamicFields.map((field) => field.label), ["构图", "色彩", "证据"], "Schema 标题")
equal(dynamicFields.map((field) => field.required), [true, true, true], "Schema 必填项")
equal(dynamicFields[2]?.value, ["主体清晰"], "字段当前值")
const nestedFields = correctionFieldsFromOutputContract(
  {
    mode: "json_schema",
    name: "nested_aesthetic",
    strict: true,
    schema: {
      type: "object",
      properties: {
        dimensions: {
          type: "object",
          properties: {
            color_material: {
              type: "object",
              properties: {
                grade: { type: "integer", minimum: 1, maximum: 5, title: "色彩材质等级" },
                evidence: { type: "array", items: { type: "string", minLength: 1 }, title: "色彩材质证据" },
              },
              required: ["grade", "evidence"],
              additionalProperties: false,
            },
          },
          required: ["color_material"],
          additionalProperties: false,
        },
        labels: {
          type: "array",
          items: { type: "string", enum: ["柔和", "清晰"] },
          title: "动态标签",
        },
      },
      required: ["dimensions", "labels"],
      additionalProperties: false,
    },
  },
  {
    dimensions: { color_material: { grade: 4, evidence: ["木饰面颜色自然"] } },
    labels: ["柔和"],
  },
)
equal(
  nestedFields.map((field) => field.key),
  ["dimensions.color_material.grade", "dimensions.color_material.evidence", "labels"],
  "嵌套 Schema 必须逐叶展示",
)
equal(nestedFields[0]?.value, 4, "嵌套标量当前值")
equal(nestedFields[1]?.value, ["木饰面颜色自然"], "嵌套数组当前值")
equal(nestedFields[2]?.itemSchema, { type: "string", enum: ["柔和", "清晰"] }, "动态标签项 Schema")
truthy(true, "renderer contract")

console.log("correction contract renderer contract: passed")

===== FILE frontend/src/features/correction-contract/contract-renderer.ts =====
import type {
  CorrectionContractNode,
  CorrectionLayer,
  CorrectionNodeGroup,
  CorrectionNodeValue,
} from "./types"

const layerOrder: readonly CorrectionLayer[] = ["A", "B", "V3"]

export type PromptCorrectionField = {
  key: string
  label: string
  description: string
  type: string
  required: boolean
  value: unknown
  fieldSchema?: Record<string, unknown>
  itemSchema?: Record<string, unknown>
  minimum?: number
  maximum?: number
  minItems?: number
  maxItems?: number
}

function schemaType(schema: Record<string, unknown>): string {
  const rawType = Array.isArray(schema.type)
    ? schema.type.find((item) => item !== "null")
    : schema.type
  return typeof rawType === "string" ? rawType : "object"
}

function correctionSchemaLeaves(
  schema: Record<string, unknown>,
  current: unknown,
  prefix: string[] = [],
  inheritedRequired = true,
): PromptCorrectionField[] {
  const properties = schema.properties
  if (
    schemaType(schema) === "object"
    && properties
    && typeof properties === "object"
    && !Array.isArray(properties)
    && Object.keys(properties).length > 0
  ) {
    const required = new Set(
      Array.isArray(schema.required)
        ? schema.required.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string")
        : [],
    )
    const currentRecord = current && typeof current === "object" && !Array.isArray(current)
      ? current as Record<string, unknown>
      : {}
    return Object.entries(properties as Record<string, unknown>).flatMap(([key, rawChild]) => {
      if (!rawChild || typeof rawChild !== "object" || Array.isArray(rawChild)) return []
      return correctionSchemaLeaves(
        rawChild as Record<string, unknown>,
        currentRecord[key],
        [...prefix, key],
        inheritedRequired && required.has(key),
      )
    })
  }

  const key = prefix.join(".")
  const itemSchema = schema.items && typeof schema.items === "object" && !Array.isArray(schema.items)
    ? schema.items as Record<string, unknown>
    : undefined
  const field: PromptCorrectionField = {
    key,
    label: typeof schema.title === "string" && schema.title ? schema.title : prefix.at(-1) ?? "output",
    description: typeof schema.description === "string" ? schema.description : `核对提示词输出字段 ${key || "output"}`,
    type: schemaType(schema),
    required: inheritedRequired,
    value: current,
    fieldSchema: { ...schema },
    ...(itemSchema ? { itemSchema: { ...itemSchema } } : {}),
  }
  for (const bound of ["minimum", "maximum", "minItems", "maxItems"] as const) {
    if (typeof schema[bound] === "number") field[bound] = schema[bound]
  }
  return [field]
}

export function correctionFieldsFromOutputContract(
  contract: Record<string, unknown>,
  result: Record<string, unknown>,
): PromptCorrectionField[] {
  if (contract.mode !== "json_schema") return []
  const schema = contract.schema
  if (!schema || typeof schema !== "object" || Array.isArray(schema)) return []
  const schemaRecord = schema as Record<string, unknown>
  return correctionSchemaLeaves(schemaRecord, result)
}

export function groupCorrectionNodes(
  nodes: readonly CorrectionContractNode[],
): CorrectionNodeGroup {
  const grouped: CorrectionNodeGroup = { A: [], B: [], V3: [] }
  nodes.forEach((node, index) => {
    if (!grouped[node.layer]) return
    grouped[node.layer].push({ ...node, order: node.order ?? index })
  })
  for (const layer of layerOrder) {
    grouped[layer].sort((left, right) => (left.order ?? 0) - (right.order ?? 0))
  }
  return grouped
}

export type WorkflowCorrectionNodeGroup = {
  group_key: string
  node_instance_id: string
  foreach_item_key: string | null
  workflow_node_key: string
  workflow_kind: string
  workflow_order: number
  module_type_key: string | null
  label: string
  nodes: CorrectionContractNode[]
}

export type WorkflowCorrectionGroupState = "editable" | "read_only" | "skipped"
export type WorkflowCorrectionPresentation = "primary" | "supplementary"

export type WorkflowCorrectionDisclosure = {
  defaultOpen: boolean
  groups: WorkflowCorrectionNodeGroup[]
  executedGroups: number
  skippedGroups: number
  editableGroups: number
}

export function workflowCorrectionGroupState(
  group: WorkflowCorrectionNodeGroup,
): WorkflowCorrectionGroupState {
  if (
    group.nodes.length > 0
    && group.nodes.every((node) => (
      node.read_only_reason === "route_not_selected"
      || node.metadata?.read_only_reason === "route_not_selected"
    ))
  ) return "skipped"
  return group.nodes.some((node) => correctionNodeEditable(node)) ? "editable" : "read_only"
}

export function groupWorkflowCorrectionNodes(
  nodes: readonly CorrectionContractNode[],
): WorkflowCorrectionNodeGroup[] {
  const grouped = new Map<string, WorkflowCorrectionNodeGroup>()
  nodes.forEach((node, index) => {
    const workflowNodeKey = typeof node.metadata?.workflow_node_key === "string"
      ? node.metadata.workflow_node_key
      : ""
    const nodeInstanceId = typeof node.metadata?.node_instance_id === "string"
      ? node.metadata.node_instance_id
      : workflowNodeKey
    if (!nodeInstanceId) return
    const workflowOrder = typeof node.metadata?.workflow_order === "number"
      ? node.metadata.workflow_order
      : index
    const workflowKind = typeof node.metadata?.workflow_kind === "string"
      ? node.metadata.workflow_kind
      : "unknown"
    const workflowDisplayName = typeof node.metadata?.workflow_display_name === "string"
      ? node.metadata.workflow_display_name
      : node.label
    const moduleTypeKey = typeof node.metadata?.module_type_key === "string"
      ? node.metadata.module_type_key
      : null
    const foreachItemKey = typeof node.metadata?.foreach_item_key === "string"
      ? node.metadata.foreach_item_key
      : null
    const groupKey = foreachItemKey === null
      ? nodeInstanceId
      : `${nodeInstanceId}:item:${JSON.stringify(foreachItemKey)}`
    const group = grouped.get(groupKey) ?? {
      group_key: groupKey,
      node_instance_id: nodeInstanceId,
      foreach_item_key: foreachItemKey,
      workflow_node_key: workflowNodeKey,
      workflow_kind: workflowKind,
      workflow_order: workflowOrder,
      module_type_key: moduleTypeKey,
      label: foreachItemKey === null
        ? workflowDisplayName
        : `${workflowDisplayName} · ${foreachItemKey}`,
      nodes: [],
    }
    group.nodes.push({ ...node, order: node.order ?? index })
    grouped.set(groupKey, group)
  })
  return [...grouped.values()]
    .map((group) => ({
      ...group,
      nodes: [...group.nodes].sort((left, right) => (left.order ?? 0) - (right.order ?? 0)),
    }))
    .sort((left, right) => left.workflow_order - right.workflow_order)
}

export function workflowCorrectionDisclosure(
  nodes: readonly CorrectionContractNode[],
  presentation: WorkflowCorrectionPresentation = "primary",
): WorkflowCorrectionDisclosure {
  const groups = groupWorkflowCorrectionNodes(nodes)
  const states = groups.map(workflowCorrectionGroupState)
  return {
    defaultOpen: presentation === "primary",
    groups,
    executedGroups: states.filter((state) => state !== "skipped").length,
    skippedGroups: states.filter((state) => state === "skipped").length,
    editableGroups: states.filter((state) => state === "editable").length,
  }
}

export function correctionNodeOptions(node: CorrectionContractNode): unknown[] {
  const options = node.options ?? node.allowed_values ?? node.values
  return Array.isArray(options) ? options : []
}

export function correctionNodeValueType(
  node: Pick<CorrectionContractNode, "type">,
): "text" | "number" | "boolean" | "json" | "enum" {
  const type = String(node.type || "text").toLowerCase()
  if (type === "enum" || type === "enumeration") return "enum"
  if (type === "boolean" || type === "bool") return "boolean"
  if (
    type === "integer"
    || type === "int"
    || type === "number"
    || type === "float"
    || type === "decimal"
    || type.startsWith("integer_")
    || type.startsWith("number_")
    || type.startsWith("float_")
  ) return "number"
  if (type === "list" || type === "array" || type === "object" || type === "json_object") return "json"
  if (type === "rule_hit" || type === "rule_judgement" || type === "rule_judgment") return "json"
  if (type === "text" || type === "string") return "text"
  return "json"
}

export function correctionNodeEditable(node: CorrectionContractNode): boolean {
  return node.editable !== false && node.metadata?.editable !== false && !node.read_only
}

export function correctionNodeDisplayValue(
  node: CorrectionContractNode,
  value: CorrectionNodeValue,
): CorrectionNodeValue {
  if (value !== undefined && value !== null) return value
  if (node.metadata && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(node.metadata, "frozen_value")) {
    return node.metadata.frozen_value
  }
  return value
}

export function correctionNodeInputValue(
  node: CorrectionContractNode,
  value: CorrectionNodeValue,
): string {
  const displayValue = correctionNodeDisplayValue(node, value)
  if (displayValue === undefined || displayValue === null) return ""
  if (typeof displayValue === "string") return displayValue
  if (typeof displayValue === "number" || typeof displayValue === "boolean") return String(displayValue)
  return JSON.stringify(displayValue, null, 2)
}

export function parseCorrectionNodeInput(
  node: CorrectionContractNode,
  rawValue: string,
): CorrectionNodeValue {
  const valueType = correctionNodeValueType(node)
  if (valueType === "text") return rawValue
  if (valueType === "enum") {
    // Native select values are strings; preserve numeric/boolean contract types.
    const option = correctionNodeOptions(node).find(value => String(value) === rawValue)
    return option === undefined ? rawValue : option
  }
  if (valueType === "number") {
    // 空白视为未填：Number("") === 0，不能把清空的输入框静默保存成 0
    if (!rawValue.trim()) return null
    const value = Number(rawValue)
    return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : rawValue
  }
  if (valueType === "boolean") return rawValue === "true"
  try {
    return JSON.parse(rawValue)
  } catch {
    return rawValue
  }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/correction-contract/contract-renderer.tsx =====
import { useMemo } from "react"
import { CaretDown, FloppyDisk, LockKey, WarningCircle } from "@phosphor-icons/react"

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea"
import {
  correctionNodeDisplayValue,
  correctionNodeEditable,
  correctionNodeInputValue,
  correctionNodeOptions,
  correctionNodeValueType,
  groupCorrectionNodes,
  parseCorrectionNodeInput,
  workflowCorrectionDisclosure,
  workflowCorrectionGroupState,
} from "./contract-renderer"
import type {
  CorrectionContractNode,
  CorrectionDraft,
  CorrectionNodeValue,
  CorrectionView,
} from "./types"
import { ruleModuleLabels } from "@/features/workflow-manager/runtime-node-details"
import { correctionPaidRerunNodeKeys } from "./correction-view-state"
import type { WorkflowCorrectionPresentation } from "./contract-renderer"
import { deductionRuleLabel } from "./deduction-breakdown"

export { groupCorrectionNodes, groupWorkflowCorrectionNodes, correctionNodeOptions, correctionNodeValueType } from "./contract-renderer"

export function renderArrayCorrectionNode(
  node: CorrectionContractNode,
  value: CorrectionNodeValue,
  onChange: (value: CorrectionNodeValue) => void,
  disabled = false,
) {
  const values = Array.isArray(value) ? value : []
  const rules = node.metadata?.deduction_rule_options
  if (rules?.length) {
    const hits = values.map(item => item && typeof item === "object" ? item as Record<string, unknown> : {})
    const update = (index: number, patch: Record<string, unknown>) => onChange(hits.map((hit, i) => i === index ? {...hit, ...patch} : hit))
    const unused = rules.filter(rule => !hits.some(hit => hit.rule_id === rule.rule_id))
    return <div className="space-y-3">
      {!hits.length && <p className="text-sm">不命中扣分规则</p>}
      {hits.map((hit, index) => <div key={index} className="space-y-2 border border-[var(--line)] p-3 rounded-md">
        <label className="block text-xs font-semibold">命中规则<select aria-label={`${node.label} 第 ${index + 1} 条规则`} disabled={disabled} value={String(hit.rule_id ?? "")} className="visual-native-select mt-1 w-full" onChange={event => update(index, {rule_id: event.target.value})}>
          {!rules.some(rule => rule.rule_id === hit.rule_id) && <option value={String(hit.rule_id ?? "")} disabled>原始规则：{String(hit.rule_id ?? "未知")}</option>}
          {rules.map(rule => <option key={rule.rule_id} value={rule.rule_id} disabled={hits.some((other, i) => i !== index && other.rule_id === rule.rule_id)}>{deductionRuleLabel(rule.rule_id)}（维度内扣 {rule.deduction ?? "未记录"} 分）</option>)}
        </select></label>
        <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{rules.find(rule => rule.rule_id === hit.rule_id)?.description}</p>
        <Textarea aria-label={`${node.label} 第 ${index + 1} 条命中依据`} disabled={disabled} value={String(hit.evidence ?? "")} onChange={event => update(index, {evidence: event.target.value})} placeholder="填写具体对象、位置和可见问题" />
        <button type="button" disabled={disabled || hits.length <= (node.minItems ?? 0)} className="visual-native-button underline disabled:opacity-50" onClick={() => onChange(hits.filter((_, i) => i !== index))}>取消这条命中</button>
      </div>)}
      <button type="button" disabled={disabled || !unused.length || hits.length >= (node.maxItems ?? Infinity)} className="visual-native-button border border-[var(--line-strong)] px-3 py-2 disabled:opacity-50" onClick={() => unused[0] && onChange([...hits, {rule_id: unused[0].rule_id, confidence: "high", evidence: ""}])}>添加规则命中</button>
      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">更改严重度或取消误判；保存后重算总分，原模型证据保留。</p>
    </div>
  }
  const rawItemSchema = node.metadata?.output_array_item_schema
  const itemSchema = rawItemSchema && typeof rawItemSchema === "object" && !Array.isArray(rawItemSchema)
    ? rawItemSchema as Record<string, unknown>
    : {}
  const itemType = typeof itemSchema.type === "string" ? itemSchema.type : "string"
  // Historical contracts can omit the item schema, including for object arrays.
  // Preserve their JSON types instead of coercing every item to a string.
  if (typeof itemSchema.type !== "string" && !Array.isArray(itemSchema.enum)) {
    return <Textarea
      className="min-h-28 font-data text-xs"
      value={correctionNodeInputValue(node, value)}
      disabled={disabled}
      onChange={(event) => onChange(parseCorrectionNodeInput(node, event.target.value))}
      aria-label={node.label}
    />
  }
  const itemOptions = Array.isArray(itemSchema.enum) ? itemSchema.enum : []
  const minimum = typeof node.minItems === "number" ? node.minItems : 0
  const maximum = typeof node.maxItems === "number" ? node.maxItems : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY
  const replaceAt = (index: number, next: unknown) => {
    const updated = [...values]
    updated[index] = next
    onChange(updated)
  }
  const parseItem = (raw: string): unknown => {
    if (itemType === "integer" || itemType === "number") {
      const numeric = Number(raw)
      return Number.isFinite(numeric) ? numeric : raw
    }
    if (itemType === "boolean") return raw === "true"
    if (itemType === "object" || itemType === "array") {
      try {
        return JSON.parse(raw)
      } catch {
        return raw
      }
    }
    return raw
  }
  const emptyItem = itemType === "object" ? {} : itemType === "array" ? [] : itemType === "boolean" ? false : itemType === "integer" || itemType === "number" ? 0 : ""
  return (
    <div className="space-y-2" data-array-correction={node.node_key}>
      {values.map((item, index) => (
        <div key={`${node.node_key}-${index}`} className="flex items-start gap-2">
          {itemOptions.length && itemType === "string" ? <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <Input aria-label={`${node.label} 第 ${index + 1} 项`} list={`array-choices-${node.node_key}-${index}`}
              value={String(item ?? "")} disabled={disabled} placeholder="选择或手动补写" maxLength={2000}
              onChange={event => replaceAt(index, event.target.value)} />
            <datalist id={`array-choices-${node.node_key}-${index}`}>{itemOptions.map(option => <option key={String(option)} value={String(option)} />)}</datalist>
          </div> : itemOptions.length ? (
            <select
              className="visual-native-select min-w-0 flex-1 disabled:bg-alias-surface-014"
              value={String(item ?? "")}
              disabled={disabled}
              onChange={(event) => replaceAt(index, parseItem(event.target.value))}
              aria-label={`${node.label} 第 ${index + 1} 项`}
            >
              <option value="">请选择</option>
              {itemOptions.map((option) => <option key={String(option)} value={String(option)}>{String(option)}</option>)}
            </select>
          ) : itemType === "object" || itemType === "array" ? (
            <Textarea
              className="min-h-20 flex-1 font-data text-xs"
              value={typeof item === "string" ? item : JSON.stringify(item, null, 2)}
              disabled={disabled}
              onChange={(event) => replaceAt(index, parseItem(event.target.value))}
              aria-label={`${node.label} 第 ${index + 1} 项`}
            />
          ) : (
            <Input
              className="flex-1"
              type={itemType === "integer" || itemType === "number" ? "number" : "text"}
              value={String(item ?? "")}
              disabled={disabled}
              onChange={(event) => replaceAt(index, parseItem(event.target.value))}
              aria-label={`${node.label} 第 ${index + 1} 项`}
            />
          )}
          <button
            type="button"
            className="visual-native-button shrink-0 border border-[var(--line-strong)] px-3 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
            disabled={disabled || values.length <= minimum}
            onClick={() => onChange(values.filter((_, itemIndex) => itemIndex !== index))}
            aria-label={`删除${node.label}第 ${index + 1} 项`}
          >删除</button>
        </div>
      ))}
      <button
        type="button"
        className="visual-native-button border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white px-3 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
        disabled={disabled || values.length >= maximum}
        onClick={() => onChange([...values, emptyItem])}
      >添加一项</button>
    </div>
  )
}

export function renderCorrectionNode(
  node: CorrectionContractNode,
  value: CorrectionNodeValue,
  onChange: (value: CorrectionNodeValue) => void,
  disabled = false,
) {
  const readOnly = disabled || !correctionNodeEditable(node)
  const valueType = correctionNodeValueType(node)
  const inputValue = correctionNodeInputValue(node, value)
  if (valueType === "enum") {
    const options = correctionNodeOptions(node)
    const unknownCurrent = inputValue !== "" && !options.some((option) => String(option) === inputValue)
    const field = node.node_key.split(".").at(-1) ?? ""
    const customText = options.length > 0 && options.every(option => typeof option === "string")
      && !["grade", "level", "level_cap", "final_level", "is_single", "source", "confidence", "status"].includes(field)
    if (customText) return <div>
      <Input aria-label={node.label} list={`choices-${node.node_key}`} value={inputValue} disabled={readOnly}
        placeholder="选择已有值或手动补写" onChange={event => onChange(event.target.value)} maxLength={2000} />
      <datalist id={`choices-${node.node_key}`}>{options.map(option => <option key={String(option)} value={String(option)} />)}</datalist>
      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">可选择已有值，也可手动补写；提交后保留人工来源。</p>
    </div>
    return (
      <select
        className="visual-native-select flex min-w-0 w-full focus-visible:border-foreground focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary focus-visible:ring-offset-1 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-alias-surface-014"
        value={inputValue}
        disabled={readOnly}
        onChange={(event) => onChange(parseCorrectionNodeInput(node, event.target.value))}
        aria-label={node.label}
      >
        {!options.some((option) => option === "") && <option value="" disabled>请选择</option>}
        {unknownCurrent && <option value={inputValue} disabled>原始值：{inputValue}（不在当前选项中）</option>}
        {options.map((option) => (
          <option key={String(option)} value={String(option)}>{node.metadata?.option_labels?.[String(option)] ?? (option === "" ? "未触发（空）" : String(option))}</option>
        ))}
      </select>
    )
  }
  if (valueType === "number") {
    return (
      <Input
        type="number"
        value={inputValue}
        min={node.minimum ?? node.min}
        max={node.maximum ?? node.max}
        disabled={readOnly}
        onChange={(event) => onChange(parseCorrectionNodeInput(node, event.target.value))}
        aria-label={node.label}
      />
    )
  }
  if (valueType === "boolean") {
    return (
      <label className="visual-native-choice flex min-h-11 items-center gap-3 text-sm">
        <input
          type="checkbox"
          className="visual-native-checkbox size-4"
          checked={Boolean(correctionNodeDisplayValue(node, value))}
          disabled={readOnly}
          onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.checked)}
          aria-label={node.label}
        />
        <span>{eventLabel(Boolean(correctionNodeDisplayValue(node, value)))}</span>
      </label>
    )
  }
  if (valueType === "text") {
    return (
      <Input
        value={inputValue}
        disabled={readOnly}
        onChange={(event) => onChange(parseCorrectionNodeInput(node, event.target.value))}
        aria-label={node.label}
      />
    )
  }
  if (String(node.type).toLowerCase() === "array") {
    return renderArrayCorrectionNode(node, value, onChange, readOnly)
  }
  return (
    <Textarea
      value={inputValue}
      disabled={readOnly}
      onChange={(event) => onChange(parseCorrectionNodeInput(node, event.target.value))}
      aria-label={node.label}
      className="min-h-28 font-data text-xs"
    />
  )
}

function eventLabel(value: boolean): string {
  return value ? "是" : "否"
}

function layerLabel(layer: "A" | "B" | "V3"): string {
  return layer === "A" ? "调用 A" : layer === "B" ? "调用 B" : "等级撮合器"
}

export function CorrectionContractRenderer({
  view,
  draft,
  onChange,
  onSubmit,
  pending = false,
  submitDisabled = false,
  submitBlockReason = null,
  disabled = false,
  presentation = "primary",
}: {
  view: CorrectionView
  draft: CorrectionDraft
  onChange: (nodeKey: string, patch: Partial<CorrectionDraft[string]>) => void
  onSubmit?: () => void
  pending?: boolean
  submitDisabled?: boolean
  submitBlockReason?: string | null
  disabled?: boolean
  presentation?: WorkflowCorrectionPresentation
}) {
  const grouped = useMemo(() => groupCorrectionNodes(view.nodes), [view.nodes])
  const workflowDisclosure = useMemo(() => workflowCorrectionDisclosure(view.nodes, presentation), [view.nodes, presentation])
  const workflowGroups = workflowDisclosure.groups
  const paidRerunNodeKeys = useMemo(
    () => correctionPaidRerunNodeKeys(draft, view),
    [draft, view],
  )
  const paidRerunRequired = paidRerunNodeKeys.length > 0
  if (!view.contract) {
    return (
      <section className="border-y border-alias-edge-051 bg-alias-surface-075 px-5 py-5" aria-label="纠偏合同不可用">
        <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-alias-content-041"><WarningCircle />当前运行没有可编辑的纠偏合同</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-029">{view.unavailable_reason || "历史快照缺少完整合同，只能查看原始结果。"}</p>
        <CorrectionPublicationNotice view={view} />
      </section>
    )
  }
  if (workflowGroups.length) {
    return (
      <section className="border-y border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051" aria-label="合同驱动纠偏面板">
        <CorrectionPublicationNotice view={view} />
        <details key={`${view.run_id}:${view.item_id}:${view.evaluation_id}:${presentation}`} className="visual-native-details group" open={workflowDisclosure.defaultOpen}>
          <summary className="flex min-h-16 list-none items-center justify-between gap-4 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-inset focus-visible:ring-primary md:px-7">
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-bold">{presentation === "primary" ? "评测结果与逐项纠偏" : "工作流逐项纠偏与运行证据"}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                本轮执行 {workflowDisclosure.executedGroups} 步 · 可纠偏 {workflowDisclosure.editableGroups} 步
                {workflowDisclosure.skippedGroups ? ` · ${workflowDisclosure.skippedGroups} 步未走` : ""}
                {presentation === "primary" ? "；按执行顺序核对结果和纠偏。" : "；需要排查或做流程节点纠偏时再展开。"}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2">
              <Badge tone={view.snapshot_status === "frozen" ? "success" : "warning"}>{view.snapshot_status === "frozen" ? "历史快照" : "只读兼容"}</Badge>
              <CaretDown className="transition-transform duration-[var(--visual-ease-out-ms)] ease-[var(--visual-ease-out)] group-open:rotate-180" aria-hidden="true" />
            </div>
          </summary>
          <div className="space-y-4 border-t border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-5 md:px-7">
            <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
              按实际工作流顺序查看每一步；只有标记为“可逐条纠偏”的内容可以修改，其余只用于核对本轮输入、输出和状态。
            </p>
            {view.unavailable_reason && <p className="border border-alias-edge-053 bg-alias-surface-068 px-3 py-2 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-033 rounded-md">{view.unavailable_reason}</p>}
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {workflowGroups.map((group, groupIndex) => {
                const first = group.nodes[0]
                const groupState = workflowCorrectionGroupState(group)
                const editable = groupState === "editable"
                const resultVisible = presentation === "primary" && groupState !== "skipped"
                  && ["prompt", "foreach_image", "rule_module", "rule_contract", "level_matcher", "aggregate_case"].includes(group.workflow_kind)
                return (
                  <details key={group.group_key} className="visual-native-details border rounded-md border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white" open={editable || resultVisible}>
                    <summary className="flex min-h-14 list-none items-center justify-between gap-3 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-inset focus-visible:ring-primary">
                      <div>
                        <p className="font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">步骤 {groupIndex + 1} · {workflowKindLabel(group.workflow_kind)}{group.module_type_key ? ` · ${ruleModuleLabels[group.module_type_key] ?? "规则模块"}` : ""}</p>
                        <h3 className="mt-1 text-sm font-bold">{group.label}</h3>
                      </div>
                      <Badge tone={editable ? "active" : "neutral"}>
                        {groupState === "skipped" ? "本次未执行" : editable ? "可逐条纠偏" : "运行证据（只读）"}
                      </Badge>
                    </summary>
                    <div className="space-y-3 border-t border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-4">
                      {first && <WorkflowAssetSummary node={first} />}
                      {group.nodes.map((node) => <CorrectionNodeCard key={node.node_key} node={node} view={view} draft={draft} disabled={disabled} onChange={onChange} />)}
                    </div>
                  </details>
                )
              })}
            </div>
            <details className="visual-native-details border rounded-md border-[var(--line)] bg-white">
              <summary className="min-h-11 font-semibold focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-inset focus-visible:ring-primary">技术快照信息</summary>
              <p className="border-t border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-3 font-data text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                合同版本 <span className="visual-code">{view.contract.contract_version || "—"}</span> · 合同哈希 <span className="visual-code">{view.contract.contract_hash.slice(0, 12)}</span>…
                {view.read_only ? " · 当前快照不可编辑" : ""}
              </p>
            </details>
            {onSubmit && (
              <footer className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-4">
                <div className="max-w-3xl text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                  <p>只提交已修改且填写理由的节点；人工覆盖会保留审计记录，后续重跑按冻结工作流版本处理。</p>
                  {submitBlockReason && <p className="text-alias-content-033">{submitBlockReason}</p>}
                  {paidRerunRequired && <p className="mt-2 border border-alias-edge-053 bg-alias-surface-068 px-3 py-2 font-semibold text-alias-content-033 rounded-md" role="alert">本次修改会改变下游提示词输入，并产生真实模型费用；提交前还会要求再次确认。</p>}
                </div>
                <CorrectionSaveButton view={view} draft={draft} disabled={disabled} submitDisabled={submitDisabled} pending={pending} onSubmit={onSubmit} />
              </footer>
            )}
          </div>
        </details>
      </section>
    )
  }
  return (
    <section className="space-y-4 border-y border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 px-5 py-5" aria-label="合同驱动纠偏面板">
      <CorrectionPublicationNotice view={view} />
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <p className="text-sm font-bold">合同驱动纠偏</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">本轮版本 <span className="visual-code">{view.contract.contract_version || "—"}</span> · 合同哈希 <span className="visual-code">{view.contract.contract_hash.slice(0, 12)}</span>…</p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Badge tone={view.snapshot_status === "frozen" ? "success" : "warning"}>{view.snapshot_status === "frozen" ? "历史快照" : "只读兼容"}</Badge>
          {view.read_only && <Badge tone="warning"><LockKey />不可编辑</Badge>}
        </div>
      </header>
      {view.unavailable_reason && <p className="border border-alias-edge-053 bg-alias-surface-068 px-3 py-2 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-033 rounded-md">{view.unavailable_reason}</p>}
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {(["A", "B", "V3"] as const).map((layer) => {
          const nodes = grouped[layer]
          if (!nodes.length) return null
          return (
            <section key={layer} className="space-y-3 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-4 first:border-t-0 first:pt-0" aria-label={`${layerLabel(layer)}纠偏节点`}>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3"><h3 className="text-sm font-bold">{layerLabel(layer)}</h3><span className="font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{nodes.length} 项</span></div>
              <div className="space-y-3">
                {nodes.map((node) => <CorrectionNodeCard key={node.node_key} node={node} view={view} draft={draft} disabled={disabled} onChange={onChange} />)}
              </div>
            </section>
          )
        })}
      </div>
      {onSubmit && (
        <footer className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-4">
          <div className="max-w-3xl text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            <p>只提交已修改且填写理由的节点；冻结的等级规则仍由服务端重算。</p>
            {submitBlockReason && <p className="text-alias-content-033">{submitBlockReason}</p>}
            {paidRerunRequired && <p className="mt-2 border border-alias-edge-053 bg-alias-surface-068 px-3 py-2 font-semibold text-alias-content-033 rounded-md" role="alert">本次修改会改变下游提示词输入，并产生真实模型费用；提交前还会要求再次确认。</p>}
          </div>
          <CorrectionSaveButton view={view} draft={draft} disabled={disabled} submitDisabled={submitDisabled} pending={pending} onSubmit={onSubmit} />
        </footer>
      )}
    </section>
  )
}

/** The in-form and paired-workbench actions share the exact same label and gate. */
export function CorrectionSaveButton({ view, draft, disabled = false, submitDisabled = false, pending = false, onSubmit }: {
  view: CorrectionView
  draft: CorrectionDraft
  disabled?: boolean
  submitDisabled?: boolean
  pending?: boolean
  onSubmit: () => void
}) {
  const paidRerunRequired = correctionPaidRerunNodeKeys(draft, view).length > 0
  const workflow = workflowCorrectionDisclosure(view.nodes, "primary").groups.length > 0
  return <Button disabled={disabled || view.read_only || submitDisabled || pending} onClick={onSubmit}>
    <FloppyDisk />{pending ? "正在保存" : paidRerunRequired ? "确认费用并真实重跑" : workflow ? "保存节点纠偏" : "保存合同纠偏"}
  </Button>
}

export function CorrectionPublicationNotice({ view }: { view: CorrectionView }) {
  const item = view.publication
  if (!item) return null
  const labels = {
    superseded_source_not_published: "仅更新历史：当前来源已更新或删除，本次纠偏不覆盖当前下游",
    not_published: "尚无纠偏发布记录",
    queued: "纠偏已保存，等待下游投递与读回",
    delivered: item.read_back_verified ? "纠偏已投递，下游读回一致" : "纠偏已投递，读回证据待核对",
    failed: "纠偏已保存，下游投递未完成，请在增量运行中查看异常",
    unknown: "发布证据不完整，请核对投递记录",
  }
  return <div role="status" className="border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-5 py-3 text-xs leading-5">
    <p className="font-bold">纠偏作用于源版本 {item.source_version ?? "未知"} · {labels[item.status]}</p>
    <p className="text-[var(--muted)]">人工纠偏仅约束原源版本；新源版本重新自动评测。保存成功不等于下游已更新。</p>
  </div>
}

export function CorrectionNodeCard({ node, view, draft, disabled, onChange }: {
  node: CorrectionContractNode
  view: CorrectionView
  draft: CorrectionDraft
  disabled: boolean
  onChange: (nodeKey: string, patch: Partial<CorrectionDraft[string]>) => void
}) {
  const nodeDraft = draft[node.node_key] ?? {
    value: node.human_value ?? node.current_value ?? node.model_value,
    reason: node.reason ?? "",
    evidence: [],
  }
  const editable = !disabled && !view.read_only && correctionNodeEditable(node)
  const evidenceDescription = correctionEvidenceDescription(node)
  const evidenceText = correctionEvidenceText(nodeDraft.evidence)
  return (
    <article className="space-y-2 border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white px-4 py-4 rounded-md" data-testid={`correction-node-${node.node_key}`}>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-2"><div><p className="text-sm font-semibold">{node.label}</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{node.description}</p></div>{!editable && <Badge tone="neutral"><LockKey />{node.read_only_reason === "route_not_selected" ? "本次未执行" : "只读"}</Badge>}{node.inheritance?.status && node.inheritance.status !== "current" && <Badge tone={node.inheritance.status === "inherited" ? "success" : "warning"}>{node.inheritance.status === "inherited" ? "已继承" : node.inheritance.status === "new" ? "新增待确认" : "合同已变化"}</Badge>}</div>
      <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(220px,0.7fr)]">
        <div>{renderCorrectionNode(node, nodeDraft.value, (value) => onChange(node.node_key, { value }), !editable)}</div>
        <div className="space-y-3"><label className="block text-xs font-semibold">人工理由{node.required && "（必填）"}<Textarea className="mt-2 min-h-24" value={nodeDraft.reason} disabled={!editable} placeholder={evidenceDescription} onChange={(event) => onChange(node.node_key, { reason: event.target.value })} /></label><label className="block text-xs font-semibold">人工证据{node.evidence && typeof node.evidence === "object" && !Array.isArray(node.evidence) && node.evidence.required && "（必填）"}<Textarea className="mt-2 min-h-20" value={evidenceText} disabled={!editable} placeholder={evidenceDescription} onChange={(event) => onChange(node.node_key, { evidence: event.target.value.trim() ? [{ text: event.target.value }] : [] })} /></label><p className="text-xs leading-4 text-[var(--muted)]">{evidenceDescription}</p></div>
      </div>
      {node.steps && node.steps.length > 0 && <ol className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{node.steps.map((step, index) => <li key={`${node.node_key}-step-${index}`}>{index + 1}. {step}</li>)}</ol>}
    </article>
  )
}

function WorkflowAssetSummary({ node }: { node: CorrectionContractNode }) {
  const metadata = node.metadata ?? {}
  const parameters = metadata.selected_parameters && typeof metadata.selected_parameters === "object"
    ? metadata.selected_parameters as Record<string, unknown>
    : {}
  const promptVersion = metadata.prompt_version && typeof metadata.prompt_version === "object"
    ? metadata.prompt_version as Record<string, unknown>
    : null
  const modelVersion = metadata.model_version && typeof metadata.model_version === "object"
    ? metadata.model_version as Record<string, unknown>
    : null
  const ruleVersion = metadata.rule_version && typeof metadata.rule_version === "object"
    ? metadata.rule_version as Record<string, unknown>
    : null
  const componentVersion = metadata.component_version && typeof metadata.component_version === "object"
    ? metadata.component_version as Record<string, unknown>
    : null
  const moduleTypeKey = typeof metadata.module_type_key === "string" ? metadata.module_type_key : null
  if (!promptVersion && !modelVersion && !ruleVersion && !componentVersion && !Object.keys(parameters).length) return null
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 border-y border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
      {moduleTypeKey && <span><strong className="text-[var(--ink)]">规则模块</strong> {ruleModuleLabels[moduleTypeKey] ?? moduleTypeKey}</span>}
      {promptVersion && <span><strong className="text-[var(--ink)]">提示词版本</strong> <span className="visual-code">{String(promptVersion.reference ?? promptVersion.version ?? promptVersion.version_id ?? "—")}</span></span>}
      {ruleVersion && <span><strong className="text-[var(--ink)]">规则模块版本</strong> <span className="visual-code">{String(ruleVersion.version ?? ruleVersion.revision ?? ruleVersion.version_id ?? "—")}</span></span>}
      {modelVersion && <span><strong className="text-[var(--ink)]">模型与运行参数</strong> {String(modelVersion.name ?? modelVersion.model_id ?? "—")} · {String(parameters.max_tokens ?? "—")} Token · {thinkingLevelLabel(parameters.thinking_level)}</span>}
    </div>
  )
}

function workflowKindLabel(kind: string) {
  return ({
    upstream_connector: "读取上游数据",
    prompt: "AI 提示词调用",
    rule_module: "规则判断",
    rule_contract: "规则与红线判断",
    level_matcher: "等级撮合",
    downstream_projection: "写入下游",
    branch: "分成多条路线",
    join: "汇合多条路线",
  } as Record<string, string>)[kind] ?? "工作流步骤"
}

function thinkingLevelLabel(value: unknown) {
  return ({ auto: "自动思考", low: "快速思考", medium: "平衡思考", high: "深度思考" } as Record<string, string>)[String(value)] ?? "思考层级未记录"
}

function correctionEvidenceDescription(node: CorrectionContractNode): string {
  const evidence = node.evidence
  return evidence && typeof evidence === "object" && !Array.isArray(evidence) && typeof evidence.description === "string"
    ? evidence.description
    : "请填写可复核的图片或规则证据"
}

function correctionEvidenceText(evidence: Array<Record<string, unknown>>): string {
  const first = evidence[0]
  if (!first) return ""
  const value = first.text ?? first.new_evidence ?? first.description ?? first.value
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : value == null ? "" : JSON.stringify(value)
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/correction-contract/correction-view-state.test.ts =====
import type { CorrectionView } from "./types"
import {
  correctionDraftFromView,
  correctionChangedNodeKeys,
  correctionPaidRerunNodeKeys,
  correctionSubmissionPayload,
  correctionSubmitBlocker,
  mergeCorrectionResponse,
  updateCorrectionDraft,
} from "./correction-view-state.ts"

function equal<T>(actual: T, expected: T, message: string) {
  if (JSON.stringify(actual) !== JSON.stringify(expected)) {
    throw new Error(`${message}: ${JSON.stringify(actual)} !== ${JSON.stringify(expected)}`)
  }
}

const view: CorrectionView = {
  schema_version: "correction-view-v1",
  lane: "baseline",
  run_id: 11,
  item_id: 22,
  evaluation_id: 33,
  category_key: "inspiration_image",
  snapshot_status: "frozen",
  read_only: false,
  contract: {
    contract_version: "2026.08.18.1",
    contract_hash: "hash-1",
    category_key: "inspiration_image",
  },
  review_revision: 4,
  nodes: [
    {
      node_key: "call_a.title",
      layer: "A",
      path: "call_a.title",
      label: "标题",
      description: "标题字段",
      type: "text",
      semantic_version: "1",
      compatibility_key: "title",
      required: true,
      evidence: { description: "图片证据", required: true },
      current_value: "旧标题",
      human_value: "人工标题",
      reason: "已有人工判断",
      evidence_entries: [{ text: "画面可见" }],
    },
    {
      node_key: "v3.final_level",
      layer: "V3",
      path: "final_level",
      label: "最终等级",
      description: "服务端重算",
      type: "enum",
      options: ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"],
      semantic_version: "1",
      compatibility_key: "final-level",
      required: true,
      evidence: { description: "等级依据", required: true },
      current_value: "L3",
      model_value: "L3",
    },
  ],
}

const draft = correctionDraftFromView(view)
equal(draft["call_a.title"]?.value, "人工标题", "优先回填人工值")
equal(draft["call_a.title"]?.reason, "已有人工判断", "回填人工理由")
equal(draft["call_a.title"]?.evidence, [{ text: "画面可见" }], "回填人工证据")
equal(draft["call_a.title"]?.dirty, false, "历史值初始不脏")

const changed = updateCorrectionDraft(draft, "v3.final_level", {
  value: "L2",
  reason: "边界证据",
  evidence: [{ text: "等级边界可见" }],
})
equal(changed["v3.final_level"]?.dirty, true, "修改节点标记为脏")
const payload = correctionSubmissionPayload(changed, view, "idem-1")
equal(payload.contract_hash, "hash-1", "提交沿用合同哈希")
equal(payload.review_revision, 4, "提交沿用修订号")
equal(payload.idempotency_key, "idem-1", "提交携带幂等键")
equal(payload.nodes.map((node) => node.node_key), ["v3.final_level"], "只提交被修改节点")
equal(correctionPaidRerunNodeKeys(changed, view), [], "普通纠偏不要求真实付费重跑")

const paidView: CorrectionView = {
  ...view,
  nodes: view.nodes.map((node) => node.node_key === "v3.final_level"
    ? { ...node, metadata: { paid_rerun_required: true } }
    : node),
}
equal(
  correctionPaidRerunNodeKeys(changed, paidView),
  ["v3.final_level"],
  "只识别本次已修改且会触发下游提示词的节点",
)
const paidPayload = correctionSubmissionPayload(changed, paidView, "idem-paid", {
  realExecutionConfirmed: true,
})
equal(paidPayload.real_execution_confirmed, true, "明确费用确认才写入真实重跑授权")

const mixedPaidDraft = updateCorrectionDraft(changed, "call_a.title", {
  value: "另一个修改",
  reason: "普通字段也被修改",
})
let mixedPaidRejected = false
try {
  correctionSubmissionPayload(mixedPaidDraft, paidView, "idem-mixed-paid", {
    realExecutionConfirmed: true,
  })
} catch (error) {
  mixedPaidRejected = error instanceof Error
    && error.message.includes("一次只提交一个节点")
}
equal(mixedPaidRejected, true, "付费节点与普通节点混合修改时拒绝整单提交")

const response: CorrectionView = {
  ...view,
  review_revision: 5,
  nodes: view.nodes.map((node) => node.node_key === "v3.final_level"
    ? { ...node, human_value: "L2", reason: "边界证据", evidence_entries: [{ text: "等级边界可见" }] }
    : node),
}
const merged = mergeCorrectionResponse(changed, response)
equal(merged["v3.final_level"]?.value, "L2", "保存后读取服务端人工值")
equal(merged["v3.final_level"]?.reason, "边界证据", "保存后读取服务端理由")
equal(merged["v3.final_level"]?.dirty, false, "保存响应清除脏状态")

console.log("correction view state contract: passed")

// 2026-09-20 run 373：运营只填理由没改值，前端照样让提交，后端把"值没变"的覆盖也记成一次纠偏，
// 十一次里十次是空纠偏。只写理由不能算纠偏，也不能提交。
const reasonOnly = updateCorrectionDraft(draft, "v3.final_level", { reason: "分数偏高" })
equal(reasonOnly["v3.final_level"]?.dirty, true, "只改理由仍标记为脏（用于未保存提示）")
equal(correctionChangedNodeKeys(reasonOnly, view), [], "只改理由不算改过值")
equal(
  correctionSubmitBlocker(reasonOnly, view),
  "只填理由不会覆盖结果：请先把字段改成正确的值",
  "只写理由时拦截提交并说明原因",
)
let reasonOnlyRejected = false
try {
  correctionSubmissionPayload(reasonOnly, view, "idem-reason-only")
} catch (error) {
  reasonOnlyRejected = error instanceof Error && error.message.includes("只填理由不会覆盖结果")
}
equal(reasonOnlyRejected, true, "只写理由的提交被拒绝，不再记成空纠偏")

// 改回原值同样不算纠偏（避免误点后提交一条什么都没改的记录）
const revertedToModel = updateCorrectionDraft(draft, "v3.final_level", { value: "L3", reason: "看错了" })
equal(correctionChangedNodeKeys(revertedToModel, view), [], "值与原值相同不算纠偏")

// 改了值但没写理由：拦下来，别让后端返回 Pydantic 的原始 422
const noReason = updateCorrectionDraft(draft, "v3.final_level", { value: "L2" })
equal(
  correctionSubmitBlocker(noReason, view),
  "请为已修改的字段填写纠偏理由：最终等级",
  "改了值没写理由时点名提示",
)
let noReasonRejected = false
try {
  correctionSubmissionPayload(noReason, view, "idem-no-reason")
} catch (error) {
  noReasonRejected = error instanceof Error && error.message.includes("填写纠偏理由")
}
equal(noReasonRejected, true, "改了值没理由的提交被拒绝")

// 真改了值 + 有理由：照常提交
equal(correctionChangedNodeKeys(changed, view), ["v3.final_level"], "真改值才进提交清单")
equal(correctionSubmitBlocker(changed, view), null, "改了值又写了理由就放行")

// 只改证据也算纠偏
const evidenceOnly = updateCorrectionDraft(draft, "call_a.title", {
  evidence: [{ text: "新证据" }],
  reason: "补证据",
})
equal(correctionChangedNodeKeys(evidenceOnly, view), ["call_a.title"], "只补证据也算纠偏")

// 键顺序不同不算改过值
const reordered = updateCorrectionDraft(draft, "call_a.title", {
  value: "人工标题",
  reason: "顺序变了但值没变",
})
equal(correctionChangedNodeKeys(reordered, view), [], "同值不同写法不算纠偏")

===== FILE frontend/src/features/correction-contract/correction-view-state.ts =====
import type {
  CorrectionDraft,
  CorrectionDraftNode,
  CorrectionNodeValue,
  CorrectionSubmissionRequest,
  CorrectionView,
} from "./types"
import { correctionNodeValueType } from "./contract-renderer.ts"

export function correctionValueFromNode(node: CorrectionView["nodes"][number]): CorrectionNodeValue {
  if (node.human_value !== undefined) return node.human_value
  if (node.current_value !== undefined) return node.current_value
  return node.model_value
}

function evidenceFromNode(node: CorrectionView["nodes"][number]): Array<Record<string, unknown>> {
  if (Array.isArray(node.evidence_entries)) return node.evidence_entries.map((item) => ({ ...item }))
  const raw = (node as CorrectionView["nodes"][number] & { evidence?: unknown }).evidence
  return Array.isArray(raw)
    ? raw.filter((item): item is Record<string, unknown> => Boolean(item && typeof item === "object")).map((item) => ({ ...item }))
    : []
}

export function correctionDraftFromView(view: CorrectionView): CorrectionDraft {
  return Object.fromEntries(view.nodes.map((node) => [
    node.node_key,
    {
      value: correctionValueFromNode(node),
      reason: node.reason ?? "",
      evidence: evidenceFromNode(node),
      dirty: false,
    } satisfies CorrectionDraftNode,
  ]))
}

export function updateCorrectionDraft(
  draft: CorrectionDraft,
  nodeKey: string,
  patch: Partial<CorrectionDraftNode>,
): CorrectionDraft {
  const current = draft[nodeKey] ?? { value: undefined, reason: "", evidence: [], dirty: false }
  return {
    ...draft,
    [nodeKey]: {
      ...current,
      ...patch,
      dirty: true,
    },
  }
}

export function correctionDraftHasChanges(draft: CorrectionDraft | null | undefined): boolean {
  return Boolean(draft && Object.values(draft).some((node) => node.dirty))
}

// 稳定序列化：对象键排序后比较，避免键顺序差异被当成"改过值"。
function stableJson(value: unknown): string {
  if (value === null || typeof value !== "object") return JSON.stringify(value) ?? "null"
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map(stableJson).join(",")}]`
  const entries = Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)
    .filter(([, item]) => item !== undefined)
    .sort(([left], [right]) => (left < right ? -1 : left > right ? 1 : 0))
  return `{${entries.map(([key, item]) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${stableJson(item)}`).join(",")}}`
}

function nodeIsSubmittable(node: CorrectionView["nodes"][number]): boolean {
  return node.editable !== false && !node.read_only
}

/** 只写理由不算纠偏：必须真的改了值或证据，才会覆盖结果。 */
export function correctionNodeChanged(
  node: CorrectionView["nodes"][number],
  draftNode: CorrectionDraftNode | undefined,
): boolean {
  if (!draftNode?.dirty || !nodeIsSubmittable(node)) return false
  if (stableJson(draftNode.value) !== stableJson(correctionValueFromNode(node))) return true
  return stableJson(draftNode.evidence ?? []) !== stableJson(evidenceFromNode(node))
}

export function correctionChangedNodeKeys(
  draft: CorrectionDraft | null | undefined,
  view: CorrectionView | null | undefined,
): string[] {
  if (!draft || !view) return []
  return view.nodes.filter((node) => correctionNodeChanged(node, draft[node.node_key])).map((node) => node.node_key)
}

/**
 * 提交前的拦截理由；返回 null 表示可以提交。
 * 2026-09-20：运营在真值复核轮里只填了理由没改值，前端照样让提交，后端把"值没变"的
 * 覆盖也记成一次纠偏，结果一条都没生效（run 373 十一次里十次是空纠偏）。
 */
export function correctionSubmitBlocker(
  draft: CorrectionDraft | null | undefined,
  view: CorrectionView | null | undefined,
): string | null {
  if (!draft || !view) return "纠偏面板尚未加载"
  const changed = correctionChangedNodeKeys(draft, view)
  if (!changed.length) {
    return Object.values(draft).some((node) => node.dirty)
      ? "只填理由不会覆盖结果：请先把字段改成正确的值"
      : "请先修改要纠偏的字段"
  }
  const invalidNumbers = view.nodes.filter((node) => (
    changed.includes(node.node_key)
    && correctionNodeValueType(node) === "number"
    && !(typeof draft[node.node_key]?.value === "number" && Number.isFinite(draft[node.node_key]?.value))
  ))
  if (invalidNumbers.length) {
    return `请为数值字段填写有效数字：${invalidNumbers.map((node) => node.label || node.node_key).join("、")}`
  }
  const missingReason = changed.filter((key) => !(draft[key]?.reason ?? "").trim())
  if (missingReason.length) {
    const labels = view.nodes
      .filter((node) => missingReason.includes(node.node_key))
      .map((node) => node.label || node.node_key)
    return `请为已修改的字段填写纠偏理由：${labels.join("、")}`
  }
  return null
}

export function correctionPaidRerunNodeKeys(
  draft: CorrectionDraft | null | undefined,
  view: CorrectionView | null | undefined,
): string[] {
  if (!draft || !view) return []
  return view.nodes
    .filter((node) => (
      correctionNodeChanged(node, draft[node.node_key])
      && node.metadata?.paid_rerun_required === true
    ))
    .map((node) => node.node_key)
}

export function mergeCorrectionResponse(
  _previous: CorrectionDraft,
  response: CorrectionView,
): CorrectionDraft {
  return correctionDraftFromView(response)
}

export function correctionSubmissionPayload(
  draft: CorrectionDraft,
  view: CorrectionView,
  idempotencyKey: string,
  options: { realExecutionConfirmed?: boolean } = {},
): CorrectionSubmissionRequest {
  if (!view.contract) {
    throw new Error("当前运行没有可提交的纠偏合同")
  }
  const blocker = correctionSubmitBlocker(draft, view)
  if (blocker) throw new Error(blocker)
  const nodes = view.nodes
    .filter((node) => correctionNodeChanged(node, draft[node.node_key]))
    .map((node) => {
      const nodeDraft = draft[node.node_key]
      if (!nodeDraft) throw new Error(`缺少节点 ${node.node_key} 的草稿`)
      return {
        node_key: node.node_key,
        human_value: nodeDraft.value,
        reason: nodeDraft.reason.trim(),
        evidence: nodeDraft.evidence,
      }
    })
  if (options.realExecutionConfirmed && nodes.length !== 1) {
    throw new Error("涉及真实模型重跑时请一次只提交一个节点，避免重复计费")
  }
  return {
    contract_hash: view.contract.contract_hash,
    review_revision: view.review_revision,
    idempotency_key: idempotencyKey,
    ...(options.realExecutionConfirmed ? { real_execution_confirmed: true } : {}),
    nodes,
  }
}

/**
 * 保存纠偏时锁定的目标条目。TanStack Query v5 会把最新一次渲染的配置同步给
 * 进行中的 mutation，回调闭包里的“当前条目”可能已经换成下一条；
 * 因此目标必须随 mutate 的 variables 传入，回调只认 variables。
 */
export interface CorrectionSaveTarget {
  runId: number
  itemId: number
}

/** 保存响应只能合并进仍停留在同一条目上的草稿，否则会把 A 的结果写进 B。 */
export function correctionSaveTargetIsCurrent(
  target: CorrectionSaveTarget,
  current: CorrectionSaveTarget,
): boolean {
  return target.runId === current.runId && target.itemId === current.itemId
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/correction-contract/deduction-breakdown.tsx =====
import type { CorrectionNodeMetadata } from "./types"

const severity: Record<string, string> = {minor_defect: "轻微缺陷", obvious_defect: "明显缺陷", severe_defect: "严重缺陷"}
export const deductionRuleLabel = (key: string) => severity[key] ?? key
const numberText = (value: number | null | undefined) => value == null ? "未记录" : String(value)

export function DeductionBreakdown({value, onCorrect}: {
  value: NonNullable<CorrectionNodeMetadata["deduction_breakdown"]>
  onCorrect: (key: string) => void
}) {
  return <div className="min-w-0 space-y-3 text-sm" aria-label="维度扣分明细">
    <p className="font-semibold">美感初始分 {numberText(value.initial_score)} · 本次实际扣分 {numberText(value.total_deduction)}</p>
    <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">以下为已保存的计算结果。纠偏规则命中后，保存将按本轮冻结规则重新计算；修改全局扣分标准需新建评测机制版本。</p>
    <div className="overflow-x-auto"><table className="w-full min-w-[520px] border-collapse text-left text-xs">
      <thead className="bg-alias-surface-051"><tr>{["维度 / 权重", "命中规则与证据", "实际扣分", "纠偏"].map(label => <th key={label} className="border-b border-[var(--line)] p-2 align-top">{label}</th>)}</tr></thead>
      <tbody>{value.rows.map(row => <tr key={row.key} className="border-b border-[var(--line)]">
        <td className="p-2 align-top"><strong>{row.label}</strong><p>{numberText(row.share)}%</p></td>
        <td className="max-w-lg p-2 align-top leading-5 [overflow-wrap:anywhere]">{row.hits.length ? row.hits.map((hit, index) => <div key={`${hit.rule_id}-${index}`} className="mb-2">
          <p className="font-semibold">{deductionRuleLabel(hit.rule_id)} · 规则分 {numberText(hit.deduction)}</p>
          <p>规则：{hit.description || "本轮快照未提供规则正文"}</p><p className="text-[var(--muted)]">命中依据：{hit.evidence || "未记录"}</p>
        </div>) : "未命中扣分规则"}{row.cap_applied && <p>已应用上限：{row.cap_reason || "以冻结评分记录为准"}</p>}</td>
        <td className="p-2 align-top"><strong>{numberText(row.point_deduction)} 分</strong><p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">维度内扣分 {numberText(row.applied_deduction)}</p></td>
        <td className="p-2 align-top">{row.correction_node_key ? <button type="button" className="visual-native-button whitespace-nowrap border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white px-2 py-2 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary" onClick={() => onCorrect(row.correction_node_key!)}>纠偏命中</button> : <span className="text-[var(--muted)]">本轮无对应纠偏字段</span>}</td>
      </tr>)}</tbody>
    </table></div>
  </div>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/correction-contract/native-evaluation-detail.tsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
import { FloppyDisk, PencilSimple } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { CorrectionContractRenderer, CorrectionNodeCard, CorrectionPublicationNotice } from "./contract-renderer.tsx"
import { correctionNodeEditable, workflowCorrectionGroupState } from "./contract-renderer"
import { correctionPaidRerunNodeKeys } from "./correction-view-state"
import { nativeCorrectionValueText, type nativeEvaluationPresentation } from "./native-evaluation-presentation"
import type { CorrectionDraft, CorrectionView } from "./types"
import { DeductionBreakdown } from "./deduction-breakdown"

export function NativeEvaluationDetail({ view, draft, presentation, onChange, onSubmit, pending = false, submitDisabled = false, submitBlockReason = null, disabled = false }: {
  view: CorrectionView
  draft: CorrectionDraft
  presentation: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof nativeEvaluationPresentation>>
  onChange: (nodeKey: string, patch: Partial<CorrectionDraft[string]>) => void
  onSubmit?: () => void
  pending?: boolean
  submitDisabled?: boolean
  submitBlockReason?: string | null
  disabled?: boolean
}) {
  const [activeKey, setActiveKey] = useState(presentation.sections[0].key)
  const [editingKey, setEditingKey] = useState<string | null>(null)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (editingKey) {
      const element = document.getElementById(`native-field-${view.item_id}-${editingKey}`)
      element?.scrollIntoView({block: "center"})
      element?.focus({preventScroll: true})
    }
  }, [editingKey, activeKey, view.item_id])
  const section = presentation.sections.find((item) => item.key === activeKey) ?? presentation.sections[0]
  const paid = correctionPaidRerunNodeKeys(draft, view).length > 0
  const dirty = Object.values(draft).filter((item) => item.dirty).length
  return (
    <section className="native-evaluation-detail min-w-0 border-y border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white" aria-label="调用 A、调用 B 与等级撮合纠偏">
      <CorrectionPublicationNotice view={view} />
      <div className="native-evaluation-sticky-heading" data-testid="correction-evidence-sticky-heading">
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 bg-alias-surface-051 px-5 py-4">
        <div><h3 className="text-sm font-bold">评测细节与纠偏</h3><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">查看本轮实际结果，点字段旁的“纠偏”修改结果和理由。</p></div>
        {(disabled || view.read_only) && <Badge>只读</Badge>}
      </header>
      {view.unavailable_reason && <p className="px-5 py-3 text-xs text-alias-content-033">{view.unavailable_reason}</p>}
      <div role="tablist" aria-label="评测纠偏阶段" className="native-evaluation-tabs flex flex-wrap border-y border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
        {presentation.sections.map((item) => (
          <button key={item.key} id={`native-tab-${view.item_id}-${item.key}`} type="button" role="tab" aria-selected={item.key === section.key} aria-controls={`native-evaluation-${view.item_id}-${item.key}`}
            tabIndex={item.key === section.key ? 0 : -1}
            onKeyDown={(event) => {
              if (!["ArrowLeft", "ArrowRight", "Home", "End"].includes(event.key)) return
              event.preventDefault()
              const items = presentation.sections
              const index = items.findIndex((entry) => entry.key === item.key)
              const next = event.key === "Home" ? 0 : event.key === "End" ? items.length - 1 : (index + (event.key === "ArrowRight" ? 1 : -1) + items.length) % items.length
              setActiveKey(items[next].key)
              document.getElementById(`native-tab-${view.item_id}-${items[next].key}`)?.focus()
            }}
            className={"visual-native-button rounded-none border-x-0 border-t-0 " + (`min-h-12 flex-1 border-b-2 px-4 py-3 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-inset focus-visible:ring-primary ${item.key === section.key ? "border-primary bg-alias-surface-044" : "border-transparent"}`)}
            onClick={() => setActiveKey(item.key)}>{item.title}<span className="ml-2 text-xs font-normal text-[var(--muted)]">{item.groups.reduce((sum, group) => sum + group.nodes.length, 0)} 项</span></button>
        ))}
      </div>
      </div>
      <div role="tabpanel" id={`native-evaluation-${view.item_id}-${section.key}`} aria-labelledby={`native-tab-${view.item_id}-${section.key}`} className="divide-y divide-[var(--line-strong)]">
        {section.groups.map((group) => {
          const skipped = workflowCorrectionGroupState(group) === "skipped"
          return <section key={group.group_key} className="native-evaluation-group">
            {section.key === "V3" && <h4 className="bg-alias-surface-051 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold">{group.label}</h4>}
            {skipped ? <p className="px-5 py-4 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">本次未执行{section.title}；该分支没有结果可纠偏。</p> : group.nodes.map((node) => {
              const value = draft[node.node_key] ? draft[node.node_key].value : node.human_value ?? node.current_value ?? node.model_value
              const editable = !disabled && !view.read_only && correctionNodeEditable(node)
              const editing = editingKey === node.node_key
              return <div key={node.node_key} id={`native-field-${view.item_id}-${node.node_key}`} tabIndex={-1} className="native-evaluation-field border-b border-[var(--line)] last:border-b-0 focus:outline-none" data-testid={`native-field-${node.node_key}`}>
                <div className="native-evaluation-row grid gap-2 px-4 py-3 md:grid-cols-[minmax(0,160px)_minmax(0,1fr)_auto]">
                  <div className="min-w-0"><p className="text-xs font-semibold">{node.label}</p>{draft[node.node_key]?.dirty && <Badge tone="warning">待保存</Badge>}</div>
                  <div className="min-w-0">{node.metadata?.deduction_breakdown ? <DeductionBreakdown value={node.metadata.deduction_breakdown} onCorrect={key => {
                    const target = presentation.sections.find(item => item.groups.some(group => group.nodes.some(entry => entry.node_key === key)))
                    if (target) { setActiveKey(target.key); setEditingKey(key) }
                  }} /> : <p className="max-h-48 overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-sm leading-6">{nativeCorrectionValueText(value)}</p>}{node.reason && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">人工理由：{node.reason}</p>}</div>
                  <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" disabled={pending} aria-label={`${node.label}${editable ? "纠偏" : "查看详情"}`} onClick={() => setEditingKey(editing ? null : node.node_key)}><PencilSimple />{editing ? "收起" : editable ? "纠偏" : "查看详情"}</Button>
                </div>
                {editing && <div className="bg-alias-surface-051 px-4 pb-4"><CorrectionNodeCard node={node} view={view} draft={draft} disabled={disabled || pending} onChange={onChange} /></div>}
              </div>
            })}
          </section>
        })}
      </div>
      {onSubmit && <footer className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-051 px-5 py-4">
        <p className={submitBlockReason && dirty ? "text-xs text-alias-content-033" : "text-xs text-[var(--muted)]"}>
          {submitBlockReason && dirty
            ? submitBlockReason
            : dirty
              ? `${dirty} 项修改待保存。`
              : "修改会保留原始结果及人工纠偏记录。"}
        </p>
        {paid && <p role="alert" className="w-full text-xs text-alias-content-033">本次修改会重新调用下游提示词并产生真实模型费用，提交前需确认。</p>}
        <Button disabled={disabled || view.read_only || pending || submitDisabled || !dirty} onClick={onSubmit}><FloppyDisk />{pending ? "正在保存" : paid ? "确认费用并真实重跑" : "保存节点纠偏"}</Button>
      </footer>}
      <details className="visual-native-details border-t border-[var(--line)]"><summary className="font-semibold">技术快照信息</summary><p className="break-all px-5 pb-3 font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">合同版本 <span className="visual-code">{view.contract?.contract_version ?? "—"}</span> · 合同哈希 <span className="visual-code">{view.contract?.contract_hash ?? "—"}</span></p></details>
      {presentation.evidenceNodes.length > 0 && <CorrectionContractRenderer presentation="supplementary" view={{ ...view, nodes: presentation.evidenceNodes }} draft={draft} onChange={onChange} disabled={disabled} />}
    </section>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/correction-contract/native-evaluation-presentation.ts =====
import { groupWorkflowCorrectionNodes, type WorkflowCorrectionNodeGroup } from "./contract-renderer.ts"
import type { CorrectionContractNode } from "./types"

export type NativeEvaluationSection = {
  key: "A" | "B" | "V3"
  title: string
  groups: WorkflowCorrectionNodeGroup[]
}

/** Both native prompts can have layer A. Use the runtime's frozen A/B node
 * identities; unknown prompt graphs retain their actual workflow presentation. */
export function nativeEvaluationPresentation(nodes: readonly CorrectionContractNode[]) {
  const groups = groupWorkflowCorrectionNodes(nodes)
  const prompts = groups.filter((group) => ["prompt", "foreach_image"].includes(group.workflow_kind))
  if (!prompts.length || prompts.some((group) => (
    group.workflow_kind !== "prompt" || !["prompt_a", "prompt_b"].includes(group.node_instance_id)
  ))) return null
  const rules = groups.filter((group) => ["rule_module", "rule_contract", "level_matcher"].includes(group.workflow_kind))
  const sections: NativeEvaluationSection[] = []
  for (const [key, title, groupId] of [["A", "调用 A", "prompt_a"], ["B", "调用 B", "prompt_b"]] as const) {
    const calls = prompts.filter((group) => group.node_instance_id === groupId)
    if (calls.length) sections.push({ key, title, groups: calls })
  }
  if (rules.length) sections.push({ key: "V3", title: "等级撮合器", groups: rules })
  const primaryKeys = new Set(sections.flatMap((section) => section.groups.flatMap((group) => group.nodes.map((node) => node.node_key))))
  return { sections, evidenceNodes: nodes.filter((node) => !primaryKeys.has(node.node_key)) }
}

export function nativeCorrectionValueText(value: unknown): string {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return "未返回"
  if (typeof value === "boolean") return value ? "是" : "否"
  if (typeof value === "string") return value === "" ? "（空）" : value
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.length ? value.map(nativeCorrectionValueText).join("\n") : "（空）"
  if (typeof value === "object") return JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)
  return String(value)
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/correction-contract/types.ts =====
import type { CorrectionNote, CorrectionPublication } from "@/lib/types"

export type CorrectionLayer = "A" | "B" | "V3"

export type CorrectionNodeValue = unknown

export type CorrectionFieldIdentity = {
  node_instance_id: string
  output_contract_hash: string
  field_path: string
  item_key?: string
}

export type CorrectionNodeMetadata = Record<string, unknown> & {
  deduction_rule_options?: Array<{rule_id: string; description: string; deduction: number | null}>
  deduction_breakdown?: {
    initial_score: number | null
    total_deduction: number | null
    rows: Array<{
      key: string; label: string; share: number | null
      raw_deduction: number | null; applied_deduction: number | null
      point_deduction: number | null; cap_applied: boolean; cap_reason: string | null
      hits: Array<{rule_id: string; evidence?: string; description?: string; deduction?: number}>
      correction_node_key: string | null
    }>
  }
  node_instance_id?: string
  workflow_node_key?: string
  workflow_kind?: string
  workflow_order?: number
  workflow_display_name?: string
  foreach_item_key?: string
  foreach_item_status?: string
  field_identity?: CorrectionFieldIdentity
  paid_rerun_required?: boolean
  option_labels?: Record<string, string>
}

export type CorrectionContractNode = {
  node_key: string
  layer: CorrectionLayer
  path: string
  order?: number
  label: string
  description: string
  type: string
  semantic_version: string
  compatibility_key: string
  required: boolean
  evidence: {
    description: string
    required?: boolean
    [key: string]: unknown
  }
  options?: unknown[]
  allowed_values?: unknown[]
  values?: unknown[]
  min?: number
  max?: number
  minimum?: number
  maximum?: number
  minItems?: number
  maxItems?: number
  recompute_ref?: string
  metadata?: CorrectionNodeMetadata
  model_value?: CorrectionNodeValue
  current_value?: CorrectionNodeValue
  human_value?: CorrectionNodeValue
  reason?: string
  evidence_value?: unknown
  evidence_entries?: Array<Record<string, unknown>>
  inheritance?: {
    status: "inherited" | "new" | "changed" | "current" | string
    [key: string]: unknown
  }
  editable?: boolean
  read_only?: boolean
  read_only_reason?: string
  steps?: string[]
  [key: string]: unknown
}

export type CorrectionContractIdentity = {
  contract_version: string | null
  contract_hash: string
  category_key: string
}

export type EffectiveBaselineTruth = {
  truth_kind: "level" | "structured_fields"
  expected_level: string | null
  expected_result: Record<string, unknown>
  truth_contract_key: string
  workflow_input: Record<string, unknown>
}

export type CorrectionView = {
  notes?: CorrectionNote[]
  schema_version: string
  lane?: "baseline" | "incremental" | "candidate" | string
  run_id: number | null
  item_id: number | null
  evaluation_id: number | null
  category_key: string | null
  snapshot_status: "frozen" | "legacy_read_only" | string
  snapshot_source?: string
  read_only: boolean
  unavailable_reason?: string | null
  unavailable_nodes?: string[]
  contract: CorrectionContractIdentity | null
  review_revision: number
  nodes: CorrectionContractNode[]
  idempotent_replay?: boolean
  truth_status?: "available" | "unavailable" | string
  truth_source?: "baseline_import" | "revision" | string
  truth_revision?: number
  truth_hash?: string
  effective_truth?: EffectiveBaselineTruth
  known_truth_fields?: string[]
  pending_truth_fields?: string[]
  effective_next_run?: boolean
  route_field_confirmation?: {
    from_contract_key: string
    to_contract_key: string
    fields: string[]
  }
  publication?: CorrectionPublication | null
}

export type CorrectionDraftNode = {
  value: CorrectionNodeValue
  reason: string
  evidence: Array<Record<string, unknown>>
  dirty?: boolean
}

export type CorrectionDraft = Record<string, CorrectionDraftNode>

export type CorrectionSubmissionRequest = {
  contract_hash: string
  review_revision: number
  idempotency_key: string
  real_execution_confirmed?: boolean
  nodes: Array<{
    node_key: string
    human_value: CorrectionNodeValue
    reason: string
    evidence: Array<Record<string, unknown>>
  }>
}

export type CorrectionNodeGroup = Record<CorrectionLayer, CorrectionContractNode[]>

===== FILE frontend/src/features/evaluation-detail/detail-model.ts =====
/**
 * 人工纠偏三段式评测细节的纯逻辑层。
 *
 * 把一条评测结果拆成运营可直接识别的三大部分：调用A、调用B、等级撮合器。
 * 这里只做「读取与翻译」，不做任何评分计算——最终分数与等级仍由服务端权威引擎决定。
 */

export type DetailTone = "neutral" | "active" | "warning" | "danger" | "success"

export type DetailRow = {
  /** 字段中文名，运营看到的第一列 */
  label: string
  /** 模型给出的结果，已转成可读文本 */
  value: string
  /** 该行的判定色，用于让异常项自己跳出来 */
  tone?: DetailTone
  /** 字段口径提示，例如「0-100 整数」 */
  hint?: string
  /** 模型的判断依据原文 */
  evidence?: string[]
  /** 该行是否已被人工改过 */
  corrected?: boolean
  /** 该项是机制新增、前端还没配中文名的；用于提示运营「这是新项不是异常」 */
  isNew?: boolean
  /** 可点击纠偏时的提交目标；缺省表示这一行不能直接改 */
  correction?: RowCorrectionTarget
  /** 不能直接改的原因，例如「由撮合器算出」；与 correction 互斥 */
  derivedNote?: string
  /** 人工已纠偏后的值，用于和模型判断并列对照 */
  humanValue?: string
}

/** 纠偏弹窗需要的控件形态 */
export type RowValueKind =
  | "text"
  | "multiline"
  | "integer"
  | "enum"
  | "multi_enum"
  | "string_list"
  | "rule_hit"

export type RowCorrectionTarget = {
  /** 服务端 node_type，决定回写分支 */
  nodeType:
    | "call_a_field"
    | "precheck_field"
    | "aesthetic_score"
    | "dimension_rule"
    | "track"
    | "final_level"
  /** 服务端 node_path */
  nodePath: string
  valueKind: RowValueKind
  /** 提交时用于乐观并发校验的当前值，必须是服务端存的原始形态 */
  currentValue: unknown
  options?: Array<{ value: string; label: string }>
  minimum?: number
  maximum?: number
  hint?: string
  /** rule_hit 专用：提交新命中对象时服务端要求 rule_id 与节点一致 */
  ruleId?: string
  /** 改完是否会带动分数与等级重算；用于在弹窗里如实告知运营 */
  recomputes: boolean
}

export type DetailGroup = {
  title: string
  description?: string
  rows: DetailRow[]
  /** 该组为空时显示的说明，避免运营误判成「数据丢了」 */
  emptyText?: string
  note?: string
}

export type DetailSectionKey = "A" | "B" | "V3"

export type DetailSection = {
  key: DetailSectionKey
  title: string
  /** 该段一句话职责，帮运营建立心智 */
  description: string
  /** 该段最关键的一个结论，显示在段头右侧 */
  headline: string | null
  headlineHint: string | null
  headlineTone: DetailTone
  groups: DetailGroup[]
  /** 该段数据缺失的原因；有值时整段降级为说明而不是空表 */
  unavailableReason: string | null
}

/**
 * 已知生产字段的展示顺序与口径提示。
 *
 * 这是「排序与美化」用的，不是白名单：调用A新增字段时不在这里的会自动追加到末尾并标记为新增，
 * 不需要改前端就能看到。要给新字段配中文名和口径提示时，再往这里补一行。
 */
const KNOWN_PRODUCTION_FIELDS: Array<{
  key: string
  label: string
  hint?: string
  valueKind: RowValueKind
}> = [
  { key: "title", label: "素材标题", hint: "10 字内", valueKind: "text" },
  { key: "seotitle", label: "搜索标题", hint: "28 字内", valueKind: "text" },
  { key: "category", label: "素材类目", hint: "一级分类，二级分类", valueKind: "text" },
  { key: "style", label: "素材风格", hint: "无法判断时写「无法判断」", valueKind: "text" },
  { key: "tags", label: "素材标签", hint: "至少 4 个", valueKind: "string_list" },
  { key: "cons", label: "素材缺点", hint: "只依据可见证据", valueKind: "multiline" },
  { key: "design", label: "设计说明", hint: "无法判断时不得编造", valueKind: "multiline" },
  { key: "score", label: "素材分数", hint: "0-100 整数，不是最终等级", valueKind: "integer" },

  { key: "image_defects", label: "图片缺陷", hint: "空或「有水印」", valueKind: "enum" },

  },
]

/** 值非空就意味着命中了负面信号的字段；用于让异常项自己跳出来 */
const SIGNAL_FIELDS: Record<string, DetailTone> = {

  // 不叫「赛道归属」——那是「撮合输入」里的行名，同名会让运营分不清输入与步骤

/** 未命中或未启用的步骤不代表出错，单独标色避免运营误读成异常 */
const NEUTRAL_STEPS = new Set([
  "track_adjustment_skipped",
  "media_skipped",
  "veto_skipped",
])

export function isPlainObject(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value)
}

/** 机制新增项的统一标记：让运营知道这项是新的、还没配中文名，而不是显示成乱码 */
export const NEW_ITEM_HINT = "机制新增项"

/**
 * 按「已知顺序在前、新增项追加在后」合并键。
 *
 * 机制升级新增字段或规则时，前端无需改动就能把新项显示出来；已知项仍保持人工排好的顺序。
 */
export function mergeKeyOrder(
  knownKeys: readonly string[],
  actualKeys: readonly string[],
): Array<{ key: string; known: boolean }> {
  const actual = new Set(actualKeys)
  const ordered = knownKeys
    .filter((key) => actual.has(key))
    .map((key) => ({ key, known: true }))
  const extras = actualKeys
    .filter((key) => !knownKeys.includes(key))
    .map((key) => ({ key, known: false }))
  return [...ordered, ...extras]
}

/**
 * 取中文标签：优先用机制自带的 label，其次查前端已知映射，都没有就回落到原始键。
 *
 * 回落到原始键是有意的——显示英文键比编一个错译更有利于运营反馈「这项缺名字」。
 */
export function resolveLabel(
  key: string,
  knownLabels: Record<string, string>,
  dynamicLabel?: unknown,
): string {
  if (typeof dynamicLabel === "string" && dynamicLabel.trim()) return dynamicLabel.trim()
  return knownLabels[key] ?? key
}
